## Trust and Loan Companies Act {#trust-and-loan-companies-act .Title-of-Act}

[S.C.]{.abbr title="Statutes of Canada"} 1991, c. 45
:::

Assented to 1991-12-13

An Act to revise and amend the law governing federal trust and loan
companies and to provide for related and consequential matters
::::

:::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: Enacts
Her Majesty, by and with the advice and consent of the Senate and House
of Commons of Canada, enacts as follows:
:::

## [Short Title]{.HTitleText1} {#h-452760 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Short title

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** This Act may be cited as
the [Trust and Loan Companies Act](/eng/acts/T-19.8).

## [PART I]{.HLabel1}[Interpretation and Application]{.HTitleText1} {#h-452763 .Part}

### [Definitions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-452764 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** In this Act,

[[affairs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[affairs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, with respect to a company, means
    the relationships among the company and its affiliates and the
    shareholders, directors and officers of the company and its
    affiliates, but does not include the business of the company or any
    of its affiliates; ([affaires internes]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[affiliate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[affiliate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an entity that is affiliated
    with another entity within the meaning of section
    6; ([groupe]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Financial Consumer Agency
    of Canada established under section 3 of the [Financial Consumer
    Agency of Canada Act](/eng/acts/F-11.1); ([Agence]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[annual statement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[annual statement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the annual financial
    statement of a company within the meaning of paragraph
    313(1)(a); ([rapport annuel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[assets]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[assets]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a company that is a
    trust company pursuant to subsection 57(2), includes assets held by
    the company in respect of guaranteed trust
    money; ([actif]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[auditor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[auditor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a firm of
    accountants; ([vérificateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[bank holding company]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[bank holding company]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a body corporate
    that is incorporated or formed under Part XV of the [Bank
    Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01); ([société de portefeuille
    bancaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[bearer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[bearer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a security, means the
    person in possession of a security payable to bearer or endorsed in
    blank; ([porteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[bearer form]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[bearer form]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a security, means
    a security in bearer form as determined in accordance with
    subsection 86(2); ([titre au porteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[beneficial ownership]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[beneficial ownership]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes ownership
    through one or more trustees, legal representatives, agents or other
    intermediaries; ([véritable propriétaire]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"} et [propriété effective]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[body corporate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[body corporate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an incorporated body
    wherever or however incorporated; ([personne
    morale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[branch]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[branch]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a company, means an
    agency, the head office and any other office of the
    company; ([bureau]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Canadian financial institution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Canadian financial institution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
    financial institution that is incorporated or formed by or under an
    Act of Parliament or of the legislature of a province; ([institution
    financière canadienne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[central securities register]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[central securities register]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or [[securities
    register]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the register referred to in
    section 253; ([registre central des valeurs
    mobilières]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou [registre des valeurs
    mobilières]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Commissioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Commissioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Commissioner of the
    Financial Consumer Agency of Canada appointed under section 4 of the
    [Financial Consumer Agency of Canada
    Act](/eng/acts/F-11.1); ([commissaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[common-law partner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[common-law partner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to an
    individual, means a person who is cohabiting with the individual in
    a conjugal relationship, having so cohabited for a period of at
    least one year; ([conjoint de fait]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[company]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[company]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a body corporate to which this
    Act applies; ([société]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[complainant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[complainant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a company or any
    matter concerning a company, means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a registered holder or beneficial owner, and a
      former registered holder or beneficial owner, of a security of a
      company or any of its affiliates,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a director or an officer, or a former director or
      officer, of a company or any of its affiliates, or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any other person who, in the discretion of a
      court, is a proper person to make an application under section
      339, 343 or 537; ([plaignant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[consumer provision]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[consumer provision]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a provision
    referred to in paragraph (d) or (d.1) of the definition [[consumer
    provision]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 2 of the [Financial
    Consumer Agency of Canada Act](/eng/acts/F-11.1); ([disposition
    visant les consommateurs]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[court]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[court]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the Province of Ontario, the Superior Court of
      Justice,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the Province of Quebec, the Superior Court of
      the Province,

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the Provinces of Nova Scotia, British Columbia
      and Prince Edward Island, the Supreme Court of the Province,

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the Provinces of New Brunswick, Manitoba,
      Saskatchewan and Alberta, the Court of Queen's Bench for the
      Province,

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the Province of Newfoundland and Labrador, the
      Trial Division of the Supreme Court of the Province, and

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in Yukon and the Northwest Territories, the
      Supreme Court, and in Nunavut, the Nunavut Court of
      Justice; ([tribunal]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[court of appeal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[court of appeal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the court to which an
    appeal lies from a decision or order of a court; ([cour
    d'appel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[creditor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[creditor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a company that is a
    trust company pursuant to subsection 57(2), includes a person who
    has money deposited with the company as guaranteed trust
    money; ([créancier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[debt obligation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[debt obligation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a bond, debenture,
    note or other evidence of indebtedness of an entity, whether secured
    or unsecured; ([titre de créance]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[deposit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[deposit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of money received by a
    company that is a trust company pursuant to subsection 57(2), means
    money received as guaranteed trust money; ([dépôt]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[deposit liabilities]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[deposit liabilities]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a company
    that is a trust company pursuant to subsection 57(2), means
    liabilities of the company in respect of guaranteed trust
    money; ([dette]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[director]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[director]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a natural person occupying
    the position of director, by whatever name called, of a body
    corporate, and [[board of directors]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or
    [[directors]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} refers to the directors of a body
    corporate as a body; ([administrateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"},
    [conseil d'administration]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou
    [conseil]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a body corporate, trust,
    partnership, fund, an unincorporated association or organization,
    Her Majesty in right of Canada or of a province, an agency of Her
    Majesty in either of such rights and the government of a foreign
    country or any political subdivision thereof and any agency
    thereof; ([entité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[equity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[equity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a company, means its
    equity as determined in accordance with the regulations; ([capitaux
    propres]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[federal credit union]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[federal credit union]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
    in section 2 of the [Bank Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01); ([coopérative de
    crédit fédérale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[federal financial institution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[federal financial institution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a company,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a bank,

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an association to which the [Cooperative Credit
      Associations Act](/eng/acts/C-41.01) applies, or

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an insurance company or a fraternal benefit
      society incorporated or formed under the [Insurance Companies
      Act](/eng/acts/I-11.8); ([institution financière
      fédérale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[fiduciary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[fiduciary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any person acting in a
    fiduciary capacity and includes a personal representative of a
    deceased person; ([représentant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[financial institution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[financial institution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a company,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a bank or an authorized foreign bank within the
      meaning of section 2 of the [Bank Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01),

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an association to which the [Cooperative Credit
      Associations Act](/eng/acts/C-41.01) applies,

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an insurance company or a fraternal benefit
      society incorporated or formed under the [Insurance Companies
      Act](/eng/acts/I-11.8),

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a trust, loan or insurance corporation
      incorporated by or under an Act of the legislature of a province,

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a cooperative credit society incorporated and
      regulated by or under an Act of the legislature of a province,

    - [(g)]{.lawlabel} an entity that is incorporated or formed by or
      under an Act of Parliament or of the legislature of a province and
      that is primarily engaged in dealing in securities, including
      portfolio management and investment counselling, and

    - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a foreign institution; ([institution
      financière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[foreign institution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[foreign institution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an entity that is

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} engaged in the business of banking, the trust,
      loan or insurance business, the business of a cooperative credit
      society or the business of dealing in securities or is otherwise
      engaged primarily in the business of providing financial services,
      and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} incorporated or formed otherwise than by or under
      an Act of Parliament or of the legislature of a
      province; ([institution étrangère]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[former Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[former Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Trust Companies Act or
    the Loan Companies Act; ([loi antérieure]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[former-Act company]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[former-Act company]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a body corporate
    referred to in paragraph 12(b) or (c); ([société
    antérieure]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[form of proxy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[form of proxy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a written or printed
    form that, when completed and executed by or on behalf of a
    shareholder, constitutes a proxy; ([formulaire de
    procuration]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[going-private transaction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[going-private transaction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
    going-private transaction as defined in the
    regulations; ([transaction de fermeture]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[guarantee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[guarantee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a letter of
    credit; ([garantie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[guaranteed trust money]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[guaranteed trust money]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means money that is
    received in trust for investment by a company that is a trust
    company pursuant to subsection 57(2) and that is subject to a
    guarantee by the company in respect of the payment of interest or
    repayment of the principal or both; ([fonds en fiducie
    garantie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[head office]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[head office]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the office required to be
    maintained by a company pursuant to section
    242; ([siège]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[holder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[holder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a security certificate, the person
      in possession of the certificate issued or endorsed to that person
      or to bearer or in blank, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the ownership of a share, the
      shareholder of the share within the meaning of section
      7; ([détenteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[holding body corporate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[holding body corporate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a holding body
    corporate within the meaning of section 4; ([société
    mère]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[incorporated]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[incorporated]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, when used with reference to a
    body corporate that is incorporated by or under an Act of Parliament
    or of the legislature of a province, also refers to a body corporate
    that is continued by or under any such Act; ([constitué en personne
    morale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[incorporating instrument]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[incorporating instrument]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the special
    Act, letters patent, instrument of continuance or other constating
    instrument by which a body corporate was incorporated or continued
    and includes any amendment to or restatement of the constating
    instrument; ([acte constitutif]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[incorporator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[incorporator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a company,
    means a person who applied for letters patent to incorporate the
    company; ([fondateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[insurance holding company]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[insurance holding company]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a body
    corporate that is incorporated or formed under Part XVII of the
    [Insurance Companies Act](/eng/acts/I-11.8); ([société de
    portefeuille d'assurances]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[issuer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[issuer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a security, means the
    entity that issues or issued the
    security; ([émetteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[letters patent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[letters patent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an instrument
    authorized to be issued under this Act, means letters patent in a
    form approved by the Superintendent; ([lettres
    patentes]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Minister of
    Finance; ([ministre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[minor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[minor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in the
    applicable provincial law and in the absence of any such law has the
    same meaning as the word "child" in the United Nations Convention on
    the Rights of the Child adopted in the United Nations General
    Assembly on November 20, 1989; ([mineur]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[non-WTO Member foreign institution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[non-WTO Member foreign institution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
    foreign institution that is not controlled by a WTO Member
    resident; ([institution étrangère d'un non-membre de
    l'OMC]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in relation to a body corporate, a chief
      executive officer, president, vice-president, secretary,
      controller, treasurer and any other natural person designated as
      an officer of the body corporate by by-law or by resolution of the
      directors of the body corporate, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in relation to any other entity, any natural
      person designated as an officer of the entity by by-law, by
      resolution of the members thereof or
      otherwise; ([dirigeant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[order form]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[order form]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a security, means
    a security in order form as determined in accordance with subsection
    86(3); ([titre à ordre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[ordinary resolution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[ordinary resolution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a resolution
    passed by a majority of the votes cast by or on behalf of the
    shareholders who voted in respect of that resolution; ([résolution
    ordinaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a natural person, an entity or
    a personal representative; ([personne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[personal representative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[personal representative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who
    stands in place of and represents another person and, without
    limiting the generality of the foregoing, includes, as the
    circumstances require, a trustee, an executor, an administrator, a
    committee, a guardian, a tutor, a curator, an assignee, a receiver,
    an agent or an attorney of any person; ([représentant
    personnel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means prescribed by
    regulation; ([Version anglaise seulement]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[property]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[property]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a company that is a
    trust company pursuant to subsection 57(2), includes property held
    by the company in respect of guaranteed trust
    money; ([biens]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[proxy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[proxy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a completed and executed form of
    proxy by means of which a shareholder appoints a proxyholder to
    attend and act on the shareholder's behalf at a meeting of
    shareholders; ([procuration]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[proxyholder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[proxyholder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the person appointed by
    proxy to attend and act on behalf of a shareholder at a meeting of
    shareholders; ([fondé de pouvoir]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[real property]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[real property]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a leasehold interest
    in real property; ([biens immeubles]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[recorded address]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[recorded address]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in relation to a person who is a shareholder of a
      company, the latest postal address of the person according to the
      central securities register of the company, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in relation to a person in any other respect, the
      latest postal address of the person according to the records of
      the branch concerned; ([adresse enregistrée]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[registered form]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[registered form]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a security,
    means a security in registered form as determined in accordance with
    subsection 86(4); ([titre nominatif]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[regulated foreign entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[regulated foreign entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an entity
    that is

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} incorporated or formed otherwise in a country or
      territory, other than Canada, in which a trade agreement listed in
      Schedule IV of the [Bank Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01) is applicable, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to financial services regulation in that
      country or territory; ([entité étrangère
      réglementée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[regulatory capital]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[regulatory capital]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a company,
    has the meaning given that expression by the regulations; ([capital
    réglementaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[resident Canadian]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[resident Canadian]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a natural person who
    is

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a Canadian citizen ordinarily resident in Canada,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a Canadian citizen not ordinarily resident in
      Canada who is a member of a prescribed class of persons, or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a permanent resident within the meaning of
      subsection 2(1) of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
      Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) and ordinarily resident in Canada, except a
      permanent resident who has been ordinarily resident in Canada for
      more than one year after the time at which the individual first
      became eligible to apply for Canadian citizenship; ([résident
      canadien]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[residential property]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[residential property]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means real property
    consisting of buildings that are used, or are to be used, to the
    extent of at least one half of the floor space thereof, as one or
    more private dwellings; ([immeuble résidentiel]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[securities underwriter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[securities underwriter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who,
    as principal, agrees to purchase securities with a view to the
    distribution of the securities or who, as agent for a body corporate
    or other person, offers for sale or sells securities in connection
    with a distribution of the securities, and includes a person who
    participates, directly or indirectly, in a distribution of
    securities, other than a person whose interest in the distribution
    of securities is limited to receiving a distributor's or seller's
    commission payable by a securities underwriter; ([souscripteur à
    forfait]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[security]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[security]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in relation to a body corporate, a share of any
      class of shares of the body corporate or a debt obligation of the
      body corporate, and includes a warrant of the body corporate, but
      does not include a deposit with a financial institution or any
      instrument evidencing such a deposit, and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in relation to any other entity, any ownership
      interest in or debt obligation of the
      entity; ([titre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou [valeur
      mobilière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[security interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[security interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an interest in or
    charge on property by way of mortgage, lien, pledge or otherwise
    taken by a creditor or guarantor to secure the payment or
    performance of an obligation; ([sûreté]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[send]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[send]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes
    deliver; ([envoyer]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[series]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[series]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of shares, means a
    division of a class of shares; ([série]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[significant interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[significant interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a significant
    interest determined in accordance with section 8; ([intérêt
    substantiel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[special resolution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[special resolution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a resolution passed
    by a majority of not less than two thirds of the votes cast by or on
    behalf of the shareholders who voted in respect of that resolution
    or signed by all the shareholders entitled to vote on that
    resolution; ([résolution extraordinaire]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[squeeze-out transaction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[squeeze-out transaction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a transaction
    by a company that is not a distributing company that requires an
    amendment to a by-law referred to in subsection 222(1) and that
    would directly or indirectly result in the interest of a holder of
    shares of a class of shares being terminated without their consent
    and without substituting an interest of equivalent value in shares
    issued by the company that have rights and privileges equal to or
    greater than those of the shares of the affected
    class; ([transaction d'éviction]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[subordinated indebtedness]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[subordinated indebtedness]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
    instrument evidencing an indebtedness of a company that by its terms
    provides that the indebtedness will, in the event of the insolvency
    or winding-up of the company, be subordinate in right of payment to
    all deposit liabilities of the company and all other liabilities of
    the company except those that, by their terms, rank equally with or
    are subordinate to such indebtedness; ([titre
    secondaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[subsidiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[subsidiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an entity that is a
    subsidiary of another entity within the meaning of section
    5; ([filiale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[substantial investment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[substantial investment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a substantial
    investment determined in accordance with section 10; ([intérêt de
    groupe financier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Superintendent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Superintendent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Superintendent of
    Financial Institutions appointed pursuant to the [Office of the
    Superintendent of Financial Institutions
    Act](/eng/acts/O-2.7); ([surintendant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[trade]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[trade]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of securities, means any
    sale or disposition of securities for valuable
    consideration; ([opération]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[transfer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[transfer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of securities, includes
    a transmission by operation of law; ([transfert]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[voting share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[voting share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a share of any class of
    shares of a body corporate carrying voting rights under all
    circumstances or by reason of an event that has occurred and is
    continuing or by reason of a condition that has been
    fulfilled; ([action avec droit de vote]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[WTO Member resident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[WTO Member resident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a WTO Member
    resident within the meaning of section 11.1. ([résident d'un membre
    de l'OMC]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-452885 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Major shareholder

**[[2.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.1 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
this Act, a person is a major shareholder of a body corporate if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the aggregate of the shares of any class of voting
  shares of the body corporate that are beneficially owned by the person
  and that are beneficially owned by any entities controlled by the
  person is more than 20 per cent of the outstanding shares of that
  class of voting shares of the body corporate; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the aggregate of the shares of any class of
  non-voting shares of the body corporate that are beneficially owned by
  the person and that are beneficially owned by any entities controlled
  by the person is more than 30 per cent of the outstanding shares of
  that class of non-voting shares of the body corporate.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 479
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Widely held

**[[2.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.2 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
this Act, a body corporate is widely held if it has no major
shareholder.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 479
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations --- distributing company

- **[[2.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may make regulations respecting the determination
  of what constitutes a distributing company for the purposes of this
  Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption --- company

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On the application of a company, the Superintendent
  may determine that it is not or was not a distributing company if the
  Superintendent is satisfied that the determination would not prejudice
  any of its security holders.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption --- class of companies

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent may determine that members of a
  class of companies are not or were not distributing companies if the
  Superintendent is satisfied that the determination would not prejudice
  any security holder of a member of the class.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 54, s. 369
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Control

- **[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of this Act,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person controls a body corporate if securities of
    the body corporate to which are attached more than 50 per cent of
    the votes that may be cast to elect directors of the body corporate
    are beneficially owned by the person and the votes attached to those
    securities are sufficient, if exercised, to elect a majority of the
    directors of the body corporate;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person controls an unincorporated entity, other
    than a limited partnership, if more than 50 per cent of the
    ownership interests, however designated, into which the entity is
    divided are beneficially owned by that person and the person is able
    to direct the business and affairs of the entity;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the general partner of a limited partnership
    controls the limited partnership; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a person controls an entity if the person has any
    direct or indirect influence that, if exercised, would result in
    control in fact of the entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed control

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person who controls an entity is deemed to control
  any entity that is controlled, or deemed to be controlled, by the
  entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed control

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person is deemed to control, within the meaning of
  paragraph (1)(a) or (b), an entity if the aggregate of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any securities of the entity that are beneficially
    owned by that person, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any securities of the entity that are beneficially
    owned by any entity controlled by that person

  is such that, if that person and all of the entities referred to in
  paragraph (b) that beneficially own securities of the entity were one
  person, that person would control the entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Guidelines

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, for any purpose of any provision of
  this Act that refers to control within the meaning of paragraph
  (1)(d), make guidelines respecting what constitutes such control,
  including guidelines describing the policy objectives that the
  guidelines and the relevant provisions of the Act are intended to
  achieve and, if any such guidelines are made, the reference to
  paragraph (1)(d) in that provision shall be interpreted in accordance
  with the guidelines.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 3
- 2001, c. 9, s. 480
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Holding body corporate

**[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** A body corporate is the
holding body corporate of any entity that is its subsidiary.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 4
- 2001, c. 9, s. 481
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsidiary

**[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** An entity is a subsidiary
of another entity if it is controlled by the other entity.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 5
- 2001, c. 9, s. 481
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Affiliated entities

- **[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} One
  entity is affiliated with another entity if one of them is controlled
  by the other or both are controlled by the same person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Affiliated entities

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), for the purposes of
  subsections 270(1) and 288(1), one entity is affiliated with another
  entity if one of them is controlled, determined without regard to
  paragraph 3(1)(d), by the other or both are controlled, determined
  without regard to paragraph 3(1)(d), by the same person.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 6
- 2001, c. 9, s. 482
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Shareholder

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of this Act, a person is a shareholder of a body corporate
  when, according to the securities register of the body corporate, the
  person is the owner of one or more shares of the body corporate or is
  entitled to be entered in the securities register or like record of
  the body corporate as the owner of the share or shares.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Holder of a share

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A reference in this Act to the holding of a share by
  or in the name of any person is a reference to the fact that the
  person is registered or is entitled to be registered in the securities
  register or like record of the body corporate as the holder of that
  share.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Significant interest

- **[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A person
  has a significant interest in a class of shares of a company where the
  aggregate of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any shares of that class beneficially owned by the
    person, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any shares of that class beneficially owned by
    entities controlled by the person

  exceeds 10 per cent of all of the outstanding shares of that class of
  shares of the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Increasing significant interest

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person who has a significant interest in a class of
  shares of a company increases that significant interest in the class
  of shares where the person or any entity controlled by the person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} acquires beneficial ownership of additional shares
    of that class, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} acquires control of any entity that beneficially
    owns shares of that class,

  in such number as to increase the percentage of shares of that class
  that are beneficially owned by the person and by any entities
  controlled by the person.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acting in concert

- **[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of Part VII, if two or more persons have agreed, under any
  agreement, commitment or understanding, whether formal or informal,
  verbal or written, to act jointly or in concert in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} shares of a company that they beneficially own,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} shares or ownership interests that they
    beneficially own of any entity that beneficially owns shares of a
    company, or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} shares or ownership interests that they
    beneficially own of any entity that controls any entity that
    beneficially owns shares of a company,

  those persons shall be deemed to be a single person who is acquiring
  beneficial ownership of the aggregate number of shares of the company
  or shares or ownership interests of the entity that are beneficially
  owned by them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Without limiting the generality of subsection (1),
  any agreement, commitment or understanding by or between two or more
  persons who beneficially own shares of a company or shares or
  ownership interests of any entity referred to in paragraph (1)(b) or
  (c),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} whereby any of them or their nominees may veto any
    proposal put before the board of directors of the company, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} pursuant to which no proposal put before the board
    of directors of the company may be approved except with the consent
    of any of them or their nominees,

  shall be deemed to be an agreement, commitment or understanding
  referred to in subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, persons shall be
  presumed not to have agreed to act jointly or in concert solely by
  reason of the fact that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} one is the proxyholder of one or more of the others
    in respect of shares or ownership interests referred to in
    subsection (1); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} they vote the voting rights attached to shares or
    ownership interests referred to in subsection (1) in the same
    manner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where in the opinion of the Superintendent it is
  reasonable to conclude that an agreement, commitment or understanding
  referred to in subsections (1) and (2) exists by or among two or more
  persons, the Superintendent may designate those persons as persons who
  have agreed to act jointly or in concert.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contravention

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A person contravenes a provision of Part VII if the
  person agrees to act jointly or in concert with one or more other
  persons in such a manner that a deemed single person contravenes the
  provision.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 9
- 2001, c. 9, s. 483
- 2007, c. 6, s. 337
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-9-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 9"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Substantial investment in body corporate

- **[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person has a substantial investment in a body corporate where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the voting rights attached to the aggregate of any
    voting shares of the body corporate beneficially owned by the person
    and by any entities controlled by the person exceed 10 per cent of
    the voting rights attached to all of the outstanding voting shares
    of the body corporate; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the aggregate of any shares of the body corporate
    beneficially owned by the person and by any entities controlled by
    the person represents ownership of greater than 25 per cent of the
    shareholders' equity of the body corporate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Increasing substantial investment in
  body corporate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person who has a substantial investment in a body
  corporate pursuant to paragraph (1)(a) increases that substantial
  investment when the person or any entity controlled by the person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} acquires beneficial ownership of additional voting
    shares of the body corporate in such number as to increase the
    percentage of voting rights attached to the aggregate of the voting
    shares of the body corporate beneficially owned by the person and by
    any entities controlled by the person; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} acquires control of any entity that beneficially
    owns any voting shares of the body corporate in such number as to
    increase the percentage of voting rights attached to the aggregate
    of the voting shares of the body corporate beneficially owned by the
    person and by any entities controlled by the person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person who has a substantial investment in a body
  corporate pursuant to paragraph (1)(b) increases that substantial
  investment when the person or any entity controlled by the person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} acquires beneficial ownership of additional shares
    of the body corporate in such number as to increase the percentage
    of the shareholders' equity of the body corporate represented by the
    aggregate of the shares of the body corporate beneficially owned by
    the person and by any entities controlled by the person; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} acquires control of any entity that beneficially
    owns any shares of the body corporate in such number as to increase
    the percentage of the shareholders' equity of the body corporate
    represented by the aggregate of the shares of the body corporate
    beneficially owned by the person and by any entities controlled by
    the person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}New substantial investment

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} where a person has a substantial investment in a
    body corporate pursuant to paragraph (1)(a) and the person, or any
    entity controlled by the person,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} purchases or otherwise acquires beneficial
      ownership of shares of the body corporate, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} acquires control of any entity that beneficially
      owns shares of the body corporate,

    in such number as to cause the shareholders' equity of the body
    corporate represented by the aggregate of the shares of the body
    corporate beneficially owned by the person and by any entities
    controlled by the person to exceed 25 per cent of the shareholders'
    equity of the body corporate, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where a person has a substantial investment in a
    body corporate pursuant to paragraph (1)(b) and the person or any
    entity controlled by the person

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} purchases or otherwise acquires beneficial
      ownership of voting shares of the body corporate, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} acquires control of any entity that beneficially
      owns voting shares of the body corporate,

    in such number as to cause the voting rights attached to the
    aggregate of the voting shares beneficially owned by the person and
    by any entities controlled by the person to exceed 10 per cent of
    the voting rights attached to all of the outstanding voting shares
    of the body corporate,

  the acquisition is deemed to cause the person to increase a
  substantial investment in the body corporate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Substantial investment in
  unincorporated entity

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A person has a substantial investment in an
  unincorporated entity where the aggregate of any ownership interests,
  however designated, into which the entity is divided, beneficially
  owned by the person and by any entities controlled by the person
  exceeds 25 per cent of all of the ownership interests into which the
  entity is divided.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Increasing substantial investment in
  unincorporated entities

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A person who has a substantial investment in an
  unincorporated entity increases that substantial investment when the
  person or any entity controlled by the person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} acquires beneficial ownership of additional
    ownership interests in the unincorporated entity in such number as
    to increase the percentage of ownership interests in the
    unincorporated entity beneficially owned by the person and by any
    entities controlled by the person; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} acquires control of any entity that beneficially
    owns ownership interests in the unincorporated entity in such number
    as to increase the percentage of ownership interests beneficially
    owned by the person and by any entities controlled by the person.

**[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2005, c. 54,
s. 370\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 11"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}WTO Member resident

- **[[11.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, a WTO
  Member resident is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a natural person who is ordinarily resident in a
    country or territory that is a WTO Member as defined in subsection
    2(1) of the [World Trade Organization Agreement Implementation
    Act](/eng/acts/W-11.8), other than Canada;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a body corporate, association, partnership or other
    organization that is incorporated, formed or otherwise organized in
    a country or territory that is a WTO Member, as defined in
    subsection 2(1) of the [World Trade Organization Agreement
    Implementation Act](/eng/acts/W-11.8), other than Canada, and that
    is controlled,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} directly or indirectly, by one or more persons
      referred to in paragraph (a), or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} by a government of a WTO Member, whether
      federal, state or local, or an agency of one of those governments;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a trust established by one or more persons referred
    to in paragraph (a) or (b) or a trust in which one or more of those
    persons have more than 50 per cent of the beneficial interest; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a body corporate, association, partnership or other
    organization that is controlled, directly or indirectly, by a trust
    referred to in paragraph (c).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a body corporate is controlled by one or more
    persons if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} securities of the body corporate to which are
      attached more than 50 per cent of the votes that may be cast to
      elect directors of the body corporate are beneficially owned by
      the person or persons, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the votes attached to those securities are
      sufficient to elect a majority of the directors of the body
      corporate;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an association, partnership or other organization
    is controlled by one or more persons if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} more than 50 per cent of the ownership interests,
      however designated, into which the association, partnership or
      other organization is divided are beneficially owned by the person
      or persons, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the person or persons are able to direct the
      business and affairs of the association, partnership or other
      organization;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a body corporate, association, partnership or other
    organization is controlled by one or more persons if the person or
    persons have, directly or indirectly, control in fact of the body
    corporate, association, partnership or other organization; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a body corporate, association, partnership or other
    organization that controls another body corporate, association,
    partnership or other organization is deemed to control any body
    corporate, association, partnership or other organization that is
    controlled or deemed to be controlled by the other body corporate,
    association, partnership or other organization.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1999, c. 28, s. 137
:::

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-453028 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of Act

**[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** This Act applies to every
body corporate

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is incorporated or continued under this Act,

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} to which the Trust Companies Act applied immediately
  before the coming into force of this section, or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} to which the Loan Companies Act applied immediately
  before the coming into force of this section,

and that is not discontinued under this Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conflicting provisions

**[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** Where there is a conflict
or inconsistency between a provision of this Act and a provision of the
incorporating instrument of a former-Act company, the provision of this
Act prevails.

## [PART II]{.HLabel1}[Status and Powers]{.HTitleText1} {#h-453037 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Corporate powers

- **[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company has the capacity of a natural person and, subject to this Act,
  the rights, powers and privileges of a natural person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers restricted

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company shall not carry on any business or exercise
  any power that it is restricted by this Act from carrying on or
  exercising, or exercise any of its powers in a manner contrary to this
  Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Business in Canada

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A company may carry on business throughout Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers outside Canada

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Act, a company has the capacity to
  carry on its business, conduct its affairs and exercise its powers in
  any jurisdiction outside Canada to the extent and in the manner that
  the laws of that jurisdiction permit.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Policies and procedures --- integrity or
security

**[[14.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14.1 .sectionLabel}** A company shall
establish and adhere to policies and procedures to protect itself
against threats to its integrity or security, including foreign
interference.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 26, s. 518](#1410887-1437505){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410887-1437505.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No invalidity

**[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** No act of a company,
including any transfer of property to or by a company, is invalid by
reason only that the act or transfer is contrary to the company's
incorporating instrument or this Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}By-law not necessary

**[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** It is not necessary for a
company to pass a by-law in order to confer any particular power on the
company or its directors.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No personal liability

**[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** The shareholders of a
company are not, as shareholders, liable for any liability, act or
default of the company except as otherwise provided by this Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No constructive notice

**[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** No person is affected by
or is deemed to have notice or knowledge of the contents of a document
concerning a company by reason only that the document has been filed
with the Superintendent or the Minister or is available for inspection
at a branch of the company.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authority of directors and officers

- **[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  company and no guarantor of an obligation of a company may assert
  against a person dealing with the company or against a person who has
  acquired rights from the company that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the company's incorporating instrument or any
    by-laws of the company have not been complied with;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the persons named as directors of the company in
    the most recent return sent to the Superintendent under section 499
    are not the directors of the company;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the place named in the incorporating instrument or
    by-laws of the company is not the place where the head office of the
    company is situated;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a person held out by the company as a director,
    officer or representative of the company has not been duly appointed
    or has no authority to exercise the powers and perform the duties
    that are customary in the business of the company or usual for a
    director, officer or representative; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a document issued by any director, officer or
    representative of the company with actual or usual authority to
    issue the document is not valid or not genuine.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- knowledge

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of a person
  who has or ought to have knowledge of a situation described in that
  subsection by virtue of their relationship to the company.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 19
- 2005, c. 54, s. 371
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-19-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 19"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sunset provision

- **[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and (4),
  companies shall not carry on business after June 30, 2026.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, by order, extend by up
  to six months the time during which companies may continue to carry on
  business. No more than one order may be made under this subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order not a regulation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The order is not a regulation for the purposes of the
  [Statutory Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22). However, it shall be
  published in Part II of the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- dissolution

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If Parliament dissolves on the day set out in
  subsection (1) or on any day within the six-month period before that
  day or on any day within an extension ordered under subsection (2),
  companies may continue to carry on business until the end of the 180th
  day after the first day of the first session of the next Parliament.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 20
- 1997, c. 15, s. 341
- 2001, c. 9, s. 484
- 2006, c. 4, s. 202
- 2007, c. 6, s. 338
- 2012, c. 5, s. 163
- 2016, c. 7, s. 117
- 2018, c. 12, s. 354
- [2021, c. 23, s. 153](#1307127-1311333){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1307127-1311333.html"
  role="button"}
- [2024, c. 17, s. 403](#1468528-1482255){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1468528-1482255.html"
  role="button"}
:::

:::

## [PART III]{.HLabel1}[Incorporation, Continuance and Discontinuance]{.HTitleText1} {#h-453078 .Part}

### [Formalities of Incorporation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-453079 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incorporation of company

**[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** On the application of one
or more persons made in accordance with this Act, the Minister may,
subject to this Part, issue letters patent incorporating a company.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restrictions on incorporation

**[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22 .sectionLabel}** Letters patent
incorporating a company may not be issued if the application therefor is
made by or on behalf of

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} Her Majesty in right of Canada or in right of a
  province, an agency of Her Majesty in either of those rights, or an
  entity controlled by Her Majesty in either of those rights;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the government of a foreign country or any political
  subdivision thereof;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} an agency of the government of a foreign country or
  any political subdivision thereof; or

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} an entity, other than a foreign institution or any
  subsidiary of a foreign institution, that is controlled by the
  government of a foreign country or any political subdivision thereof.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsidiary of foreign institution

**[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23 .sectionLabel}** If a proposed company
would be a subsidiary of a foreign institution that is engaged in trust
or loan business and the application for letters patent to incorporate
the company is made by a non-WTO Member foreign institution, letters
patent to incorporate the company may not be issued unless the Minister
is satisfied that treatment as favourable for companies to which this
Act applies exists or will be provided in the jurisdiction in which the
foreign institution principally carries on business, either directly or
through a subsidiary.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 23
- 1999, c. 28, s. 138
- 2001, c. 9, s. 485
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for incorporation

- **[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application for letters patent to incorporate a company setting out
  the names of the first directors of the company shall be filed with
  the Superintendent, together with such other information, material and
  evidence as the Superintendent may require.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publishing notice of intent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Before filing an application referred to in
  subsection (1), the applicant or one of the applicants, as the case
  may be, shall, at least once a week for a period of four consecutive
  weeks, publish, in a form satisfactory to the Superintendent, a notice
  of intention to make the application in the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) and in a newspaper in general
  circulation at or near the place where the head office of the company
  is to be situated.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Objections to incorporation

- **[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  person who objects to the proposed incorporation of a company may,
  within thirty days after the date of the last publication under
  subsection 24(2) in respect of the proposed company, submit the
  objection in writing to the Superintendent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister to be informed

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of an objection under subsection (1), the
  Superintendent shall inform the Minister of the objection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inquiry into objection and report

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of an objection under subsection (1), and
  if the application for the issuance of the letters patent to which the
  objection relates has been received, the Superintendent shall, if
  satisfied that it is necessary and in the public interest to do so,
  hold or cause to be held a public inquiry into the objection as it
  relates to the application and, on completion of the inquiry, the
  Superintendent shall report the findings of the inquiry to the
  Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report to be made available

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Within thirty days after receiving a report under
  subsection (3), the Minister shall make the report available to the
  public.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rules governing proceedings

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the approval of the Governor in Council,
  the Superintendent may make rules governing the proceedings at public
  inquiries held under this section.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Matters for consideration

**[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** Before issuing letters
patent to incorporate a company, the Minister shall take into account
all matters that the Minister considers relevant to the application,
including

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the nature and sufficiency of the financial resources
  of the applicant or applicants as a source of continuing financial
  support for the company;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the soundness and feasibility of the plans of the
  applicant or applicants for the future conduct and development of the
  business of the company;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the business record and experience of the applicant
  or applicants;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the character and integrity of the applicant or
  applicants or, if the applicant or any of the applicants is a body
  corporate, its reputation for being operated in a manner that is
  consistent with the standards of good character and integrity;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} whether the company will be operated responsibly by
  persons with the competence and experience suitable for involvement in
  the operation of a financial institution;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the impact of any integration of the businesses and
  operations of the applicant or applicants with those of the company on
  the conduct of those businesses and operations; and

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} the best interests of the financial system in Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 26
- 2001, c. 9, s. 486
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of letters patent

- **[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} There
  shall be set out in the letters patent incorporating a company

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the company;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the province in which the head office of the
    company is to be situated; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date that the company came, or is to come, into
    existence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provisions in letters patent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may set out in the letters patent
  incorporating a company any provision not contrary to this Act that
  the Minister considers advisable in order to take into account the
  particular circumstances of the proposed company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Terms and conditions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may impose such terms and conditions in
  respect of the issuance of letters patent incorporating a company as
  the Minister considers necessary or appropriate.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 27
- 2005, c. 54, s. 372
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-27-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 27"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of issue of letters patent

**[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** The Superintendent shall
cause to be published in the [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/)
a notice of the issuance of letters patent incorporating a company.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}First directors

**[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** The first directors of a
company are the directors named in the application for letters patent to
incorporate the company.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of letters patent

**[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30 .sectionLabel}** A company comes into
existence on the date provided therefor in its letters patent.

### [Continuance]{.HTitleText2} {#h-453137 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Federal corporations

- **[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A body
  corporate incorporated under the [Canada Business Corporations
  Act](/eng/acts/C-44) or any other Act of Parliament, other than a
  federal credit union, may apply to the Minister for letters patent
  continuing the body corporate as a company under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other corporations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A body corporate incorporated otherwise than by or
  under an Act of Parliament may, if so authorized by the laws of the
  jurisdiction where it is incorporated, apply to the Minister for
  letters patent continuing the body corporate as a company under this
  Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 31
- 1994, c. 24, s. 34(F)
- 2010, c. 12, s. 2124
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-31-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 31"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for continuance

- **[[32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  a body corporate applies for letters patent under subsection 31(1) or
  (2), sections 22 to 26 apply in respect of the application, with such
  modifications as the circumstances require.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special resolution approval

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a body corporate applies for letters patent
  under subsection 31(1) or (2), the application must be duly authorized
  by a special resolution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copy of special resolution

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A copy of the special resolution referred to in
  subsection (2) shall be filed with the application.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power to issue letters patent

- **[[33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On the
  application of a body corporate under subsection 31(1) or (2), the
  Minister may, subject to this Part, issue letters patent continuing
  the body corporate as a company under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issue of letters patent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where letters patent are issued to a body corporate
  under subsection (1), section 27 applies in respect of the issue of
  letters patent, with such modifications as the circumstances require.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of letters patent

**[[34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34 .sectionLabel}** On the day set out in the
letters patent continuing a body corporate as a company under subsection
33(1),

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the body corporate becomes a company as if it had
  been incorporated under this Act; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the letters patent are deemed to be the incorporating
  instrument of the continued company.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copy of letters patent

- **[[35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  a body corporate is continued as a company under this Part, the
  Superintendent shall forthwith send a copy of the letters patent to
  the appropriate official or public body in the jurisdiction in which
  the body corporate was authorized to apply to be continued under this
  Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of issuance of letters patent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent shall cause to be published in the
  [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) a notice of the issuance
  of letters patent continuing a body corporate as a company under this
  Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effects of continuance

**[[36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36 .sectionLabel}** Where a body corporate is
continued as a company under this Part,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the property of the body corporate continues to be
  the property of the company;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the company continues to be liable for the
  obligations of the body corporate;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} an existing cause of action or claim by or against
  the body corporate or any liability of the body corporate to
  prosecution is unaffected;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} a civil, criminal or administrative action or
  proceeding pending by or against the body corporate may continue to be
  prosecuted by or against the company;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} a conviction against, or any ruling, order or
  judgment in favour of or against the body corporate may be enforced by
  or against the company;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} a person who, on the day the body corporate becomes a
  company, was the holder of a security issued by the body corporate is
  not deprived of any right or privilege available to the person at that
  time in respect of the security or relieved of any liability in
  respect thereof, but any such right or privilege may be exercised only
  in accordance with this Act; and

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} the by-laws of the body corporate, except those that
  are in conflict with this Act, continue as the by-laws of the company.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional

- **[[37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding any other provision
  of this Act or the regulations, the Minister may, on the
  recommendation of the Superintendent, by order, grant to a company in
  respect of which letters patent were issued under subsection 33(1)
  permission to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} engage in a business activity specified in the
    order that a company is not otherwise permitted by this Act to
    engage in and that the body corporate continued as the company was
    engaging in at the time the application for the letters patent was
    made;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} continue to have issued and outstanding debt
    obligations the issue of which is not authorized by this Act if the
    debt obligations were outstanding at the time the application for
    the letters patent was made;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 1994, c. 47, s. 202\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} hold assets that a company is not otherwise
    permitted by this Act to hold if the assets were held by the body
    corporate continued as the company at the time the application for
    the letters patent was made;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} acquire and hold assets that a company is not
    otherwise permitted by this Act to acquire or hold if the body
    corporate continued as the company was obliged, at the time the
    application for the letters patent was made, to acquire those
    assets; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} maintain outside Canada any records or registers
    required by this Act to be maintained in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duration

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The permission granted under subsection (1) shall be
  expressed to be granted for a period specified in the order not
  exceeding

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} with respect to any activity described in paragraph
    (1)(a), thirty days after the date of issue of the letters patent
    or, where the activity is conducted pursuant to an agreement
    existing on the date of issue of the letters patent, the expiration
    of the agreement;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} with respect to any matter described in paragraph
    (1)(b), ten years; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} with respect to any matter described in any of
    paragraph (1)(d) to (f), two years.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (2)(a) does not apply to a company if the
  body corporate continued as the company was, at any time before the
  coming into force of this subsection, engaged in any personal property
  leasing activity in which a financial leasing entity, as defined in
  subsection 449(1), is not permitted to engage.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Renewal

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), the Minister may, on the
  recommendation of the Superintendent, by order, renew a permission
  granted by order under subsection (1) with respect to any matter
  described in paragraphs (1)(b) to (e) for such further period or
  periods as the Minister considers necessary.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall not grant to a company any
  permission

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} with respect to matters described in paragraph
    (1)(b), that purports to be effective more than ten years after the
    date of the approval for the company to commence and carry on
    business, unless the Minister is satisfied on the basis of evidence
    on oath provided by an officer of the company that the company will
    not be able at law to redeem at the end of the ten years the
    outstanding debt obligations to which the permission relates; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} with respect to matters referred to in paragraphs
    (1)(d) and (e), that purports to be effective more than ten years
    after the date of the approval for the company to commence and carry
    on business.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 37
- 1994, c. 47, s. 202
- 1997, c. 15, s. 342
- 1999, c. 31, s. 213(F)
- 2007, c. 6, s. 339
- 2009, c. 2, s. 288
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-37-20070420.html "Link to previous version of section 37"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Discontinuance]{.HTitleText2} {#h-453199 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transferring to other federal Acts

- **[[38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} apply, under the [Bank Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01), for
    letters patent continuing the company as a bank or a bank holding
    company under that Act, or amalgamating and continuing the company
    as a bank or a bank holding company under that Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} apply, with the approval in writing of the
    Minister, under the [Canada Business Corporations
    Act](/eng/acts/C-44), for a certificate of continuance as a
    corporation under that Act;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} apply, with the approval in writing of the
    Minister, under the [Canada Cooperatives Act](/eng/acts/C-1.7), for
    a certificate of continuance, or a certificate of continuance and a
    certificate of amalgamation, as a cooperative under that Act;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} apply, under the [Cooperative Credit Associations
    Act](/eng/acts/C-41.01), for letters patent continuing the company
    as an association under that Act, or amalgamating and continuing the
    company as an association under that Act; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} apply, under the [Insurance Companies
    Act](/eng/acts/I-11.8), for letters patent continuing the company as
    a company (other than a mutual company) or an insurance holding
    company under that Act, or amalgamating and continuing the company
    as a company (other than a mutual company) or an insurance holding
    company under that Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for approval

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The approval referred to in paragraph (1)(b) or (c)
  may be given only if the Minister is satisfied that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the company has published, once a week for four
    consecutive weeks in the [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/)
    and in a newspaper in general circulation at or near the place where
    the head office of the company is situated, a notice of its
    intention to apply for the approval;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the company is not carrying on any of the fiduciary
    activities referred to in section 412;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} unless the company is a subsidiary of another
    company and it uses the name of the other company in its name as
    permitted by section 48, the company will not use the word
    "[fiduciaire]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", "fiduciary",
    "[fiducie]{.otherLang lang="fr"}", "loan", "loanco", "prêt", "trust"
    or "trustco" in its name after the certificate referred to in that
    paragraph is issued;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the company does not hold deposits, other than
    deposits that are made by a person who controls the company or by a
    person who has a significant interest in a class of shares of the
    company and that are not insured by the Canada Deposit Insurance
    Corporation; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the application has been authorized by a special
    resolution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Withdrawing application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a special resolution authorizing the application
  for the certificate or letters patent so states, the directors of the
  company may, without further approval of the shareholders, withdraw
  the application before it is acted on.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on other transfers

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A company may not apply to be continued, or to be
  amalgamated and continued, as the case may be, as a body corporate
  other than one referred to in subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, ss. 38, 559
- 1994, c. 24, s. 34(F)
- 1997, c. 15, s. 343
- 2001, c. 9, s. 487
- 2007, c. 6, s. 340
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-38-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 38"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Act ceases to apply

**[[39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39 .sectionLabel}** If a company applies for
a certificate or letters patent referred to in section 38 in accordance
with that section and the certificate is given or the letters patent are
issued, this Act ceases to apply to the company as of the day the
certificate or the letters patent take effect.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 39
- 1994, c. 24, s. 34(F)
- 2007, c. 6, s. 340
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-39-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 39"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[40]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2007, c. 6,
s. 340\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-40-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 40"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Corporate Name]{.HTitleText2} {#h-453226 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibited names

- **[[41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company may not be incorporated under this Act with a name

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is prohibited by an Act of Parliament;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that is, in the opinion of the Superintendent,
    deceptively misdescriptive;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that is the same as or, in the opinion of the
    Superintendent, substantially the same as or confusingly similar to,
    any existing

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} trademark or trade name, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} corporate name of a body corporate,

    except where the trademark or trade name is being changed or the
    body corporate is being dissolved or is changing its corporate name
    and consent to the use of the trademark, trade name or corporate
    name is signified to the Superintendent in such manner as the
    Superintendent may require;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} that is the same as or, in the opinion of the
    Superintendent, substantially the same as or confusingly similar to,
    the known name under or by which any entity carries on business or
    is identified; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} that is reserved under section 45 for another
    company or a proposed company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(a) does not apply with respect to any
  former-Act company incorporated by or under an Act of Parliament that
  expressly authorizes the use of any name that would otherwise be
  prohibited.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 41
- 1996, c. 6, s. 112
- 1997, c. 15, s. 344
- [2014, c. 20, s. 366(E)](#1034903-1145555){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1034903-1145555.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-41-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 41"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Trust company
