**[[42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42 .sectionLabel}** A company that is a trust
company pursuant to subsection 57(2) must have the word "fiduciaire",
"fiduciary", "fiducie", "trust" or "trustco" included in its name.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Affiliated company

**[[43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43 .sectionLabel}** Despite section 41, a
company that is affiliated with another entity may, with the consent of
that entity, be incorporated with, or change its name to, substantially
the same name as that of the affiliated entity.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 43
- 1996, c. 6, s. 113
- 2001, c. 9, s. 488
- 2007, c. 6, s. 341
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-43-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 43"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}French or English form of name

- **[[44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-44 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  name of a company may be set out in its letters patent in an English
  form, a French form, an English form and a French form or in a
  combined English and French form, and the company may use and be
  legally designated by any such form.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternate name

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company may identify itself outside Canada by its
  name in any language and the company may use and be legally designated
  by any such form of its name outside Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other name

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4) and section 260, a company
  may carry on business under or identify itself by a name other than
  its corporate name.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Directions

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where a company is carrying on business under or
  identifying itself by a name other than its corporate name, the
  Superintendent may, by order, direct the company not to use that other
  name if the Superintendent is of the opinion that that other name is a
  name referred to in any of paragraphs 41(1)(a) to (e).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 44
- 1996, c. 6, s. 114
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reserved name

**[[45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45 .sectionLabel}** The Superintendent may,
on request, reserve for ninety days a name for a proposed company or for
a company that intends to change its name.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Directing change of name

- **[[46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  through inadvertence or otherwise a company

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} comes into existence or is continued with a name,
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} on an application to change its name, is granted a
    name

  that is prohibited by section 41, the Superintendent may, by order,
  direct the company to change its name and the company shall comply
  with that direction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revoking name

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a company has been directed under subsection (1)
  to change its name and has not, within sixty days after the service of
  the direction, changed its name to a name that is not prohibited by
  this Act, the Superintendent may revoke the name of the company and
  assign to it a name and, until changed in accordance with section 220
  or 222, the name of the company is thereafter the name so assigned.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 46
- 1996, c. 6, s. 115
- 2001, c. 9, s. 489
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction re trust company name

- **[[47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  entity incorporated or formed by or under an Act of Parliament other
  than this Act shall use the word "fiduciaire", "fiduciary", "fiducie",
  "trust" or "trustco" or any word or words of import equivalent to any
  of those words in its name.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction re loan company name

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No entity incorporated or formed by or under any Act
  of Parliament other than this Act shall use the word "loan", "loanco"
  or "prêt" or any word or words of import equivalent to any of those
  words in its name.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (2) do not apply to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an entity the business of which is not financial
    activities; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an entity that was, on the day immediately
    preceding the day on which

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} subsection (1) comes into force, using the word
      "fiduciaire", "fiduciary", "fiducie", "trust" or "trustco" or any
      word or words of import equivalent to any of those words in its
      name, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} subsection (2) comes into force, using the word
      "loan", "loanco" or "prêt" or any word or words of import
      equivalent to any of those words in its name.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 47
- 1996, c. 6, s. 115
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsidiaries

**[[48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48 .sectionLabel}** Despite subsections 47(1)
and (2), a subsidiary of a company may use the company's name in its
name.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 48
- 1996, c. 6, s. 115
- 2001, c. 9, s. 490
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[reserved
name]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[48.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section, [[reserved name]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a name that
  includes as part thereof the word "assurance", "assurances",
  "insurance", "lifeco", "fiduciaire", "fiduciary", "fiducie", "trust",
  "trustco", "loan", "loanco" or "prêt" or any word or words of import
  equivalent to any of those words.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Termination of control required in
  certain cases

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person, other than a financial institution, who

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is carrying on business in Canada under a reserved
    name, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has control or acquires control of a company,

  shall control the company on the later of

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} one year after this section comes into force, and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} one year after the date of acquisition of the
    control.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person, other than a financial institution, who

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} controls an entity that is not a financial
    institution that carries on business in Canada under a reserved
    name, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has control or acquires control of a company,

  shall control the company on the later of

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} one year after this section comes into force, and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} one year after the date of the acquisition of the
    control.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuing control prohibited

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (3), where a financial
  institution controls an entity that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is not a financial institution,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} carries on business in Canada under a reserved
    name, and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} has control or acquires control of a company,

  the entity shall not control the company on the later of

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} one year after this section comes into force, and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} one year after the date on which the entity
    acquires control of the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (2) to (4) do not apply with respect to a
  person or entity that was carrying on business in Canada under a
  reserved name on the day immediately preceding the day on which those
  subsections come into force.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 6, s. 115
:::

## [PART IV]{.HLabel1}[Organization and Commencement]{.HTitleText1} {#h-453317 .Part}

### [Organization Meetings]{.HTitleText2} {#h-453318 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}First directors' meeting

- **[[49]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} After
  letters patent incorporating a company are issued, a meeting of the
  directors of the company shall be held at which the directors may,
  subject to this Part,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} make by-laws;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} adopt forms of share certificates and corporate
    records;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} authorize the issue of shares of the company;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} appoint officers;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} appoint an auditor to hold office until the first
    meeting of shareholders;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} make banking arrangements; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} deal with any other matters necessary to organize
    the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calling directors' meeting

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An incorporator or a director named in the
  application for letters patent may call the meeting referred to in
  subsection (1) by giving, subject to subsection 185(2), no fewer than
  five days notice of the purpose, time and place of the meeting to each
  director of the company.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calling shareholders' meeting

- **[[50]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-50 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If at
  least five million dollars, or any greater amount that the Minister
  may specify, has been received by a company in respect of which
  letters patent were issued under section 21 from the issue of its
  shares, the directors of the company shall without delay call a
  meeting of the shareholders of the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Meeting of shareholders

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The shareholders of a company shall, by resolution at
  the meeting of shareholders called pursuant to subsection (1),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} approve, amend or reject any by-law made by the
    directors of the company;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to section 172, elect directors to hold
    office for a term expiring not later than the close of the third
    annual meeting of shareholders following the election; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} appoint an auditor to hold office until the close
    of the first annual meeting of shareholders.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 50
- 2001, c. 9, s. 491
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Term of first directors

**[[51]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51 .sectionLabel}** A director named in the
application for letters patent to incorporate a company holds office
until the election of directors at the meeting of shareholders called
pursuant to subsection 50(1).

### [Commencement and Carrying on of Business]{.HTitleText2} {#h-453343 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order to commence and carry on business

- **[[52]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company shall not carry on any business until the Superintendent has,
  by order, approved the commencement and carrying on of business by the
  company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Former-Act companies --- Loan Companies
  Act

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A licence that was issued to a company under section
  76 of the Loan Companies Act and that is in effect immediately before
  the coming into force of this Part is deemed to be an order of the
  Superintendent of indeterminate duration under subsection 53(1) and
  the company remains subject to any and all other restrictions and
  conditions in the licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Former-Act companies

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A licence that was issued to a company under section
  87 of the Trust Companies Act or under section 112 of the Loan
  Companies Act and that is in effect immediately before the coming into
  force of this Part is deemed to be an order of the Superintendent of
  indeterminate duration under subsection 53(1) containing the
  authorization referred to in subsection 57(1) or the designation
  referred to in subsection 57(3), as the case may be, and the company
  remains subject to any and all other restrictions and conditions
  contained in the licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continued company

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Except in respect of a body corporate that is
  continued as a company under this Act for the purposes of forthwith
  amalgamating with one or more bodies corporate and continuing as a
  company under this Act, where letters patent continuing a body
  corporate as a company under this Act are issued, the Superintendent
  shall make an order approving the commencement and carrying on of
  business by the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amalgamated company

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where letters patent amalgamating and continuing two
  or more bodies corporate as a company under this Act are issued, the
  Superintendent shall make an order approving the commencement and
  carrying on of business by the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsection 53(2) and section 56 do not
  apply

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, subsection 53(2) and section
  56 do not apply in respect of a company referred to in subsections (4)
  and (5).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authority to make order

- **[[53]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On
  application by a company, the Superintendent may make an order
  approving the commencement and carrying on of business by the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statement of payments

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An application by a company for an order under
  subsection (1) must contain a statement setting out the amounts paid
  or to be paid by the company in connection with its incorporation and
  organization.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No payments before order

**[[54]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-54 .sectionLabel}** Until an order approving
the commencement and carrying on of business is made for a company, the
company shall not make any payment on account of incorporation or
organization expenses out of moneys received from the issue of the
shares of the company and interest thereon, except reasonable sums

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the remuneration of not more than two officers;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the payment of costs related to the issue of
  shares of the company; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} for the payment of clerical assistance, legal
  services, accounting services, office accommodation at one location,
  office expenses, advertising, stationery, postage and travel expenses.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deposits and investments before order

**[[55]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-55 .sectionLabel}** Where a company comes
into existence but no order approving the commencement and carrying on
of business is made for the company, the company may only

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} deposit, in Canada, paid-in capital of the company in
  another deposit-taking Canadian financial institution; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} invest paid-in capital of the company in unencumbered
  securities of the Government of Canada or the government of any
  province.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for order

- **[[56]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-56 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Superintendent shall not make an order approving the commencement and
  carrying on of business by a company until it has been shown to the
  satisfaction of the Superintendent that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the meeting of shareholders of the company referred
    to in subsection 50(1) has been duly held;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the company has paid-in capital of at least five
    million dollars or any greater amount that is specified by the
    Minister under subsection 50(1);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the expenses of incorporation and organization to
    be borne by the company are reasonable; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} all other relevant requirements of this Act have
    been complied with.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time limit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent shall not make an order approving
  the commencement and carrying on of business by a company more than
  one year after the day on which the company comes into existence.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 56
- 2001, c. 9, s. 492
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorization in order

- **[[57]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  order approving the commencement and carrying on of business by a
  company may, at the discretion of the Superintendent, contain the
  authorization for the company to carry on the activities referred to
  in section 412.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Trust company

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where an order approving the commencement and
  carrying on of business by a company contains the authorization
  referred to in subsection (1), subsection 52(3) or paragraph 58(1)(a),
  the company is a trust company under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mortgage investment company

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An order approving the commencement and carrying on
  of business by a company, other than a company that is a trust company
  pursuant to subsection (2), may, at the discretion of the
  Superintendent, contain a designation that the company is a mortgage
  investment company under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions of order

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An order approving the commencement and carrying on
  of business by a company may contain such conditions or limitations
  that are consistent with this Act and relate to the business of the
  company as the Superintendent deems expedient and necessary.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Variations

- **[[58]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  respect of the order approving the commencement and carrying on of
  business by a company, the Superintendent may at any time, by further
  order,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} add to the order the authorization for the company
    to carry on the activities referred to in section 412, or the
    designation referred to in subsection 57(3),

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} make the order subject to such conditions or
    limitations that are consistent with this Act and that relate to the
    business of the company as the Superintendent deems expedient and
    necessary,

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} amend or revoke any authorization contained in the
    order or any condition or limitation to which the order is subject,
    or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} revoke any designation contained in the order,

  but before making any such further order the Superintendent shall
  provide the company with an opportunity to make representations
  regarding that further order.

- [(2) to (6)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 1996, c. 6, s. 116\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 58
- 1996, c. 6, s. 116
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Public notice

- **[[59]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-59 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On the
  making of an order approving the commencement and carrying on of
  business by a company, the company shall publish a notice of the
  making of the order in a newspaper in general circulation at or near
  the place where the head office of the company is located.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice in [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent shall cause to be published in the
  [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) a notice of the making of
  an order approving the commencement and carrying on of business by a
  company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application to former-Act company

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, this section does not apply to
  a company referred to in subsections 52(2) and (3).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cessation of existence

**[[60]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60 .sectionLabel}** Except for the sole
purpose of winding up the company's affairs, a company ceases to exist
one year after the day on which its incorporating instrument became
effective if it does not obtain an order approving the commencement and
carrying on of business within that year.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Allowed disbursements

- **[[61]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-61 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  an order approving the commencement and carrying on of business is not
  made for a company, no part of the moneys of the company shall be used
  for the payment of incorporation and organization expenses, other than
  remuneration and costs referred to in section 54, unless the payment
  has been approved by a special resolution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to court to settle
  disbursements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the amount allowed by a special resolution for the
  payment of any incorporation and organization expenses referred to in
  subsection (1) is considered insufficient by the directors or if no
  special resolution for the payment of such expenses is passed, the
  directors may apply to any court having jurisdiction in the place
  where the head office of the company is situated to settle and
  determine the amounts to be paid out of any moneys of the company
  before distribution of the balance to the shareholders or, where there
  are no shareholders, to the incorporators.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of application to court

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The directors shall, at least twenty-one days prior
  to the date fixed for the hearing of the application referred to in
  subsection (2), send to the shareholders or incorporators, as the case
  may be, a notice of the application, which notice shall contain a
  statement of the amounts that are proposed to be settled and
  determined by the court.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ratio payable

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In order that the amounts paid and payable under this
  section may be equitably borne by the shareholders or incorporators,
  as the case may be, the directors shall, after the amounts of the
  payments have been approved by special resolution or settled and
  determined by a court, fix the proportionate part thereof chargeable
  to each shareholder or incorporator as the ratio of the amount paid in
  by the shareholder or incorporator to the aggregate of all the amounts
  paid in by the shareholders or incorporators.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of excess

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} After the amounts referred to in this section have
  been paid, the directors shall pay, with any interest earned thereon,
  to the shareholders or incorporators, the respective balances of the
  moneys paid in by them, less the amount chargeable to each shareholder
  or incorporator under subsection (4).

## [PART V]{.HLabel1}[Capital Structure]{.HTitleText1} {#h-453422 .Part}

### [Share Capital]{.HTitleText2} {#h-453423 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power to issue shares

- **[[62]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-62
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Act and the by-laws
  of the company, shares of a company may be issued at such times and to
  such persons and for such consideration as the directors of the
  company may determine.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Shares

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Shares of a company shall be in registered form and
  shall be without nominal or par value.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Shares of former-Act company

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Shares with nominal or par value of a former-Act
  company are deemed to be shares without nominal or par value.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Shares of continued company

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where a body corporate is continued as a company
  under this Act, shares with nominal or par value issued by the body
  corporate before it was so continued are deemed to be shares without
  nominal or par value.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed share conditions

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where any right of a holder of a share with nominal
  or par value of a former-Act company or a body corporate continued as
  a company under this Act, other than a voting right, was stated or
  expressed in terms of the nominal or par value of the share
  immediately before the coming into force of this Part or the
  continuance under this Act, as the case may be, that right is
  thereafter deemed to be the same right stated or expressed without
  reference to the nominal or par value of the share.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Common shares

- **[[63]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-63 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company shall have one class of shares, to be designated as "common
  shares", which are non-redeemable and in which the rights of the
  holders thereof are equal in all respects, and those rights include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the right to vote at all meetings of shareholders
    except where only holders of a specified class of shares are
    entitled to vote;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the right to receive dividends declared on those
    shares; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the right to receive the remaining property of the
    company on dissolution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designations of shares

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No company shall designate more than one class of its
  shares as "common shares" or any variation of that term.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2012, c. 5, s. 164\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continued company

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A body corporate continued as a company under this
  Act that is not in compliance with subsection (2) on the date letters
  patent continuing it as a company are issued shall, within twelve
  months after that date, redesignate its shares to comply with that
  subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (2) do not apply in respect of a
  former-Act company whose shareholders are confined to entities
  incorporated or formed by or under an Act of Parliament or of the
  legislature of a province that are, in the opinion of the directors,
  operating as credit unions or cooperative associations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 63
- 2012, c. 5, s. 164
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-63-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 63"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Classes of shares

- **[[64]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  by-laws of a company may provide for more than one class of shares
  and, if they so provide, shall set out

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the rights, privileges, restrictions and conditions
    attaching to the shares of each class; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the maximum number, if any, of shares of any class
    that the company is authorized to issue.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Shareholder approval

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a by-law referred to in subsection (1) is made,
  the directors of the company shall submit the by-law to the
  shareholders at the next meeting of shareholders.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effective date

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A by-law referred to in subsection (1) is not
  effective until it is confirmed or confirmed with amendments by
  special resolution of the shareholders at the meeting referred to in
  subsection (2).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 64
- 2001, c. 9, s. 493
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Shares issued in series

- **[[65]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-65 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  by-laws of a company may, subject to any limitations set out in them,
  authorize the issue of any class of shares in one or more series and
  may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} fix the maximum number of shares in each series and
    determine the designation, rights, privileges, restrictions and
    conditions attaching to them; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} authorize the directors to do anything referred to
    in paragraph (a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Series participation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If any cumulative dividend or amounts payable on
  return of capital in respect of a series of shares are not paid in
  full, the shares of all series of the same class participate rateably
  in respect of accumulated dividends and return of capital.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Voting rights

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where voting rights are attached to any series of a
  class of shares, the shares of every other series of that class shall
  have the same voting rights.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on series

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} No rights, privileges, restrictions or conditions
  attached to a series of shares authorized under this section confer on
  the series a priority in respect of dividends or return of capital
  over any other series of shares of the same class that are then
  outstanding.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Material to Superintendent

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the directors exercise their authority under
  paragraph (1)(b), the directors shall, before the issue of shares of
  the series, send to the Superintendent particulars of the series of
  shares and a copy of the by-law that granted the authority to the
  directors.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 65
- 2005, c. 54, s. 373
- 2007, c. 6, s. 342(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-65-20060427.html "Link to previous version of section 65"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}One share, one vote

- **[[66]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  voting rights are attached to a share of a company, the voting rights
  may confer only one vote in respect of that share.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a share of a former-Act company issued on or before
    September 27, 1990 that entitled the holder to more than one vote,
    or to a fraction of a vote, in respect of that share; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any share of a former-Act company that is issued
    after September 27, 1990 pursuant to the conversion of a security of
    the former-Act company that was issued with such a conversion
    privilege prior to that date.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Shares non-assessable

**[[67]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-67 .sectionLabel}** Shares issued by a
company after the coming into force of this section are non-assessable
and the shareholders are not liable to the company or to its creditors
in respect thereof.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consideration for share

- **[[68]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-68 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  share of any class of shares of a company shall be issued until it is
  fully paid for in money or, with the approval of the Superintendent,
  in property.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where any share of a company is not fully paid for on
  the day this Part comes into force, the provisions of the Trust
  Companies Act or the Loan Companies Act that applied to the company
  immediately prior to that day and that relate to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the liability of holders of shares of a company
    that are not fully paid for and the enforcement of that liability,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the forfeiture of the share, and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the forfeiture of the right to vote the share

  continue to apply in respect of that share.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other currencies

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} When issuing shares, a company may provide that any
  aspect of the shares relating to money or involving the payment of or
  the liability to pay money be in a currency other than the currency of
  Canada.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Stated capital account

- **[[69]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-69 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company shall maintain a separate stated capital account for each
  class and series of shares it issues.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Addition to stated capital account

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company shall record in the appropriate stated
  capital account the full amount of any consideration it receives for
  any shares it issues.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2), a company may, subject to
  subsection (2.2), record in the stated capital account maintained for
  the shares of a class or series any part of the consideration it
  receives in an exchange if it issues shares

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in exchange for

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} property of a person who immediately before the
      exchange did not deal with the company at arm's length within the
      meaning of that expression in the [Income Tax
      Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3),

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} shares of or another interest in a body
      corporate that immediately before the exchange or because of it
      did not deal with the company at arm's length within the meaning
      of that expression in the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3), or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} property of a person who immediately before the
      exchange dealt with the company at arm's length within the meaning
      of that expression in the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) if the
      person, the company and all of the holders of shares in the class
      or series of shares so issued consent to the exchange;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} under an agreement referred to in subsection
    229(1); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to shareholders of an amalgamating body corporate
    who receive the shares in addition to or instead of securities of
    the amalgamated company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limit on addition to a stated capital
  account

  [(2.2)]{.lawlabel} On the issuance of a share, a company shall not add
  to the stated capital account in respect of the share an amount
  greater than the amount of the consideration it receives for the
  share.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Constraint on addition to a stated
  capital account

  [(2.3)]{.lawlabel} Where a company that has issued any outstanding
  shares of more than one class or series proposes to add to a stated
  capital account that it maintains in respect of a class or series of
  shares an amount that was not received by the company as consideration
  for the issue of shares, the addition must be approved by special
  resolution unless all the issued and outstanding shares are of not
  more than two classes of convertible shares referred to in subsection
  80(4).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Stated capital of former-Act company

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On the coming into force of this Part, a former-Act
  company shall record in the stated capital account maintained for each
  class and series of shares then outstanding an amount that is equal to
  the aggregate of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the aggregate amount paid up on the shares of each
    class and series of shares immediately before the coming into force
    of this Part, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the contributed surplus of the
    company that is attributable to those shares.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contributed surplus entry

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The amount of any contributed surplus recorded in the
  stated capital account pursuant to paragraph (3)(b) shall be deducted
  from the contributed surplus account of the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Share issued before coming into force

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Any amount unpaid in respect of a share issued by a
  former-Act company before the coming into force of this Part and paid
  after the coming into force of this Part shall be recorded in the
  stated capital account maintained by the company for the shares of
  that class or series.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 69
- 1997, c. 15, s. 345
- 2005, c. 54, s. 374
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-69-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 69"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Stated capital of continued company

- **[[70]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-70 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  a body corporate is continued as a company under this Act, the company
  shall record in the stated capital account maintained for each class
  and series of shares then outstanding an amount that is equal to the
  aggregate of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the aggregate amount paid up on the shares of each
    class and series of shares immediately before the body corporate was
    so continued, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the contributed surplus of the
    company that is attributable to those shares.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contributed surplus entry

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The amount of any contributed surplus recorded in the
  stated capital account pursuant to paragraph (1)(b) shall be deducted
  from the contributed surplus account of the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Shares issued before continuance

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Any amount unpaid in respect of a share issued by a
  body corporate before it was continued as a company under this Act and
  paid after it was so continued shall be recorded in the stated capital
  account maintained by the company for the shares of that class or
  series.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pre-emptive right

- **[[71]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-71 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  the by-laws of a company so provide, no shares of any class shall be
  issued unless the shares have first been offered to the shareholders
  holding shares of that class, and those shareholders have a
  pre-emptive right to acquire the offered shares in proportion to their
  holdings of the shares of that class, at such price and on such terms
  as those shares are to be offered to others.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding the existence of a pre-emptive right,
  a shareholder of a company has no pre-emptive right in respect of
  shares of a class to be issued

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for a consideration other than money;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} as a share dividend; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} pursuant to the exercise of conversion privileges,
    options or rights previously granted by the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding the existence of a pre-emptive right,
  a shareholder of a company has no pre-emptive right in respect of
  shares to be issued

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} where the issue of shares to the shareholder is
    prohibited by this Act; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where, to the knowledge of the directors of the
    company, the offer of shares to a shareholder whose recorded address
    is in a country other than Canada ought not to be made unless the
    appropriate authority in that country is provided with information
    in addition to that submitted to the shareholders at the last annual
    meeting.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conversion privileges

- **[[72]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company may issue conversion privileges, options or rights to acquire
  securities of the company, and shall set out the conditions thereof

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the documents that evidence the conversion
    privileges, options or rights; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the securities to which the conversion
    privileges, options or rights are attached.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transferable rights

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Conversion privileges, options and rights to acquire
  securities of a company may be made transferable or non-transferable,
  and options and rights to acquire such securities may be made
  separable or inseparable from any securities to which they are
  attached.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reserved shares

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where a company has granted privileges to convert any
  securities issued by the company into shares, or into shares of
  another class or series, or has issued or granted options or rights to
  acquire shares, if the by-laws limit the number of authorized shares,
  the company shall reserve and continue to reserve sufficient
  authorized shares to meet the exercise of such conversion privileges,
  options and rights.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Holding of own shares

**[[73]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73 .sectionLabel}** Except as provided in
sections 74 to 77, or unless permitted by the regulations, a company
shall not

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} hold shares of the company or of any body corporate
  that controls the company;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} hold any ownership interests of any unincorporated
  entity that controls the company;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} permit any of its subsidiaries to hold any shares of
  the company or of any body corporate that controls the company; or

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} permit any of its subsidiaries to hold any ownership
  interests of any unincorporated entity that controls the company.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Purchase and redemption of shares

- **[[74]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-74
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2) and to its
  by-laws, a company may, with the consent of the Superintendent,
  purchase, for the purpose of cancellation, any shares issued by it, or
  redeem any redeemable shares issued by it at prices not exceeding the
  redemption price thereof calculated according to a formula stated in
  its by-laws or the conditions attaching to the shares.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restrictions on purchase and redemption

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company shall not make any payment to purchase or
  redeem any shares issued by it if there are reasonable grounds for
  believing that the company is, or the payment would cause the company
  to be, in contravention of any regulation referred to in subsection
  473(1) or (2) or any direction made pursuant to subsection 473(3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Donated shares

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A company may accept from any shareholder a share of
  the company surrendered to it as a gift, but may not extinguish or
  reduce a liability in respect of an amount unpaid on any such share
  except in accordance with section 78.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Holding as personal representative

- **[[75]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company may, and may permit its subsidiaries to, hold, in the capacity
  of a personal representative, shares of the company or of any body
  corporate that controls the company or ownership interests in any
  unincorporated entity that controls the company, but only where the
  company or the subsidiary does not have a beneficial interest in the
  shares or ownership interests.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security interest

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company may, and may permit its subsidiaries to, by
  way of a security interest

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} hold shares of the company or of any body corporate
    that controls the company, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} hold any ownership interests of any entity that
    controls the company,

  where the security interest is nominal or immaterial when measured by
  criteria established by the company that have been approved in writing
  by the Superintendent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Saving

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Nothing in subsection (2) precludes a former-Act
  company or any of its subsidiaries from holding any security interest
  held immediately prior to the coming into force of this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 75
- 2005, c. 54, s. 375(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-75-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 75"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- conditions before
acquisition

- **[[75.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company may permit any of its subsidiaries to acquire shares of the
  company through the issuance of those shares by the company to the
  subsidiary if the conditions prescribed for the purposes of this
  subsection are met before the subsidiary acquires the shares.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions after acquisition

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} After a subsidiary has acquired shares under the
  purported authority of subsection (1), the conditions prescribed for
  the purposes of this subsection must be met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-compliance with conditions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a company permits any of its subsidiaries to
  acquire shares of the company under the purported authority of
  subsection (1) and one or more of the conditions prescribed for the
  purposes of subsections (1) and (2) were not met, are not met or cease
  to be met, as the case may be, then, despite section 15 and subsection
  69(2), the company must comply with the prescribed requirements.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 6, s. 343
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation of shares

- **[[76]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), where a
  company purchases shares of the company or fractions thereof or
  redeems or otherwise acquires shares of the company, the company shall
  cancel those shares.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to sell

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a company or any of its subsidiaries, through
  the realization of security, acquires any shares of the company or of
  any body corporate that controls the company or any ownership
  interests in an unincorporated entity that controls the company, the
  company shall, or shall cause its subsidiaries to, as the case may be,
  within six months after the day of the realization, sell or otherwise
  dispose of the shares or ownership interests.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsidiary holding shares

**[[77]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77 .sectionLabel}** Subject to the
regulations, a former-Act company shall cause any subsidiary of the
company that holds shares of the company, or of any body corporate that
controls the company, or any ownership interests of any unincorporated
entity that controls the company to sell or otherwise dispose of those
shares or ownership interests within six months after the day this
section comes into force.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reduction of capital

- **[[78]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-78 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  stated capital of a company may be reduced by special resolution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company shall not reduce its stated capital by
  special resolution if there are reasonable grounds for believing that
  the company is, or the reduction would cause the company to be, in
  contravention of any regulation referred to in subsection 473(1)
  or (2) or any direction made pursuant to subsection 473(3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of special resolution

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A special resolution to reduce the stated capital of
  a company shall specify the stated capital account or accounts from
  which the reduction of stated capital effected by the special
  resolution will be deducted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval by Superintendent

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A special resolution to reduce the stated capital of
  a company has no effect until it is approved in writing by the
  Superintendent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(4.1)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (4) does not apply if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the reduction in the stated capital is made solely
    as a result of changes made to the accounting principles referred to
    in subsection 313(4); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} there is to be no return of capital to shareholders
    as a result of the reduction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for approval

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} No approval to reduce the stated capital of a company
  may be given by the Superintendent unless application therefor is made
  within three months after the time of the passing of the special
  resolution and a copy of the special resolution, together with a
  notice of intention to apply for approval, has been published in the
  [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statements to be submitted

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} In addition to evidence of the passing of a special
  resolution to reduce the stated capital of a company and of the
  publication thereof, statements showing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the number of the company's shares issued and
    outstanding,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the results of the voting by class of shares of the
    company,

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the company's assets and liabilities, and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the reason why the company seeks the reduction of
    capital

  shall be submitted to the Superintendent at the time of the
  application for approval of the special resolution.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 78
- 2007, c. 6, s. 344
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-78-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 78"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recovery by action

- **[[79]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-79 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where
  any money or property was paid or distributed to a shareholder or
  other person as a consequence of a reduction of capital made contrary
  to section 78, a creditor of the company may apply to a court for an
  order compelling the shareholder or other person to pay the money or
  deliver the property to the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Shares held by personal representative

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person holding shares in the capacity of a
  personal representative and registered on the records of the company
  as a shareholder and therein described as the personal representative
  of a named person is personally liable under subsection (1), but the
  named person is subject to all the liabilities imposed by that
  subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An action to enforce a liability imposed by
  subsection (1) may not be commenced more than two years after the date
  of the act complained of.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Remedy preserved

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} This section does not affect any liability that
  arises under section 212.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adjustment of stated capital account

- **[[80]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-80 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On a
  purchase, redemption or other acquisition by a company of shares or
  fractions thereof issued by it, other than shares acquired pursuant to
  section 75 or acquired through the realization of security and sold
  pursuant to subsection 76(2), the company shall deduct from the stated
  capital account maintained for the class or series of shares so
  purchased, redeemed or otherwise acquired an amount equal to the
  result obtained by multiplying the stated capital in respect of the
  shares of that class or series by the number of shares of that class
  or series so purchased, redeemed or otherwise acquired and dividing by
  the number of shares of that class or series outstanding immediately
  before the purchase, redemption or other acquisition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company shall adjust its stated capital account or
  accounts in accordance with any special resolution referred to in
  section 78.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Shares converted to another class

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On a conversion of outstanding shares of a company
  into shares of another class or series, or on a change of outstanding
  shares of the company into shares of another class or series, the
  company shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} deduct from the stated capital account maintained
    for the class or series of shares converted or changed an amount
    equal to the result obtained by multiplying the stated capital of
    the shares of that class or series by the number of shares of that
    class or series converted or changed, and dividing by the number of
    outstanding shares of that class or series immediately before the
    conversion or change; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} record the result obtained under paragraph (a) and
    any additional consideration received pursuant to the conversion or
    change in the stated capital account maintained or to be maintained
    for the class or series of shares into which the shares have been
    converted or changed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Stated capital of convertible shares

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (3) and subject to the
  company's by-laws, where a company issues two classes of shares and
  there is attached to each class a right to convert a share of one
  class into a share of the other class and a share is so converted, the
  amount of stated capital attributable to a share in either class is
  the aggregate of the stated capital of both classes divided by the
  number of outstanding shares of both classes immediately before the
  conversion.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conversion or change of shares

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Shares issued by a company and converted into shares
  of another class or series, or changed under subsection 222(1) into
  shares of another class or series, become issued shares of the class
  or series of shares into which the shares have been converted or
  changed.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Addition to stated capital account

**[[81]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-81 .sectionLabel}** On a conversion of any
debt obligation of a company into shares of a class or series of shares,
the company shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} deduct from the liabilities of the company the
  nominal value of the debt obligation being converted; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} record the result obtained under paragraph (a) and
  any additional consideration received for the conversion in the stated
  capital account maintained or to be maintained for the class or series
  of shares into which the debt obligation has been converted.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Declaration of dividend

- **[[82]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-82 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  directors of a company may declare and a company may pay a dividend by
  issuing fully paid shares of the company or options or rights to
  acquire fully paid shares of the company and, subject to subsection
  (4), the directors of a company may declare and a company may pay a
  dividend in money or property, and where a dividend is to be paid in
  money, the dividend may be paid in a currency other than the currency
  of Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to Superintendent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The directors of a company shall notify the
  Superintendent of the declaration of a dividend at least 15 days
  before the day fixed for its payment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Share dividend

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If shares of a company are issued in payment of a
  dividend, the company shall record in the stated capital account
  maintained or to be maintained for the shares of the class or series
  issued in payment of the dividend the declared amount of the dividend
  stated as an amount of money.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When dividend not to be declared

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The directors of a company shall not declare and a
  company shall not pay a dividend if there are reasonable grounds for
  believing that the company is, or the payment would cause the company
  to be, in contravention of any regulation referred to in subsection
  473(1) or (2) or any direction made pursuant to subsection 473(3).

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2007, c. 6, s. 345\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 82
- 2001, c. 9, s. 494
- 2007, c. 6, s. 345
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-82-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 82"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Subordinated Indebtedness]{.HTitleText2} {#h-453656 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on subordinated indebtedness

- **[[83]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-83 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company shall not issue subordinated indebtedness unless the
  subordinated indebtedness is fully paid for in money or, with the
  approval of the Superintendent, in property.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}References to subordinated indebtedness

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person shall not in any prospectus, advertisement,
  correspondence or literature relating to any subordinated indebtedness
  issued or to be issued by a company refer to the subordinated
  indebtedness otherwise than as subordinated indebtedness.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed not to be a deposit

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subordinated indebtedness issued by a company is
  deemed not to be a deposit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other currencies

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} When issuing subordinated indebtedness, a company may
  provide that any aspect of the subordinated indebtedness relating to
  money or involving the payment of or the liability to pay money in
  relation thereto be in a currency other than that of Canada including,
  without restricting the generality of the foregoing, the payment of
  any interest thereon.

### [Security Certificates and Transfers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-453666 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[84]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-84 .sectionLabel}** In this section and
sections 85 to 138,

[[adverse claim]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[adverse claim]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a claim that a
    transfer was or would be wrongful or that a particular adverse
    person is the owner of or has an interest in a
    security; ([opposition]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[[bona fide]{.otherLang lang="la"} purchaser]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[[bona fide]{.otherLang lang="la"}
    purchaser]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a purchaser for value in good
    faith and without notice of any adverse claim who takes delivery of
    a security in bearer form or order form or of a security in
    registered form issued to the purchaser or endorsed to the purchaser
    or endorsed in blank; ([acheteur de bonne foi]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[clearing agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[clearing agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person designated as
    a recognized clearing agency by the Superintendent; ([agence de
    compensation et de dépôt]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[delivery]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[delivery]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means voluntary transfer of
    possession; ([livraison]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou
    [remise]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[fungible]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[fungible]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of securities, means
    securities of which any unit is, by nature or usage of trade, the
    equivalent of any other like unit; ([fongibles]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[genuine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[genuine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means free of forgery or
    counterfeit; ([authentique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[good faith]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[good faith]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means honesty in fact in the
    conduct of the transaction concerned; ([bonne foi]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[over-issue]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[over-issue]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the issue of securities in
    excess of any maximum number of securities that the issuer is
    authorized to issue; ([émission excédentaire]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[purchaser]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[purchaser]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who takes an
    interest in a security by sale, mortgage, pledge, issue, reissue,
    gift or any other voluntary
    transaction; ([acquéreur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[securities broker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[securities broker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who is
    engaged for all or part of the person's time in the business of
    buying and selling securities and who, in the transaction concerned,
    acts for, or buys a security from, or sells a security to, a
    customer; ([courtier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[security]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[security]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or [[security
    certificate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an instrument issued by a
    company that is

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in bearer, order or registered form,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} of a type commonly dealt in on securities
      exchanges or markets or commonly recognized in any area in which
      it is issued or dealt in as a medium for investment,

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} one of a class or series or by its terms
      divisible into a class or series of instruments, and

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} evidence of a share, participation or other
      interest in or obligation of a company,

    but does not include an instrument evidencing a deposit; ([valeur
    mobilière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou [certificat de valeur
    mobilière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[trust indenture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[trust indenture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning given that
    expression by section 299; ([acte de fiducie]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[unauthorized]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[unauthorized]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a signature or
    an endorsement, means a signature or an endorsement made without
    actual, implied or apparent authority, and includes a forgery; ([non
    autorisé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[uncertificated security]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[uncertificated security]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a security,
    not evidenced by a security certificate, the issue and any transfer
    of which is registered or recorded in records maintained for that
    purpose by or on behalf of a company; ([valeur mobilière sans
    certificat]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[valid]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[valid]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means issued in accordance with the
    applicable law or validated under section
    100. ([valide]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provisions governing transfers of
securities

**[[85]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85 .sectionLabel}** The transfer of a
security is governed by sections 86 to 138.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security a negotiable instrument

- **[[86]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-86 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  security is a negotiable instrument but, in the case of any
  inconsistency between the provisions of the [Bills of Exchange
  Act](/eng/acts/B-4) and this Act, this Act prevails to the extent of
  the inconsistency.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Bearer form

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A security is in bearer form if it is payable to
  bearer according to its terms and not by reason of any endorsement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order form

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A security is in order form where the security is not
  a share and, by its terms, it is payable to the order or assigns of
  any person therein specified with reasonable certainty or to the
  person or the person's order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registered form

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A security is in registered form if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it specifies a person entitled to the security or
    to the rights it evidences, and its transfer is capable of being
    recorded in a securities register; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it bears a statement that it is in registered form.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Status of guarantor

**[[87]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-87 .sectionLabel}** A guarantor for an issuer
of a security is deemed to be an issuer to the extent of the guarantee,
whether or not the guarantor's obligation is noted on the security.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rights of holder

- **[[88]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-88
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to Part VII, every security
  holder is entitled at the holder's option to a security certificate
  that complies with this Act or to a non-transferable written
  acknowledgement of the holder's right to obtain a security certificate
  that complies with this Act from a company in respect of the
  securities of that company held by the security holder.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fee for security certificate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company may charge a fee, not exceeding a
  prescribed amount, for a security certificate issued in respect of a
  transfer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Joint holders

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A company is not required to issue more than one
  security certificate in respect of securities held jointly by several
  persons, and delivery of a security certificate to one of several
  joint holders is sufficient delivery to all joint holders of the
  security.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 88
- 1999, c. 31, s. 214
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Signatures

- **[[89]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-89 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  security certificate shall be signed by or bear the printed or
  otherwise mechanically reproduced signature of at least one of the
  following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a director or officer of the company;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a registrar or transfer agent of the company or a
    branch transfer agent or a natural person on their behalf; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a trustee who certifies it in accordance with a
    trust indenture.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuation of validity of signature

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a security certificate contains a person's printed
  or mechanically reproduced signature, the company may issue the
  security certificate even if the person has ceased to be a director or
  officer of the company. The security certificate is as valid as if the
  person were a director or officer at the date of its issue.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 89
- 2005, c. 54, s. 377
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-89-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 89"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of share certificate

**[[90]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-90 .sectionLabel}** There shall be stated on
the face of each share certificate issued by a company after the coming
into force of this section

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the company;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a statement that the company is subject to the [Trust
  and Loan Companies Act](/eng/acts/T-19.8);

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name of the person to whom the share certificate
  is issued; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the number and class of shares and the designation of
  any series that the certificate represents.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restrictions and charges

- **[[91]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-91 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  charge in favour of a company and no restriction on transfer other
  than a constraint under Part VII is effective against a transferee of
  a security issued by the company if the transferee has no actual
  knowledge of the charge or restriction unless it or a reference to it
  is noted conspicuously on the security certificate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No restriction

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If any of the issued shares of a distributing company
  remain outstanding and are held by more than one person, the company
  may not restrict the transfer or ownership of its shares except by way
  of a constraint under Part VII.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuance

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a body corporate that is continued as a company
  under this Act has outstanding security certificates and the words
  "private company" or "private corporation" appear on the certificates,
  those words are deemed to be a notice of a charge or restriction for
  the purposes of subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 91
- 2005, c. 54, s. 378
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-91-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 91"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Particulars of class

- **[[92]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-92 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} There
  shall be stated legibly on a share certificate issued after the coming
  into force of this section by a company that is authorized to issue
  shares of more than one class or series

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the rights, privileges, restrictions and conditions
    attached to the shares of each class and series existing when the
    share certificate is issued; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that the class or series of shares that the
    certificate represents has rights, privileges, restrictions or
    conditions attached thereto and that the company will furnish a
    shareholder, on demand and without charge, with a full copy of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the text of the rights, privileges, restrictions
      and conditions attached to each class authorized to be issued and
      to each series in so far as those rights, privileges, restrictions
      and conditions have been fixed by the directors, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the text of the authority of the directors, if
      the directors are so authorized, to fix the rights, privileges,
      restrictions and conditions of subsequent series of shares.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a share certificate issued by a company
  contains the statement mentioned in paragraph (1)(b), the company
  shall provide a shareholder, on demand and without charge, with a full
  copy of the texts referred to in subparagraphs (1)(b)(i) and (ii).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fractional share

**[[93]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-93 .sectionLabel}** A company may issue a
certificate for a fractional share or may issue in place thereof a scrip
certificate in bearer form that entitles the holder to receive a
certificate for a full share by exchanging scrip certificates
aggregating a full share.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Scrip certificates

**[[94]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-94 .sectionLabel}** The directors of a
company may attach conditions to any scrip certificate issued by the
company, including conditions that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the scrip certificate becomes void if not exchanged
  for a share certificate representing a full share before a specified
  date; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any shares for which the scrip certificate is
  exchangeable may, notwithstanding any pre-emptive right, be issued by
  the company to any person and the proceeds thereof may be distributed
  rateably to the holders of all the scrip certificates.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Holders of fractional shares

- **[[95]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-95 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  holder of a fractional share issued by a company is not entitled to
  exercise voting rights or to receive a dividend in respect of the
  fractional share.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Holders of scrip certificates

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A holder of a scrip certificate is not entitled to
  exercise voting rights or to receive a dividend in respect of the
  scrip certificate.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dealings with registered owner

- **[[96]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-96 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company or a trustee within the meaning of section 299 may, subject to
  subsections 140(5) to (7) and sections 141 to 144 and 148, treat the
  registered owner of a security as the person exclusively entitled to
  vote, to receive notices, to receive any interest, dividend or other
  payment in respect of the security and to exercise all of the rights
  and powers of an owner of the security.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Constructive registered holder

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (1), a company may treat a
  person as a registered security holder entitled to exercise all of the
  rights of the security holder that the person represents, if that
  person provides the company with evidence as described in subsection
  130(4) that the person is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the heir or personal representative of a deceased
    security holder or the personal representative of the heirs of the
    deceased security holder;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the personal representative of a registered
    security holder who is a minor, an incompetent person or a missing
    person; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a liquidator of, or a trustee in bankruptcy for, a
    registered security holder.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permissible registered holder

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a person on whom the ownership of a security of a
  company devolves by operation of law, other than a person described in
  subsection (2), provides proof of that person's authority to exercise
  rights or privileges in respect of a security of the company that is
  not registered in the person's name, the company shall, subject to
  this Act, treat that person as entitled to exercise those rights or
  privileges.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Immunity of company

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A company is not required to inquire into the
  existence of, or see to the performance or observance of, any duty
  owed to a third person by a registered holder of any of its securities
  or by anyone whom it treats, as permitted or required by this Part, as
  the owner or registered holder thereof.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 96
- 2005, c. 54, s. 379
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-96-20060427.html "Link to previous version of section 96"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minors

**[[97]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-97 .sectionLabel}** If a minor exercises any
rights of ownership in the securities of a company, no subsequent
repudiation or avoidance is effective against the company.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 97
- 2005, c. 54, s. 380(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-97-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 97"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Joint shareholders

**[[98]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-98 .sectionLabel}** A company may treat as
owners of a security the survivors of persons to whom the security was
issued as joint holders, if the company receives proof satisfactory to
it of the death of any of the joint holders.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transmission of securities

- **[[99]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-99
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the provisions of Part
  VII and any applicable law relating to the collection of taxes, a
  person referred to in paragraph 96(2)(a) is entitled to become
  registered as the owner of a security, or to designate another person
  to be registered as the owner of a security, if the person referred to
  in paragraph 96(2)(a) delivers to the company or its transfer agent

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the original grant of probate or of letters of
    administration, or a copy thereof certified to be a true copy by

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the court that granted the probate or letters of
      administration,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a company that is a trust company pursuant to
      subsection 57(2) or a trust company incorporated by or under the
      laws of a province, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a lawyer or notary acting on behalf of the
      person referred to in paragraph 96(2)(a), or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of transmission by notarial will in the
    Province of Quebec, a copy thereof authenticated pursuant to the
    laws of that Province,

  together with

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an affidavit or declaration of transmission made by
    the person referred to in paragraph 96(2)(a) that states the
    particulars of the transmission, and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the security certificate that was owned by the
    deceased holder

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a transfer to the person referred
      to in paragraph 96(2)(a), with or without the endorsement of that
      person, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a transfer to any other person,
      endorsed in accordance with section 114,

    and accompanied by any assurance the company may require under
    section 130.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Excepted transmissions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (1), if the laws of the
  jurisdiction governing the transmission of a security of a deceased
  holder do not require a grant of probate or of letters of
  administration in respect of the transmission, a personal
  representative of the deceased holder is entitled, subject to Part VII
  and any applicable law relating to the collection of taxes, to become
  registered as the owner or to designate a person to be registered as
  the owner, if the personal representative delivers to the company or
  its transfer agent the following documents, namely,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the security certificate that was owned by the
    deceased holder; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} reasonable proof of the governing laws, of the
    deceased holder's interest in the security and of the right of the
    personal representative or the designated person to become the
    registered shareholder.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right of company to treat as owner

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to Part VII, delivery of the documents
  referred to in this section empowers a company or its transfer agent
  to record in a securities register the transmission of a security from
  the deceased holder to a person referred to in paragraph 96(2)(a) or
  to such person as the person referred to in that paragraph may
  designate and, thereafter, to treat the person who becomes so
  registered as the owner of that security.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Over-issue

- **[[100]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-100 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  provisions of this Part that validate a security or compel its issue
  or reissue do not apply to the extent that a validation, issue or
  reissue would result in over-issue, but

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if a valid security similar in all respects to the
    security involved in the over-issue is reasonably available for
    purchase, the person entitled to the validation or issue may compel
    the issuer to purchase and deliver such a security to that person
    against surrender of the security that the person holds; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if a valid security similar in all respects to the
    security involved in the over-issue is not reasonably available for
    purchase, the person entitled to the validation or issue may recover
    from the issuer an amount equal to the price the last purchaser for
    value paid for the invalid security.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retroactive validation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where an issuer is subsequently authorized to issue
  securities of a number equal to or exceeding the number of securities
  previously authorized plus the amount of the securities over-issued,
  the securities so over-issued are valid from the date of their issue.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment not a purchase or redemption

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A purchase or payment by an issuer under
  subsection (1) is not a purchase or payment in respect of which
  section 74 or 80 applies.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Burden of proof

**[[101]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-101 .sectionLabel}** In any action on a
security,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} unless specifically denied in the pleadings, each
  signature on the security or in a necessary endorsement is admitted;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a signature on the security is presumed to be genuine
  and authorized but, if the effectiveness of the signature is put in
  issue, the burden of establishing that it is genuine and authorized is
  on the party claiming under the signature;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if a signature is admitted or established, production
  of the instrument entitles a holder to recover on it unless the
  defendant establishes a defence or a defect going to the validity of
  the security; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the defendant establishes that a defence or defect
  exists, the plaintiff has the burden of establishing that the defence
  or defect is ineffective against the plaintiff or any person under
  whom the plaintiff claims.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Securities fungible

**[[102]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-102 .sectionLabel}** Unless otherwise
agreed, and subject to any applicable law, regulation or stock exchange
rule, a person required to deliver securities may deliver any security
of the specified issue in bearer form or registered in the name of the
transferee or endorsed to the transferee or in blank.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of defect

- **[[103]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-103 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Even
  against a purchaser for value and without notice of a defect going to
  the validity of a security, the terms of the security include those
  stated on the security and those incorporated therein by reference to
  another instrument, statute, rule, regulation or order to the extent
  that the terms so referred to do not conflict with the stated terms,
