  but such a reference is not of itself notice to a purchaser for value
  of a defect going to the validity of the security, notwithstanding
  that the security expressly states that a person accepting it admits
  the notice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Purchaser for value

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A security is valid in the hands of a purchaser for
  value without notice of any defect going to its validity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Lack of genuineness

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Except as provided in section 104, the fact that a
  security is not genuine is a complete defence even against a purchaser
  for value and without notice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ineffective defences

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} All defences of an issuer, including non-delivery and
  conditional delivery of a security but not including lack of
  genuineness, are ineffective against a purchaser for value without
  notice of the particular defence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Staleness as defect notice

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} After an event that creates a right to immediate
  performance of the principal obligation evidenced by a security, or
  that sets a date on or after which a security is to be presented or
  surrendered for redemption or exchange, a purchaser is deemed to have
  notice of any defect in its issue or of any defence of the issuer

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the event requires the payment of money or the
    delivery of securities, or both, on presentation or surrender of the
    security, and the funds or securities are available on the date set
    for payment or exchange, and the purchaser takes the security more
    than one year after that date; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the purchaser takes the security more than two
    years after the date set for presentation or surrender or the date
    on which the performance became due.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unauthorized signature

**[[104]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-104 .sectionLabel}** An unauthorized
signature on a security before or in the course of issue is ineffective,
except that the signature is effective in favour of a purchaser for
value and without notice of the lack of authority, if the signing has
been done by

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an authenticating trustee, registrar, transfer agent
  or other person entrusted by the issuer with the signing of the
  security, or of similar securities, or their immediate preparation for
  signing; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} an employee of the issuer or of a person referred to
  in paragraph (a) who, in the ordinary course of the employee's duties,
  handles the security.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Completion or alteration

- **[[105]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-105
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where a security contains the
  signatures necessary to its issue or transfer but is incomplete in any
  other respect,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any person may complete it by filling in the blanks
    in accordance with the person's authority; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} notwithstanding that the blanks are incorrectly
    filled in, the security as completed is enforceable by a purchaser
    who took it for value and without notice of the incorrectness.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Enforceability

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A completed security that has been improperly
  altered, even if fraudulently altered, remains enforceable, but only
  according to its original terms.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Warranties of agents

- **[[106]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-106 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person signing a security, as authenticating trustee, registrar,
  transfer agent or other person entrusted by the issuer with the
  signing of the security, warrants to a purchaser for value without
  notice that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the security is genuine;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person's acts in connection with the issue of
    the security are within the person's authority; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person has reasonable grounds for believing
    that the security is in the form and within the amount the issuer is
    authorized to issue.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation of liability

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Unless otherwise agreed, a person referred to in
  subsection (1) does not assume any further liability for the validity
  of a security.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Title of purchaser

- **[[107]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-107
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to Part VII, on delivery of
  a security the purchaser acquires the rights in the security that the
  purchaser's transferor had or had authority to convey, except that the
  position of a purchaser who has been a party to any fraud or
  illegality affecting the security or who as a prior holder had notice
  of an adverse claim is not improved by taking from a later [bona
  fide]{.otherLang lang="la"} purchaser.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Title of [bona fide]{.otherLang
  lang="la"} purchaser

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A [bona fide]{.otherLang lang="la"} purchaser, in
  addition to acquiring the rights of a purchaser, also acquires the
  security free from any adverse claim.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limited interest purchaser

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A purchaser of a limited interest acquires rights
  only to the extent of the interest purchased.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed notice of adverse claim

**[[108]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-108 .sectionLabel}** A purchaser of a
security, or any securities broker for a seller or purchaser, is deemed
to have notice of an adverse claim if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the security, whether in bearer form or registered
  form, has been endorsed "for collection" or "for surrender" or for
  some other purpose not involving transfer; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the security is in bearer form and has on it a
  statement that it is the property of a person other than the
  transferor, except that the mere writing of a name on a security is
  not such a statement.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of fiduciary duty

**[[109]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-109 .sectionLabel}** Notwithstanding that a
purchaser, or any securities broker for a seller or purchaser, has
notice that a security is held for a third person by, or is registered
in the name of or endorsed by, a fiduciary, neither the purchaser nor
the securities broker has any duty to inquire into the rightfulness of
the transfer or any notice of an adverse claim, except that if the
purchaser or securities broker for the seller or purchaser knows that
the consideration is to be used for, or that the transaction is for, the
personal benefit of the fiduciary or is otherwise in breach of the
fiduciary's duty, the purchaser or securities broker is deemed to have
notice of an adverse claim.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Staleness as notice

**[[110]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-110 .sectionLabel}** An event that creates a
right to immediate performance of the principal obligation evidenced by
a security or that sets a date on or after which the security is to be
presented or surrendered for redemption or exchange is not of itself
notice of an adverse claim, except in the case of a purchase

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} made more than one year after any date set for such a
  presentation or surrender; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} made more than six months after any date set for
  payment of money against such a presentation or surrender if funds are
  available for payment on that date.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Warranties to issuer

- **[[111]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-111 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who presents a security for registration of transfer or for
  payment or exchange warrants to the issuer that the person is entitled
  to the registration, payment or exchange, except that a purchaser for
  value without notice of an adverse claim who receives a new, reissued
  or re-registered security on registration of transfer warrants only
  that the purchaser has no knowledge of any unauthorized signature in a
  necessary endorsement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Warranties to purchaser

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person by transferring a security to a purchaser
  for value warrants only that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the transfer is effective and rightful;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the security is genuine and has not been materially
    altered; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person knows of nothing that might impair the
    validity of the security.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Warranties of intermediary

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where a security is delivered by an intermediary
  known by the purchaser to be entrusted with delivery of the security
  on behalf of another or with collection of a draft or other claim to
  be collected against that delivery, the intermediary by that delivery
  warrants only the intermediary's own good faith and authority even if
  the intermediary has purchased or made advances against the draft or
  other claim to be collected against the delivery.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Warranties of pledgee

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A pledgee or other holder for purposes of security
  who redelivers a security received, or after payment and on order of
  the debtor delivers that security to a third person, gives only the
  warranties of an intermediary under subsection (3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Warranties of securities broker

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A securities broker gives to the broker's customer,
  to the issuer and to a purchaser, as the case may be, the warranties
  provided in subsections (1) to (4) and has the rights and privileges
  of a purchaser under those subsections, and those warranties of and in
  favour of the broker acting as an agent are in addition to warranties
  given by the broker's customer and warranties given in favour of the
  broker's customer.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to compel endorsement

**[[112]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-112 .sectionLabel}** Where a security in
registered form is delivered to a purchaser without a necessary
endorsement, the purchaser may become a [bona fide]{.otherLang
lang="la"} purchaser only as of the time the endorsement is supplied,
but against the transferor the transfer is complete on delivery and the
purchaser has a specifically enforceable right to have any necessary
endorsement supplied.

Definition of [[appropriate person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

- **[[113]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-113 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section, section 114, subsections 121(1), 124(4) and 129(1) and
  section 133, [[appropriate person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person specified by the security or by special
    endorsement to be entitled to the security;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if a person described in paragraph (a) is described
    as a fiduciary but is no longer serving in the described capacity,
    either that person or that person's successor;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the security or endorsement mentioned in
    paragraph (a) specifies more than one person as fiduciaries and one
    or more of those persons are no longer serving in the described
    capacity, the remaining fiduciary or fiduciaries, whether or not a
    successor has been appointed;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if a person described in paragraph (a) is a natural
    person and is without capacity to act by reason of death,
    incompetence, minority or other reason, the person's fiduciary;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the security or endorsement mentioned in
    paragraph (a) specifies more than one person with right of
    survivorship and by reason of death not all of the persons can sign,
    the survivor or survivors;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a person having power to sign under any applicable
    law or a power of attorney; or

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} to the extent that a person described in any of
    paragraphs (a) to (f) may act through an agent, the person's
    authorized agent.

- Determining an [[appropriate person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Whether the person signing is an appropriate person
  is determined as of the time of signing, and an endorsement by such a
  person does not become unauthorized for the purposes of this Part by
  reason of any subsequent change of circumstances.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Endorsement

- **[[114]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-114 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  endorsement of a security in registered form is made when an
  appropriate person signs, either on the security or on a separate
  document, an assignment or transfer of the security or a power to
  assign or transfer it, or when the signature of an appropriate person
  is written without more on the back of the security.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special or blank

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An endorsement may be special or in blank.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Blank endorsement

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An endorsement in blank includes an endorsement to
  bearer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special endorsement

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A special endorsement specifies the person to whom
  the security is to be transferred, or who has power to transfer it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right of holder

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A holder may convert an endorsement in blank into a
  special endorsement.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Immunity of endorser

**[[115]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-115 .sectionLabel}** Unless otherwise
agreed, the endorser by the endorsement assumes no obligation that the
security will be honoured by the issuer.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Partial endorsement

**[[116]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-116 .sectionLabel}** An endorsement
purporting to be an endorsement of only part of a security representing
units intended by the issuer to be separately transferable is effective
to the extent of the endorsement.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of failure by fiduciary to comply

**[[117]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-117 .sectionLabel}** Failure of a fiduciary
to comply with a controlling instrument or with the law of the
jurisdiction governing the fiduciary relationship, including any law
requiring the fiduciary to obtain court approval of a transfer, does not
render the fiduciary's endorsement unauthorized for the purposes of this
Part.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of endorsement without delivery

**[[118]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-118 .sectionLabel}** An endorsement of a
security, whether special or in blank, does not constitute a transfer
until delivery of the security on which it appears or, if the
endorsement is on a separate document, until delivery of both the
security and that document.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Endorsement in bearer form

**[[119]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-119 .sectionLabel}** An endorsement of a
security in bearer form may give notice of an adverse claim under
section 108 but does not otherwise affect any of the holder's rights.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of unauthorized endorsement

- **[[120]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-120 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  owner of a security may assert the ineffectiveness of an endorsement
  against the issuer or any purchaser, other than a purchaser for value
  and without notice of an adverse claim, who has in good faith received
  a new, reissued or re-registered security on registration of transfer,
  unless the owner

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has ratified an unauthorized endorsement of the
    security; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is otherwise precluded from impugning the
    effectiveness of an unauthorized endorsement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability of issuer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An issuer who registers the transfer of a security on
  an unauthorized endorsement is liable for improper registration.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Warranties of guarantor of signature

- **[[121]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-121 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who guarantees the signature of an endorser of a security
  warrants that, at the time of signing,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the signature was genuine;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the signer was an appropriate person to endorse;
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the signer had legal capacity to sign.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation of liability

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person who guarantees the signature of an endorser
  does not otherwise warrant the rightfulness of the transfer to which
  the signature relates.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Warranties of guarantor of endorsement

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person who guarantees the endorsement of a security
  warrants both the signature and the rightfulness, in all respects, of
  the transfer to which the signature relates, but an issuer may not
  require a guarantee of endorsement as a condition to registration of
  transfer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extent of warrantor's liability

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The warranties referred to in subsections (1) to (3)
  are made to any person who, relying on the guarantee, takes or deals
  with the security, and the guarantor is liable to such a person for
  any loss resulting from breach of warranty.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Constructive delivery of a security

**[[122]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-122 .sectionLabel}** Delivery to a purchaser
occurs when

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the purchaser or a person designated by the purchaser
  acquires possession of a security;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the purchaser's securities broker acquires possession
  of a security specially endorsed to or issued in the name of the
  purchaser;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the purchaser's securities broker sends the purchaser
  confirmation of the purchase and the broker in the broker's records
  identifies a specific security as belonging to the purchaser; or

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} in respect of an identified security to be delivered
  while still in the possession of a third person, that person
  acknowledges that it is held for the purchaser.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Constructive ownership of security

- **[[123]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-123 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  purchaser is the owner of a security held for the purchaser by a
  securities broker, but a purchaser is not a holder except in the cases
  referred to in paragraphs 122(b) and (c).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ownership of part of fungible bulk

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a security is part of a fungible bulk, a purchaser
  of the security is the owner of the proportionate interest in the
  fungible bulk.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to securities broker of adverse
  claim

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Notice of an adverse claim received by a securities
  broker or by a purchaser after the broker takes delivery as a holder
  for value is not effective against the broker or the purchaser, except
  that, as between the broker and the purchaser, the purchaser may
  demand delivery of an equivalent security in respect of which no
  notice of an adverse claim has been received.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delivery of security

- **[[124]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-124
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless otherwise agreed, if a sale
  of a security is made on a stock exchange or otherwise through
  securities brokers,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the selling customer fulfils the customer's duty to
    deliver when the customer delivers the security to the selling
    securities broker or to a person designated by the selling
    securities broker or causes an acknowledgement to be made to the
    selling securities broker that it is held for the selling securities
    broker; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the selling securities broker, including a
    correspondent broker, acting for a selling customer fulfils the
    securities broker's duty to deliver by delivering the security or a
    like security to the buying securities broker or to a person
    designated by the buying securities broker or by effecting clearance
    of the sale in accordance with the rules of the exchange on which
    the transaction took place.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty to deliver

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Except as otherwise provided in this section and
  unless otherwise agreed, a transferor's duty to deliver a security
  under a contract of purchase is not fulfilled until the transferor
  delivers the security in negotiable form to the purchaser or to a
  person designated by the purchaser, or causes an acknowledgement to be
  made to the purchaser that the security is held for the purchaser.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delivery to securities broker

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A sale to a securities broker purchasing for the
  securities broker's own account is subject to subsection (2) and not
  subsection (1), unless the sale is made on a stock exchange.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transfer through clearing agency

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a security shown in the records of a clearing
  agency is evidenced by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a security certificate in the custody of the
    clearing agency or a custodian, or a nominee of either, subject to
    the instructions of the clearing agency, and is in bearer form or
    endorsed in blank by an appropriate person or registered in the name
    of the clearing agency or a custodian, or of a nominee of either, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an uncertificated security registered or recorded
    in records maintained by or on behalf of the company in the name of
    the clearing agency or a custodian, or of a nominee of either,
    subject to the instructions of the clearing agency,

  then, in addition to other methods, a transfer or pledge of the
  security or any interest therein may be effected by the making of an
  appropriate entry in the records of the clearing agency.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest in fungible bulk

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Under subsections (4) to (10), entries may be in
  respect of like securities or interests therein as part of a fungible
  bulk and may refer merely to a quantity of a particular security
  without reference to the name of the registered owner, certificate or
  bond number or the like and, in appropriate cases, may be on a net
  basis taking into account other transfers or pledges of the same
  security.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Constructive endorsement and delivery

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A transfer or pledge under subsections (4) to (10)
  has the effect of a delivery of a security in bearer form or duly
  endorsed in blank representing the amount of the obligation or the
  number of shares or rights transferred or pledged.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If a pledge or the creation of a security interest is
  intended, the making of entries has the effect of a taking of delivery
  by the pledgee or a secured party and the pledgee or secured party
  shall be deemed to have taken possession for all purposes.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Holder

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} A person depositing a security certificate or an
  uncertificated security with a clearing agency, or a transferee or
  pledgee of a security under subsections (4) to (10), is a holder of
  the security and shall be deemed to have possession of the security so
  deposited, transferred or pledged, as the case may be, for all
  purposes.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Not registration

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} A transfer or pledge under subsections (4) to (10)
  does not constitute a registration of transfer under sections 129 to
  136.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Error in records

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} That entries made in the records of the clearing
  agency as provided in subsection (4) are not appropriate does not
  affect the validity or effect of the entries nor the liabilities or
  obligations of the clearing agency to any person adversely affected
  thereby.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to reclaim possession

- **[[125]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-125 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person against whom the transfer of a security is wrongful for any
  reason, including the person's incapacity, may, against anyone except
  a [bona fide]{.otherLang lang="la"} purchaser,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} reclaim possession of the security or obtain
    possession of any new security evidencing all or part of the same
    rights; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} claim damages.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recovery where unauthorized endorsement

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the transfer of a security is wrongful by reason
  of an unauthorized endorsement, the owner may reclaim possession of
  the security or a new security even from a [bona fide]{.otherLang
  lang="la"} purchaser if the ineffectiveness of the purported
  endorsement is asserted against the purchaser under section 120.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Remedies

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The right to reclaim possession of a security may be
  specially enforced, its transfer may be restrained and the security
  may be impounded pending litigation.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to requisites for registration

- **[[126]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-126
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless otherwise agreed, a
  transferor shall, on demand, supply a purchaser with proof of the
  transferor's authority to transfer a security or with any other
  requisite that is necessary to obtain registration of the transfer of
  a security, but if the transfer is not for value, it is not necessary
  for a transferor to prove authority to transfer unless the purchaser
  pays the reasonable and necessary costs of the proof and transfer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rescission of transfer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a transferor fails to comply with a demand under
  subsection (1) within a reasonable time, the purchaser may reject or
  rescind the transfer.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Seizure of security

**[[127]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-127 .sectionLabel}** No seizure of a
security or other interest evidenced thereby is effective until the
person making the seizure obtains possession of the security.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No conversion if good faith delivery

**[[128]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-128 .sectionLabel}** An agent or bailee who
in good faith, including observance of reasonable commercial standards
if the agent or bailee is in the business of buying, selling or
otherwise dealing with securities of a company, has received securities
and sold, pledged or delivered them according to the instructions of the
agent's or bailee's principal is not liable for conversion or for
participation in breach of fiduciary duty even though the principal has
no right to dispose of the securities.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty to register transfer

- **[[129]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-129
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to Part VII, where a
  security in registered form is presented for transfer, the issuer
  shall register the transfer if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the security is endorsed by an appropriate person;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} reasonable assurance is given that the endorsement
    is genuine and effective;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the issuer has no duty to inquire into adverse
    claims or has discharged any such duty;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} all applicable laws relating to the collection of
    taxes have been complied with;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the transfer is rightful or is to a [bona
    fide]{.otherLang lang="la"} purchaser; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the fee, if any, referred to in subsection 88(2)
    has been paid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability for delay

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where an issuer has a duty to register a transfer of
  a security, the issuer is liable to the person presenting it for
  registration for any loss resulting from any unreasonable delay in
  registration or from the failure or refusal to register the transfer.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assurance of endorsements

- **[[130]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-130 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  issuer may require an assurance that each necessary endorsement on a
  security is genuine and effective by requiring a guarantee of the
  signature of the person endorsing the security and by requiring

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the endorsement is by an agent, reasonable
    assurance of authority to sign;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the endorsement is by a fiduciary, evidence of
    appointment or incumbency;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if there is more than one fiduciary, reasonable
    assurance that all who are required to sign have done so; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, assurance that corresponds as
    closely as practicable to the foregoing.

- Definition of [[guarantee of the signature]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), [[guarantee of
  the signature]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a guarantee signed by or on
  behalf of a person whom the issuer believes, on reasonable grounds, to
  be a responsible person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Standards

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An issuer may adopt reasonable standards to determine
  responsible persons for the purposes of subsection (2).

- Definition of [[evidence of appointment or
  incumbency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (1)(b), [[evidence of
  appointment or incumbency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a fiduciary appointed by a court and
    referred to in subsection 99(1), a copy of the certified court order
    referred to in subsection 99(1) and dated not earlier than sixty
    days before the day a security is presented for transfer; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of any other fiduciary, a copy of a
    document showing the appointment or other evidence believed by the
    issuer to be appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Standards

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} An issuer may adopt reasonable standards with respect
  to evidence referred to in paragraph (4)(b).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No notice to issuer

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} An issuer is deemed not to have notice of the
  contents of any document referred to in subsection (4) that is
  obtained by the issuer except to the extent that the contents relate
  directly to appointment or incumbency.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice from additional documentation

**[[131]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-131 .sectionLabel}** If an issuer, in
relation to a transfer, demands assurance other than an assurance
specified in subsection 130(1) and obtains a copy of a will, trust or
partnership agreement or a by-law or similar document, the issuer is
deemed to have notice of all matters contained therein affecting the
transfer.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limited duty of inquiry

- **[[132]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  issuer to whom a security is presented for registration has a duty to
  inquire into adverse claims if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the issuer receives written notice of an adverse
    claim at a time and in a manner that provides the issuer with a
    reasonable opportunity to act on it before the issue of a new,
    reissued or re-registered security and the notice discloses the name
    and address of the claimant, the registered owner and the issue of
    which the security is a part; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the issuer is deemed to have notice of an adverse
    claim from a document that it obtained under section 131.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Discharge of duty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An issuer may discharge a duty of inquiry by any
  reasonable means, including notifying an adverse claimant by
  registered mail sent to the address provided by the adverse claimant
  or, if no such address has been provided, to the adverse claimant's
  residence or regular place of business, that a security has been
  presented for registration of transfer by a named person and that the
  transfer will be registered unless, within thirty days after the date
  of mailing of the notice, either

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the issuer is served with a restraining order or
    other order of a court, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the issuer is provided with an indemnity bond
    sufficient in the issuer's judgment to protect the issuer and any
    registrar, transfer agent or other agent of the issuer from any loss
    that may be incurred by any of them as a result of complying with
    the adverse claim.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inquiry into adverse claims

**[[133]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-133 .sectionLabel}** Unless an issuer is
deemed to have notice of an adverse claim from a document that it
obtained under section 131 or has received notice of an adverse claim
under subsection 132(1), if a security presented for registration is
endorsed by the appropriate person, the issuer has no duty to inquire
into adverse claims and, in particular,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an issuer registering a security in the name of a
  person who is a fiduciary or who is described as a fiduciary is not
  bound to inquire into the existence, extent or correct description of
  the fiduciary relationship and thereafter the issuer may assume
  without inquiry that the newly registered owner continues to be the
  fiduciary until the issuer receives written notice that the fiduciary
  is no longer acting as such with respect to the particular security;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} an issuer registering a transfer on an endorsement by
  a fiduciary has no duty to inquire into whether the transfer is made
  in compliance with the document or with the law of the jurisdiction
  governing the fiduciary relationship; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} an issuer is deemed not to have notice of the
  contents of any court record or any registered document even if the
  record or document is in the issuer's possession and even if the
  transfer is made on the endorsement of a fiduciary to the fiduciary
  specifically or to the fiduciary's nominee.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duration of notice of adverse claim

**[[134]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-134 .sectionLabel}** A written notice of
adverse claim received by an issuer is effective for twelve months after
the day it was received unless the notice is renewed in writing.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation on issuer's liability

- **[[135]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-135
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except as otherwise provided in any
  applicable law relating to the collection of taxes, an issuer is not
  liable to the owner or any other person who incurs a loss as a result
  of the registration of a transfer of a security if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the necessary endorsements were on or with the
    security; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the issuer had no duty to inquire into adverse
    claims or had discharged any such duty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty of issuer on default

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an issuer has registered a transfer of a security
  to a person not entitled to it, the issuer shall on demand deliver a
  like security to the owner unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the issuer is not liable by virtue of subsection
    (1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the owner is precluded by subsection 136(1) from
    asserting any claim; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the delivery would result in over-issue in respect
    of which section 100 applies.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Lost or stolen security

- **[[136]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-136
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where a security has been lost,
  apparently destroyed or wrongfully taken, and the owner fails to
  notify the issuer of that fact by giving the issuer written notice of
  the owner's adverse claim within a reasonable time after the owner
  knows of the loss, destruction or taking, then, if the issuer has
  registered a transfer of the security before receiving the notice, the
  owner is precluded from asserting against the issuer any claim to a
  new security.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty to issue new security

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where the owner of a security claims that the
  security has been lost, destroyed or wrongfully taken, the issuer
  shall issue a new security in place of the original security if the
  owner

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} so requests before the issuer has notice that the
    security has been acquired by a [bona fide]{.otherLang lang="la"}
    purchaser;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} provides the issuer with a sufficient indemnity
    bond; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} satisfies any other reasonable requirements imposed
    by the issuer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty to register transfer

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If, after the issue of a new security under
  subsection (2), a [bona fide]{.otherLang lang="la"} purchaser of the
  original security presents the original security for registration of
  transfer, the issuer shall register the transfer unless registration
  would result in over-issue in respect of which section 100 applies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right of issuer to recover

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In addition to the rights that an issuer has by
  reason of an indemnity bond, the issuer may recover the new security
  issued under subsection (2) from the person to whom it was issued or
  any person taking under that person other than a [bona
  fide]{.otherLang lang="la"} purchaser.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authenticating agent's duty

**[[137]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-137 .sectionLabel}** An authenticating
trustee, registrar, transfer agent or other agent of an issuer has, in
respect of the issue, registration of transfer and cancellation of a
security of the issuer,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a duty to the issuer to exercise good faith and
  reasonable diligence; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the same obligations to the holder or owner of a
  security and the same rights, privileges and immunities as the issuer.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to agent

**[[138]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-138 .sectionLabel}** Notice to an
authenticating trustee, registrar, transfer agent or other agent of an
issuer is notice to the issuer in respect of the functions performed by
the agent.

## [PART VI]{.HLabel1}[Corporate Governance]{.HTitleText1} {#h-454065 .Part}

### [Shareholders]{.HTitleText2} {#h-454066 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Place of meetings

- **[[139]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-139
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Meetings of shareholders of a
  company shall be held at the place within Canada provided for in the
  by-laws of the company or, in the absence of any such provision, at
  the place within Canada that the directors determine.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Participation by electronic means

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Unless the by-laws provide otherwise, any person who
  is entitled to attend a meeting of shareholders may participate in the
  meeting by means of a telephonic, electronic or other communication
  facility that permits all participants to communicate adequately with
  each other during the meeting if the company makes one available. A
  person who is participating in a meeting by one of those means is
  deemed for the purposes of this Act to be present at the meeting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations
  respecting the manner of and conditions for participating in a meeting
  by means of a telephonic, electronic or other communication facility
  that permits all participants to communicate adequately with each
  other during the meeting.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 139
- 2005, c. 54, s. 381
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-139-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 139"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calling meetings

- **[[140]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-140 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  directors of a company

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} shall, after the meeting called pursuant to
    subsection 50(1), call the first annual meeting of shareholders of
    the company, which meeting must be held not later than six months
    after the end of the first financial year of the company, and
    subsequently call an annual meeting of shareholders, which meeting
    must be held not later than six months after the end of each
    financial year; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} may at any time call a special meeting of
    shareholders.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order to delay calling annual meeting

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), the company may apply to the
  court for an order extending the time for calling an annual meeting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation to notify Superintendent

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The company shall give notice of the application to
  the Superintendent before any hearing concerning the application and
  shall provide the Superintendent with a copy of any order that is
  issued.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Superintendent's right to appear

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent is entitled to appear and be heard
  in person or by counsel at any hearing concerning the application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authority to fix record date

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The directors may in advance fix a record date, that
  is within the prescribed period, for the determination of shareholders
  for any purpose, including for a determination of which shareholders
  are entitled to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} receive payment of a dividend;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} participate in a liquidation distribution;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} receive notice of a meeting of shareholders; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} vote at a meeting of shareholders.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination of record date

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If no record date is fixed,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the record date for the determination of
    shareholders who are entitled to receive notice of a meeting of
    shareholders is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} at the close of business on the day immediately
      preceding the day on which the notice is given, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if no notice is given, the day on which the
      meeting is held; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the record date for the determination of
    shareholders for any other purpose, other than to establish a
    shareholder's right to vote, is at the close of business on the day
    on which the directors pass a resolution in respect of that purpose.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of record date

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If a record date is fixed and unless notice of the
  record date is waived in writing by every holder of a share of the
  class or series affected whose name is set out in the securities
  register at the close of business on the day on which the directors
  fix the record date, notice of the record date shall be given within
  the prescribed period by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} advertisement in a newspaper in general circulation
    in the place where the company's head office is situated and in each
    place in Canada where the company has a transfer agent or where a
    transfer of its shares may be recorded; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} written notice to each stock exchange in Canada on
    which the company's shares are listed for trading.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 140
- 2005, c. 54, s. 382
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-140-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 140"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of meeting

- **[[141]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-141
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Notice of the time and place of a
  meeting of shareholders of a company shall be sent within the
  prescribed period to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} each shareholder entitled to vote at the meeting;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} each director;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the auditor of the company; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the Superintendent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(1.01)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a company that is not a
  distributing company, notice may be sent within any shorter period
  specified in its by-laws.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication in newspaper

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In addition to the notice required under subsection
  (1), where any class of shares of a company is publicly traded on a
  recognized stock exchange in Canada, notice of the time and place of a
  meeting of shareholders shall be published once a week for at least
  four consecutive weeks before the date of the meeting in a newspaper
  in general circulation in the place where the head office of the
  company is situated and in each place in Canada where the company has
  a transfer agent or where a transfer of the company's shares may be
  recorded.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 141
- 2005, c. 54, s. 383
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-141-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 141"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice not required

- **[[142]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-142
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Notice of a meeting is not required
  to be sent to shareholders who are not registered on the records of
  the company or the company's transfer agent on the record date fixed
  under paragraph 140(5)(c) or determined under paragraph 140(6)(a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of default

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Failure to receive a notice of a meeting of
  shareholders does not deprive a shareholder of the right to vote at
  the meeting.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 142
- 2005, c. 54, s. 384
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-142-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 142"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of adjourned meeting

- **[[143]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-143 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  meeting of shareholders is adjourned for less than thirty days, it is
  not necessary, unless the by-laws otherwise provide, to give notice of
  the adjourned meeting, other than by announcement at the earliest
  meeting that is adjourned.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of continuation of meeting

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a meeting of shareholders is adjourned by one or
  more adjournments for a total of thirty days or more, notice of the
  continuation of the meeting shall be given as for an original meeting
  but, unless the meeting is adjourned by one or more adjournments for a
  total of more than ninety days, subsection 160.04(1) does not apply.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 143
- 1997, c. 15, s. 346
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special business

- **[[144]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} All
  matters dealt with at a special meeting of shareholders and all
  matters dealt with at an annual meeting of shareholders, except
  consideration of the financial statements, auditor's report, election
  of directors, remuneration of directors and reappointment of the
  incumbent auditor, are deemed to be special business.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of special business

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notice of a meeting of shareholders at which special
  business is to be transacted must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} state the nature of the special business in
    sufficient detail to permit a shareholder to form a reasoned
    judgment thereon; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} contain the text of any special resolution to be
    submitted to the meeting.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Waiver of notice

- **[[145]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-145 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  shareholder and any other person entitled to attend a meeting of
  shareholders may in any manner waive notice of a meeting of
  shareholders.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Attendance at a meeting of shareholders is a waiver
  of notice of the meeting, except when a person attends the meeting for
  the express purpose of objecting to the transaction of any business on
  the grounds that the meeting is not lawfully called.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 145
- 2001, c. 9, s. 495(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proposals

- **[[146]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-146
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (1.1) and
  (1.2), a registered holder or beneficial owner of shares that may be
  voted at an annual meeting of shareholders may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} submit to the company notice of any matter that
    they propose to raise at the meeting (in this section and section
    147 referred to as a "proposal"); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} discuss at the meeting any matter in respect of
    which they would have been entitled to submit a proposal.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Eligibility to submit proposal

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} To be eligible to submit a proposal a person shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for at least the prescribed period be the
    registered holder or beneficial owner of at least the prescribed
    number of the company's outstanding shares; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} have the support of persons who, in the aggregate
    and including or not including the person who submits the proposal,
    have for at least the prescribed period been the registered holders
    or beneficial owners of at least the prescribed number of the
    company's outstanding shares.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information to be provided

  [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} A proposal is to be accompanied by the following
  information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the person submitting the
    proposal and the names and addresses of their supporters, if any;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of shares held or owned by the person
    and their supporters, if any, and the date that the shares were
    acquired.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information not part of proposal

  [(1.3)]{.lawlabel} The information provided under subsection (1.2)
  does not form part of a proposal or of the supporting statement
  referred to in subsection (3) and is not to be included for the
  purpose of the prescribed maximum number of words referred to in
  subsection (3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof may be required

  [(1.4)]{.lawlabel} If the company requests within the prescribed
  period that a person provide proof that they are eligible to submit a
  proposal, the person shall within the prescribed period provide proof
  that they meet the requirements of subsection (1.1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Management proxy

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company that solicits proxies shall, in the
  management proxy circular required by subsection 160.05(1), set out
  any proposal of a shareholder submitted for consideration at a meeting
  of shareholders or attach the proposal to the management proxy
  circular.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Supporting statement

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} At the request of the person who submits a proposal,
  the company shall set out in the management proxy circular or attach
  to it the person's statement in support of the proposal and their name
  and address. The statement and proposal together are not to exceed the
  prescribed maximum number of words.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Nomination of directors

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A proposal may include nominations for the election
  of directors if it is signed by one or more registered holders or
  beneficial owners of shares representing in the aggregate not less
  than 5% of the shares of the company or 5% of the shares of a class of
  its shares entitled to vote at the meeting at which the proposal is to
  be presented.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A company is not required to comply with
  subsections (2) and (3) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the proposal is not submitted to the company at
    least the prescribed number of days before the anniversary date of
    the notice of meeting that was sent to shareholders in respect of
    the previous annual meeting of shareholders;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it clearly appears that the primary purpose of the
    proposal is to enforce a personal claim or redress a personal
    griev­ance against the company or its directors, officers or security
    holders;

  - [(b.1)]{.lawlabel} it clearly appears that the proposal does not
    relate in a significant way to the business or affairs of the
    company;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person submitting the proposal failed within
    the prescribed period before the company receives their proposal to
    present, in person or by proxy, at a meeting of shareholders a
    proposal that at their request had been set out in or attached to a
    management proxy circular;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} substantially the same proposal was set out in or
    attached to a management proxy circular or dissident's proxy
    circular relating to, and presented to shareholders at, a meeting of
    shareholders held within the prescribed period before the receipt of
    the proposal and did not receive the prescribed minimum amount of
    support at the meeting; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the rights conferred by subsections (1) to (4) are
    being abused to secure publicity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Company may refuse to include proposal

  [(5.1)]{.lawlabel} If a person who submits a proposal fails to
  continue to hold or own shares in accordance with paragraph (1.1)(a)
  or, as the case may be, does not continue to have the support of
  persons who are in the aggregate the registered holders or beneficial
  owners of the prescribed number of shares in accordance with paragraph
  (1.1)(b) until the end of the meeting, the company is not required to
  set out any proposal submitted by that person in or attach it to a
  management proxy circular for any meeting held within the prescribed
  period after the day of the meeting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Immunity for proposal and statement

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} No company or person acting on behalf of a company
  incurs any liability by reason only of circulating a proposal or
  statement in compliance with subsections (2) and (3).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 146
- 1997, c. 15, s. 347
- 2005, c. 54, s. 385
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-146-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 146"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of refusal

- **[[147]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-147 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  company refuses to include a proposal in a management proxy circular,
  it shall in writing notify the person submitting the proposal of its
  intention to omit the proposal from the management proxy circular and
  of the reasons for the refusal. It shall notify the person within the
  prescribed period after either the day on which it receives the
  proposal or, if it has requested proof under subsection 146(1.4), the
  day on which it receives the proof.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to court

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On the application of a person submitting a proposal
  who claims to be aggrieved by a company's refusal under subsection
  (1), a court may restrain the holding of the meeting at which the
  proposal is sought to be presented and make any further order that it
  thinks fit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A company or any person claiming to be aggrieved by a
  proposal may apply to a court for an order permitting the company to
  omit the proposal from the management proxy circular, and the court,
  if it is satisfied that subsection 146(5) applies, may make such order
  as it thinks fit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to Superintendent

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An applicant under subsection (2) or (3) shall give
  the Superintendent written notice of the application and the
  Superintendent may appear and be heard at the hearing of the
  application in person or by counsel.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 147
- 2005, c. 54, s. 386
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-147-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 147"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}List of shareholders entitled to notice

- **[[148]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-148 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company shall prepare an alphabetical list of shareholders entitled to
  receive notice of a meeting showing the number of shares held by each
  shareholder

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if a record date is fixed under paragraph
    140(5)(c), no later than 10 days after that date; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if no record date is fixed, on the record date
    determined under paragraph 140(6)(a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Voting list

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The company shall prepare an alphabet­ical list of
  shareholders entitled to vote as of the record date showing the number
  of shares held by each shareholder

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if a record date is fixed under paragraph
    140(5)(d), no later than 10 days after that date; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if no record date is fixed under paragraph
    140(5)(d), no later than 10 days after a record date is fixed under
    paragraph 140(5)(c) or no later than the record date determined
    under paragraph 140(6)(a), as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Entitlement to vote

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A shareholder whose name appears on a list prepared
  under subsection (2) is entitled to vote the shares shown opposite
  their name.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Examination of list

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A shareholder may examine the list of shareholders

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} during usual business hours at the head office of
    the company or at the place where its central securities register is
    maintained; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at the meeting of shareholders for which the list
    was prepared.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 148
- 2001, c. 9, s. 496
- 2005, c. 54, s. 387
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-148-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 148"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quorum

- **[[149]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-149
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless the by-laws otherwise
  provide, a quorum of shareholders is present at a meeting of
  shareholders if the holders of a majority of the shares who are
  entitled to vote at the meeting are present in person or represented
  by proxyholders.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a quorum is present at the opening of a meeting of
  shareholders, the shareholders present may, unless the by-laws
  otherwise provide, proceed with the business of the meeting,
  notwithstanding that a quorum is not present throughout the meeting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a quorum is not present at the opening of a
  meeting of shareholders, the shareholders present may adjourn the
  meeting to a fixed time and place but may not transact any other
  business.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}One shareholder meeting

**[[150]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-150 .sectionLabel}** If a company has only
one shareholder, or only one holder of any class or series of shares,
the shareholder present in person or represented by a proxyholder
constitutes a meeting of shareholders or a meeting of shareholders of
that class or series.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}One share --- one vote

- **[[151]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-151
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where a share of a company entitles
  the holder thereof to vote at a meeting of shareholders, that share
  entitles the shareholder to one vote at the meeting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (1), a share of a
  former-Act company issued

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on or before September 27, 1990 that entitled the
    holder to more than one vote, or to a fraction of a vote, at a
    meeting of shareholders in respect of that share, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} after September 27, 1990 pursuant to the conversion
    of a security of the former-Act company that was issued with such
    conversion privilege prior to that date

  continues to entitle that holder or any subsequent holder of the share
  to exercise such voting rights.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representative shareholder

- **[[152]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  an entity is a shareholder of a company, the company shall recognize
  any natural person authorized by a resolution of the directors or
  governing body or similar authority of the entity to represent it at
  meetings of shareholders of the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A natural person authorized under subsection (1) to
  represent an entity may exercise on behalf of the entity all the
  powers the entity could exercise if it were a natural person as well
  as a shareholder.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Joint shareholders

**[[153]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153 .sectionLabel}** Unless the by-laws
otherwise provide, if two or more persons hold shares jointly, one of
those holders present at a meeting of shareholders may in the absence of
the others vote the shares, but if two or more of those persons who are
present in person or represented by proxyholder vote, they shall vote as
one on the shares jointly held by them.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Voting by hands or ballot

- **[[154]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-154
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless the by-laws otherwise
  provide, voting at a meeting of shareholders shall take place by show
  of hands except when a ballot is demanded by either a shareholder or
  proxyholder entitled to vote at the meeting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ballot

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A shareholder or proxyholder may demand a ballot
  either before or after any vote by show of hands.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Electronic voting

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1) and unless the by-laws provide
  otherwise, any vote referred to in that subsection may be held
  entirely by means of a telephonic, electronic or other communication
  facility if the company makes one available.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Voting while participating
  electronically

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Unless the by-laws provide otherwise, any person who
  is participating in a meeting of shareholders under subsection 139(2)
  and entitled to vote at that meeting may vote by means of the
  telephonic, electronic or other communication facility that the
  company has made available for that purpose.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations
  respecting the manner of and conditions for voting at a meeting of
  shareholders by means of a telephonic, electronic or other
  communication facility.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 154
- 2005, c. 54, s. 388
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-154-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 154"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Resolution in lieu of meeting

- **[[155]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except where a written statement is
  submitted by a director under section 178 or by an auditor under
  subsection 326(1),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a resolution in writing signed by all the
    shareholders entitled to vote on that resolution at a meeting of
    shareholders is as valid as if it had been passed at a meeting of
    the shareholders; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a resolution in writing dealing with all matters
    required by this Act to be dealt with at a meeting of shareholders,
    and signed by all the shareholders entitled to vote at that meeting,
    satisfies all the requirements of this Act relating to meetings of
    shareholders.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Filing resolution

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A copy of every resolution referred to in
  subsection (1) shall be kept with the minutes of the meetings of
  shareholders.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Evidence

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Unless a ballot is demanded, an entry in the minutes
  of a meeting that the chairperson declared a resolution to be carried
  or defeated is in the absence of evidence to the contrary proof of
  that fact without proof of the number or proportion of votes recorded
  in favour of or against the resolution.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 155
- 2005, c. 54, s. 389
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-155-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 155"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requisitioned meeting

- **[[156]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-156
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Shareholders who together hold not
  less than 5 per cent of the issued and outstanding shares of a company
  that carry the right to vote at a meeting sought to be held may
  requisition the directors to call a meeting of shareholders for the
  purposes stated in the requisition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A requisition referred to in subsection (1)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} must state the business to be transacted at the
    meeting and must be sent to each director and to the head office of
    the company; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} may consist of several documents of like form, each
    signed by one or more shareholders.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Directors calling meeting

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of a requisition referred to in subsection
  (1), the directors shall call a meeting of shareholders to transact
  the business stated in the requisition, unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a record date has been fixed under paragraph
    140(5)(c) and notice of it has been given under subsection 140(7);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the directors have called a meeting of shareholders
    and have given notice thereof under section 141; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the business of the meeting as stated in the
    requisition includes matters described in paragraphs 146(5)(b) to
    (e).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Shareholders' power

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the directors do not call a meeting within
  twenty-one days after receiving the requisition referred to in
  subsection (1), any shareholder who signed the requisition may call
  the meeting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Procedure

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A meeting called under this section shall be called
  as nearly as possible in the manner in which meetings are to be called
  pursuant to the by-laws and this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reimbursement

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Unless the shareholders otherwise resolve at a
  meeting called under subsection (4), the company shall reimburse the
  shareholders for any expenses reasonably incurred by them in
  requisitioning, calling and holding the meeting.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 156
- 2005, c. 54, s. 390
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-156-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 156"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Court may order meeting to be called

- **[[157]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-157 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  court may, on the application of a director, a shareholder who is
  entitled to vote at a meeting of shareholders or the Superintendent,
  order a meeting to be called, held or conducted in the manner that the
  court directs if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is impracticable to call the meeting within the
    time or in the manner in which it is to be called;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is impracticable to conduct the meeting in the
    manner required by this Act or the by-laws; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the court thinks that the meeting ought to be
    called, held or conducted within the time or in the manner that it
    directs for any other reason.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Varying quorum

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Without restricting the generality of subsection (1),
  a court may order that the quorum required by the by-laws or this Act
  be varied or dispensed with at a meeting called, held and conducted
  pursuant to this section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Valid meeting

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A meeting called, held and conducted pursuant to this
  section is for all purposes a meeting of shareholders of the company
  duly called, held and conducted.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 157
- 2005, c. 54, s. 391
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-157-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 157"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Court review of election

- **[[158]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company or a shareholder or director of a company may apply to a court
  to resolve any dispute in respect of the election or appointment of a
  director or an auditor of the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of court

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On an application under subsection (1), a court may
  make any order it thinks fit including, without limiting the
  generality of the foregoing,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an order restraining a director or auditor whose
    election or appointment is challenged from acting pending
    determination of the dispute;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an order declaring the result of the disputed
    election or appointment;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an order requiring a new election or appointment,
    and including in the order directions for the management of the
    business and affairs of the company until a new election is held or
    the new appointment is made; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an order determining the voting rights of
    shareholders and of persons claiming to own shares.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to Superintendent

- **[[159]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-159 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who makes an application under subsection 157(1) or 158(1)
  shall give notice of the application to the Superintendent before the
  hearing and shall deliver a copy of the order of the court, if any, to
  the Superintendent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Superintendent representation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent may appear and be heard in person
  or by counsel at the hearing of an application referred to in
  subsection (1).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pooling agreement

**[[160]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-160 .sectionLabel}** A written agreement
between two or more shareholders may provide that in exercising voting
rights the shares held by them will be voted as provided in the
agreement.

### [Proxies]{.HTitleText2} {#h-454307 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[160.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-160.01 .sectionLabel}** The definitions
in this section apply in this section and in sections 160.02 to 160.08.

[[intermediary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[intermediary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who holds a
    security on behalf of another person who is not the registered
    holder of the security, and includes

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a securities broker or dealer required to be
      registered to trade or deal in securities under the laws of any
      jurisdiction;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a securities depositary;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a financial institution;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a clearing agency, a securities
      dealer, trust company, association within the meaning of section 2
      of the [Cooperative Credit Associations Act](/eng/acts/C-41.01),
      bank or other person, including another clearing agency, on whose
      behalf the clearing agency or its nominee holds securities of an
      issuer;

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a trustee or administrator of a self-administered
      retirement savings plan, retirement income fund or education
      savings plan or another similar self-administered savings or
      investment plan that is registered under the [Income Tax
      Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3);

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a nominee of a person referred to in any of
      paragraphs (a) to (e); and

    - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a person who performs functions similar to those
      performed by a person referred to in any of paragraphs (a) to (e)
      and holds a security registered in their name, or in the name of
      their nominee, on behalf of another person who is not the
      registered holder of the
      security. ([intermédiaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[registrant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[registrant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2005, c. 54, s.
    392\]]{.Repealed}

[[solicitation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[solicitation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} includes

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a request for a proxy whether or not
        accompanied by or included in a form of proxy,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a request to execute or not to execute or, in
        Quebec, to sign or not to sign a form of proxy or to revoke a
        proxy,

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the sending of a form of proxy or other
        communication to a shareholder under circumstances reasonably
        calculated to result in the procurement, withholding or
        revocation of a proxy, and

      - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the sending of a form of proxy to a
        shareholder under subsection 160.04(1); but

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} does not include

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the sending of a form of proxy in response to
        an unsolicited request made by or on behalf of a shareholder,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the performance of administrative acts or
        professional services on behalf of a person soliciting a proxy,

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the sending by an intermediary of the
        documents referred to in subsection 160.07(1),

      - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a solicitation by a person in respect of
        shares of which they are the beneficial owner,

      - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed public announcement by a
        shareholder of how they intend to vote and the reasons for that
        decision,

      - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} a communication for the purpose of obtaining
        the support of persons in accord­ance with paragraph 146(1.1)(b),
        or

      - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} a communication, other than a solicitation by
        or on behalf of the management of a company, that is made to
        shareholders in the prescribed
        circumstances. ([sollicitation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[solicitation by or on behalf of the management of a company]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[solicitation by or on behalf of the management of a
    company]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a solicitation by any person
    pursuant to a resolution or instruction of, or with the acquiescence
    of, the directors or a committee of the directors of the
    company. ([sollicitation effectuée par la direction d'une société ou
    pour son compte]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 15, s. 348
- 2005, c. 54, s. 392
- [2022, c. 10, s. 231(E)](#1357196-1485875){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1357196-1485875.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-160.01-20061128.html "Link to previous version of section 160.01"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appointing proxyholder

- **[[160.02]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-160.02
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A shareholder who is entitled to
  vote at a meeting of shareholders may, by executing a form of proxy,
  appoint a proxyholder or one or more alternate proxyholders, who are
  not required to be shareholders, to attend and act at the meeting in
  the manner and to the extent authorized by the proxy and with the
  authority conferred by the proxy.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Execution of proxy

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A form of proxy shall be executed by a shareholder or
  by a shareholder's attorney authorized in writing to do so.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limit on authority

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No appointment of a proxyholder provides authority
  for the proxyholder to act in respect of the appointment of an auditor
  or the election of a director unless a nominee proposed in good faith
  for the appointment or election is named in the form of proxy, a
  management proxy circular, a dissident's proxy circular or a proposal
  under subsection 146(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Required information

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A form of proxy must indicate, in bold-face type,
  that the shareholder by whom or on whose behalf it is executed may
  appoint a proxyholder, other than a person designated in the form of
  proxy, to attend and act on the shareholder's behalf at a meeting to
  which the proxy relates, and must contain instructions as to the
  manner in which the shareholder may do so.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Validity of proxy

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A proxy is valid only at the meeting in respect of
  which it is given or at a continuation of the meeting after an
  adjournment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation of proxy

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A shareholder may revoke a proxy

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} by depositing an instrument in writing executed by
    the shareholder or by the shareholder's attorney authorized in
    writing to do so

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} at the head office of the company at any time up
      to and including the last business day before the day of a
      meeting, or a continuation of the meeting after an adjournment, at
      which the proxy is to be used, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} with the chairperson of the meeting on the day
      of the meeting or a continuation of the meeting after an
      adjournment; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other manner permitted by law.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 15, s. 348
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deposit of proxies

**[[160.03]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-160.03 .sectionLabel}** The directors may
specify, in a notice calling a meeting of shareholders or a continuation
of a meeting of shareholders after an adjournment, a time before which
executed forms of proxy to be used at the meeting or the continued
meeting must be deposited with the company or its transfer agent. The
time specified must not be more than forty-eight hours, excluding
Saturdays and holidays, before the meeting or the continued meeting.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 15, s. 348
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mandatory solicitation

- **[[160.04]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-160.04
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and
  143(2), the management of a company shall, concurrently with sending
  notice of a meeting of shareholders, send a form of proxy that is in
  accordance with the regulations to each shareholder entitled to
  receive notice of the meeting.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The management of a company is not required to send a
  form of proxy under subsection (1) if the company

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is not a distributing company; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has 50 or fewer shareholders who are entitled to
    vote at a meeting, two or more joint holders of a share being
    counted as one shareholder.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 15, s. 348
- 2005, c. 54, s. 393
- [2022, c. 10, s. 220](#1357159-1367738){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1357159-1367738.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-160.04-20061128.html "Link to previous version of section 160.04"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Soliciting proxies

- **[[160.05]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-160.05
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A person shall not solicit proxies
  unless a proxy circular that is in accordance with the regulations is
  sent to the auditor of the company, to each shareholder whose proxy is
  solicited and, in the case set out in paragraph (b), to the company as
  follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of solicitation by or on behalf of the
    management of a company, a management proxy circular, either as an
    appendix to or as a separate document accompanying the notice of the
    meeting; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of any other solicitation, a
    dissident's proxy circular stating the purposes of the solicitation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- solicitation to 15 or
  fewer shareholders

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), a person may solicit
