  voting rights attached to all of the outstanding voting shares of the
  company and that are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} shares of one or more classes of shares that are
    listed and posted for trading on a recognized stock exchange in
    Canada; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} shares none of which is beneficially owned by a
    person who is a major shareholder of the company in respect of the
    voting shares of the company or by any entity that is controlled by
    a person who is a major shareholder of the company in respect of
    such shares.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 384
- 2001, c. 9, s. 524
- 2007, c. 6, s. 357
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-384-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 384"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of section 379

**[[385]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-385 .sectionLabel}** At the expiration of
the period for compliance with an undertaking referred to in subsection
384(2), section 379 shall apply in respect of the company to which the
undertaking relates.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 385
- 2001, c. 9, s. 524
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on voting rights

- **[[386]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-386 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If,
  with respect to any company, a particular person contravenes section
  375 or 375.1 or fails to comply with an undertaking referred to in
  subsection 384(2) or with any term or condition imposed under section
  389, no person, and no entity controlled by the particular person,
  shall, in person or by proxy, exercise any voting rights

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that are attached to shares of the company
    beneficially owned by the particular person or any entity controlled
    by the particular person; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that are subject to an agreement entered into by
    the particular person, or any entity controlled by the particular
    person, pertaining to the exercise of the voting rights.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsection (1) ceases to apply

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) ceases to apply in respect of a person
  when, as the case may be,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the shares to which the contravention relates have
    been disposed of;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person ceases to control the company within the
    meaning of paragraph 3(1)(d);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the person failed to comply with an undertaking
    referred to in subsection 384(2), the company complies with section
    379; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the person failed to comply with a term or
    condition imposed under section 389, the person complies with the
    term or condition.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 386
- 1997, c. 15, s. 374
- 2001, c. 9, s. 524
:::

#### [Approval Process]{.HTitleText3} {#h-456577 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for approval

- **[[387]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-387 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application for an approval of the Minister required under this Part
  must be filed with the Superintendent and contain the information,
  material and evidence that the Superintendent may require.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Applicant

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If, with respect to any particular transaction, this
  Part applies to more than one person, any one of those persons may
  make the application to the Minister for approval on behalf of all of
  those persons.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 387
- 2001, c. 9, s. 525
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Matters for consideration

- **[[388]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-388
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), if an
  application for an approval under section 375 is made, the Minister,
  in determining whether or not to approve the transaction, shall take
  into account all matters that the Minister considers relevant to the
  application, including

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the nature and sufficiency of the financial
    resources of the applicant or applicants as a source of continuing
    financial support for the company;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the soundness and feasibility of the plans of the
    applicant or applicants for the future conduct and development of
    the business of the company;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the business record and experience of the applicant
    or applicants;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the character and integrity of the applicant or
    applicants or, if the applicant or any of the applicants is a body
    corporate, its reputation for being operated in a manner that is
    consistent with the standards of good character and integrity;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} whether the company will be operated responsibly by
    persons with the competence and experience suitable for involvement
    in the operation of a financial institution;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the impact of any integration of the businesses and
    operations of the applicant or applicants with those of the company
    on the conduct of those businesses and operations; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the best interests of the financial system in
    Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}National treatment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a transaction in respect of which subsection
  375(1) or (2) applies would cause a company to become a subsidiary of
  a foreign institution that is engaged in the trust and loan business,
  that does not have any other company as its subsidiary and that is a
  non-WTO Member foreign institution, the Minister shall not approve the
  transaction unless the Minister is satisfied that treatment as
  favourable for companies to which this Act applies exists or will be
  provided in the jurisdiction in which the foreign institution
  principally carries on business, either directly or through a
  subsidiary.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Part XII of the [Bank
  Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Nothing in subsection (1) or (2) affects the
  operation of Part XII of the [Bank Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 388
- 1999, c. 28, s. 140
- 2001, c. 9, s. 526
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Terms and conditions

**[[389]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-389 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may impose
any terms and conditions in respect of an approval given under this Part
that the Minister considers necessary to ensure compliance with any
provision of this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 389
- 2001, c. 9, s. 527
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certifying receipt of application

- **[[390]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-390 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If,
  in the opinion of the Superintendent, an application filed under this
  Part contains all the required information, the Superintendent shall
  without delay refer the application to the Minister and send a receipt
  to the applicant certifying the date on which the completed
  application was received by the Superintendent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incomplete application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If, in the opinion of the Superintendent, an
  application filed under this Part is incomplete, the Superintendent
  shall send a notice to the applicant specifying the information
  required by the Superintendent to complete the application.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 390
- 2001, c. 9, s. 528
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of decision to applicant

- **[[391]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-391
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and (3)
  and 392(1), the Minister shall, within a period of thirty days after
  the certified date referred to in subsection 390(1), send to the
  applicant

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a notice approving the transaction to which the
    application relates; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} where the Minister is not satisfied that the
    transaction to which the application relates should be approved, a
    notice to that effect, advising the applicant of the right to make
    representations to the Minister in respect of the matter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of decision

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (4) and 392(2), the notice
  referred to in paragraph (1)(a) or (b) shall be sent by the Minister
  within a period of 45 days after the certified date referred to in
  subsection 390(1) in the following cases:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the application involves the acquisition of control
    of a company;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the application is made by an eligible agent or an
    entity controlled by an eligible agent; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an application is made for the approval referred to
    in subsection 396(3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of period for notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where the Minister is unable to complete the
  consideration of an application within the period referred to in
  subsection (1), the Minister shall,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} within that period, send a notice to that effect to
    the applicant; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} within a further period of thirty days after the
    date of the sending of the notice referred to in paragraph (a) or
    within such other further period as may be agreed on by the
    applicant and the Minister, send a notice referred to in paragraph
    (1)(a) or (b) to the applicant.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where the Minister considers it appropriate to do so,
  the Minister may extend the period referred to in subsection (2) for
  one or more periods of forty-five days.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 391
- 2012, c. 31, s. 103
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-391-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 391"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reasonable opportunity to make
representations

- **[[392]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-392
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where, after receipt of the notice
  referred to in paragraph 391(1)(b), the applicant advises the Minister
  that the applicant wishes to make representations, the Minister shall
  provide the applicant with a reasonable opportunity within a period of
  thirty days after the date of the notice, or within such further
  period as may be agreed on by the applicant and the Minister, to make
  representations in respect of the matter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reasonable opportunity to make
  representations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If, after receipt of the notice sent in accordance
  with subsection 391(2) that the Minister is not satisfied that the
  transaction to which the application relates should be approved, the
  applicant advises the Minister that the applicant wishes to make
  representations, the Minister shall provide the applicant with a
  reasonable opportunity within a period of 45 days after the date of
  the notice, or within any further period that may be agreed on by the
  applicant and the Minister, to make representations in respect of the
  matter.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 392
- 2012, c. 31, s. 104(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-392-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 392"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of decision

- **[[393]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-393
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Within a period of thirty days after
  the expiration of the period for making representations referred to in
  subsection 392(1), the Minister shall, in the light of any such
  representations and having regard to the matters to be taken into
  account, send a notice to the applicant indicating whether or not the
  Minister approves the share transaction to which the application
  relates.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Within a period of forty-five days after the
  expiration of the period for making representations referred to in
  subsection 392(2), the Minister shall, in the light of any such
  representations and having regard to the matters to be taken into
  account, send a notice to the applicant indicating whether or not the
  Minister approves the share transaction to which the application
  relates.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed approval

**[[394]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-394 .sectionLabel}** Where the Minister does
not send a notice under subsection 391(1) or (3) or 393(1) within the
period provided for in those subsections, the Minister is deemed to have
approved the share transaction to which the application relates.

**[[394.1 and 395]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-394.1_and_395
.sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 1994, c. 47, s. 207\]]{.Repealed}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Constraining registration: Crown and
foreign governments

- **[[396]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-396 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  company shall record in its securities register a transfer or issue of
  any share of the company to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} Her Majesty in right of Canada or of a province or
    any agent or agency of Her Majesty in either of those rights; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the government of a foreign country or any
    political subdivision thereof, or any agent or agency thereof.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), a company may record in its
  securities register a transfer or issue of any share of the company to
  a foreign institution that is controlled by the government of a
  foreign country or any political subdivision of a foreign country, or
  by any agent or agency of a foreign government, if the share that is
  transferred or issued is beneficially owned by the foreign institution
  or by an entity controlled by the foreign institution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), a company may, with the
  Minister's approval, record in its securities register the issue of
  any share of the company to an eligible agent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application made jointly

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The application for the approval referred to in
  subsection (3) must be made jointly by the company and the eligible
  agent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Matters for consideration

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Minister, in determining whether to grant the
  approval referred to in subsection (3), shall take into account all
  matters that he or she considers relevant, including those set out in
  paragraphs 388(1)(a) to (g).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consequence of revocation of approval

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If an approval referred to in subsection (3) is
  revoked, the company shall delete any entry in its securities register
  in respect of the recording of the issuance of shares to the eligible
  agent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disposition of shareholdings

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If a company or an eligible agent fails to comply
  with any undertaking or term or condition in relation to an approval
  referred to in subsection (3), or if an eligible agent ceases to be an
  eligible agent, the Minister may, if the Minister considers it to be
  in the public interest to do so, by order, direct the eligible agent
  or former eligible agent and any person controlled by the eligible
  agent or former eligible agent to dispose of any number of shares of
  the company beneficially owned by the eligible agent or former
  eligible agent or the persons controlled by the eligible agent or
  former eligible agent that the Minister specifies in the order, within
  the time specified in the order and in the proportion, if any, as
  between the eligible agent or former eligible agent and the persons
  controlled by the eligible agent or former eligible agent that is
  specified in the order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} No direction shall be made under subsection (7)
  unless the Minister has provided each person to whom the direction
  relates and the company concerned with a reasonable opportunity to
  make representations in respect of the subject-matter of the
  direction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} Any person with respect to whom a direction has been
  made under subsection (7) may, within 30 days after the date of the
  direction, appeal the matter in accordance with section 530.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 396
- 2012, c. 5, s. 165, c. 19, s. 328, c. 31, s. 105
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-396-20120629.html "Link to previous version of section 396"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[397 and 398]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-397_and_398
.sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 1994, c. 47, s. 208\]]{.Repealed}

- **[[399]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-399
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 1994, c. 47, s.
  209\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of voting rights held by
  governments

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding section 151, where any voting shares
  of a company are beneficially owned by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} Her Majesty in right of Canada or of a province or
    any agency of Her Majesty in either of those rights, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the government of a foreign country or any
    political subdivision thereof, or any agency thereof,

  no person shall, in person or by proxy, exercise the voting rights
  attached to those shares.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) does not apply to a foreign
  institution that is controlled by the government of a foreign country
  or any political subdivision of a foreign country, or by any agent or
  agency of a foreign government, if the shares referred to in
  subsection (1) are beneficially owned by the foreign institution or by
  an entity controlled by the foreign institution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) does not apply in respect of a
  government or agency referred to in that subsection that, on September
  27, 1990, beneficially owned shares of a former-Act company where the
  exercise of the voting rights attached to those shares was not
  prohibited under subsection 41(2) of the Trust Companies Act or
  subsection 48(2) of the Loan Companies Act, as those subsections read
  immediately prior to June 1, 1992.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (3) ceases to apply where a government or
  agency referred to in that subsection acquires beneficial ownership of
  any additional voting shares of the former-Act company in such number
  that the percentage of the voting rights attached to all of the voting
  shares of the former-Act company beneficially owned by the government
  or agency is greater than the percentage of the voting rights attached
  to all of the voting shares of the former-Act company that were
  beneficially owned by the government or agency on September 27, 1990.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- eligible agent

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) does not apply in respect of the
  exercise of voting rights attached to any share referred to in
  subsection 396(3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consequence of suspension of approval

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If an approval referred to in subsection 396(3) is
  suspended, the eligible agent shall not exercise, in person or by
  proxy, any voting rights attached to any share of the company that is
  beneficially owned by the eligible agent.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 399
- 1994, c. 47, s. 209
- 2012, c. 5, s. 166, c. 19, s. 329, c. 31, s. 106
:::

**[[400 and 400.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-400_and_400.1
.sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 1994, c. 47, s. 210\]]{.Repealed}

### [DIVISION III]{.HLabel2}[Directions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-456689 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disposition of shareholdings

- **[[401]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-401 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If,
  with respect to any company, a person contravenes section 375 or 375.1
  or fails to comply with an undertaking referred to in subsection
  384(2) or with any terms and conditions imposed under section 389, the
  Minister may, if the Minister deems it in the public interest to do
  so, by order, direct that person and any person controlled by that
  person to dispose of any number of shares of the company beneficially
  owned by any of those persons that the Minister specifies in the
  order, within the time specified in the order and in the proportion,
  if any, as between the person and the persons controlled by that
  person that is specified in the order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No direction shall be made under subsection (1)
  unless the Minister has provided each person to whom the direction
  relates and the company concerned with a reasonable opportunity to
  make representations in respect of the subject-matter of the
  direction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Any person with respect to whom a direction has been
  made under subsection (1) may, within thirty days after the date of
  the direction, appeal the matter in accordance with section 530.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2001, c. 9, s. 529\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 401
- 1996, c. 6, s. 119
- 2001, c. 9, s. 529
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disposition --- threat to integrity or
security

- **[[401.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-401.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister is of the opinion
  that a person's holding or beneficial ownership of shares of a company
  poses a threat to the integrity or security of the company or the
  financial system in Canada or a threat to national security, the
  Minister may, by order, direct that person and any person controlled
  by that person to dispose of any number of shares of the company held
  or beneficially owned by any of the persons that the Minister
  specifies in the order, within the time specified in the order and in
  the proportion, if any, as between the person and the persons
  controlled by the person that is specified in the order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of rights

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A direction made under subsection (1) may also
  suspend any of the rights under Part VI attached to the shares
  referred to in that subsection until the shares are disposed of in
  accordance with the direction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No direction shall be made under subsection (1)
  unless the Minister has provided each person to whom the direction
  relates and the company concerned with a reasonable opportunity to
  make representations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Temporary direction

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If, in the Minister's opinion, the length of time
  required for representations to be made under subsection (3) might be
  prejudicial to the public interest, the Minister may make a temporary
  direction to suspend any of the rights under Part VI attached to any
  of the shares referred to in subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cessation of effect

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A temporary direction ceases to have effect on the
  earlier of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the expiry of 30 days after the day on which it
    takes effect or of a shorter period that is specified in the
    temporary direction, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if a direction is made under subsection (1) in
    respect of the shares, the day on which that direction takes effect.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Any person with respect to whom a direction has been
  made under subsection (1) may, within 30 days after the day on which
  the direction was made, appeal the matter in accordance with section
  530.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice --- Committee and Review Agency

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If a direction has been made under subsection (1)
  or (4) for reasons related to national security, the Minister shall,
  within 30 days after the day on which the direction was made, notify

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the [[Committee]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined
    in section 2 of the [National Security and Intelligence Committee of
    Parliamentarians Act](/eng/acts/N-16.6); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the [[Review Agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as
    defined in section 2 of the [National Security and Intelligence
    Review Agency Act](/eng/acts/N-16.62).

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 26, s. 520](#1410891-1417188){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410891-1417188.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to court

- **[[402]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-402 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  person fails to comply with a direction made under subsection 396(7),
  401(1) or 401.1(1), an application on behalf of the Minister may be
  made to a court for an order to enforce the direction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Court order

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A court may, on an application under subsection (1),
  make such order as the circumstances require to give effect to the
  terms of the direction and may, without limiting the generality of the
  foregoing, require the company concerned to sell the shares that are
  the subject-matter of the direction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An appeal from an order of a court under this section
  lies in the same manner as, and to the same court to which, an appeal
  may be taken from any other order of the court.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 402
- 2012, c. 31, s. 107
- [2023, c. 26, s. 521](#1410893-1417190){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410893-1417190.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-402-20121214.html "Link to previous version of section 402"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [General Provisions]{.HTitleText3} {#h-456709 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest of securities underwriter

**[[403]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-403 .sectionLabel}** This Part does not
apply to a securities underwriter in respect of shares of a body
corporate or ownership interests in an unincorporated entity that are
acquired by the underwriter in the course of a distribution to the
public of those shares or ownership interests and that are held by the
underwriter for a period of not more than six months.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Arrangements to effect compliance

- **[[404]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-404 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  directors of a company may make such arrangements as they deem
  necessary to carry out the intent of this Part and, in particular, but
  without limiting the generality of the foregoing, may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} require any person in whose name a share of the
    company is held to submit a declaration setting out

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the beneficial ownership of the share, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} such other information as the directors deem
      relevant for the purposes of this Part;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} require any person who wishes to have a transfer of
    a share registered in the name of, or to have a share issued to,
    that person to submit a declaration referred to in paragraph (a) as
    though the person were the holder of that share; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} determine the circumstances in which a declaration
    referred to in paragraph (a) is to be required, the form of the
    declaration and the times at which it is to be submitted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order of Superintendent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent may, by order, direct a company to
  obtain from any person in whose name a share of the company is held a
  declaration setting out the name of every entity controlled by that
  person and containing information concerning

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the ownership or beneficial ownership of the share;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} such other related matters as are specified by the
    Superintendent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance required

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} As soon as possible after receipt by a company of a
  direction under subsection (2),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the company shall comply with the direction; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} every person who is requested by the company to
    provide a declaration containing information referred to in
    subsection (1) or (2) shall comply with the request.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Outstanding declaration: effect

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where, pursuant to this section, a declaration is
  required to be submitted by a shareholder or other person in respect
  of the issue or transfer of any share, a company may refuse to issue
  the share or register the transfer unless the required declaration is
  submitted.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reliance on information

**[[405]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-405 .sectionLabel}** A company and any
person who is a director or an officer, employee or agent of the company
may rely on any information contained in a declaration required by the
directors pursuant to section 404 or on any information otherwise
acquired in respect of any matter that might be the subject of such a
declaration, and no action lies against the company or any such person
for anything done or omitted to be done in good faith in reliance on any
such information.

**[[406]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-406 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 1994, c.
47, s. 211\]]{.Repealed}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption regulations

**[[407]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-407 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may, by regulation, exempt from any of the provisions of this Part any
share transaction or any class of share transactions involving the
transfer of shares on the death of the beneficial owner thereof, or any
arrangement made in contemplation of the death of the beneficial owner,
to one or more members of the beneficial owner's family, or to one or
more trustees on their behalf.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Competition Act](/eng/acts/C-34)

**[[408]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-408 .sectionLabel}** Nothing in, or done
under the authority of, this Act affects the operation of the
[Competition Act](/eng/acts/C-34).

## [PART VIII]{.HLabel1}[Business and Powers]{.HTitleText1} {#h-456737 .Part}

### [General Business]{.HTitleText2} {#h-456738 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Main business

- **[[409]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-409
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Act, a company shall
  not engage in or carry on any business other than such business
  generally as appertains to the business of providing financial
  services.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, a company may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subject to section 412, act as a trustee;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} act as a financial agent, receiver, liquidator or
    sequestrator;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} provide investment counselling services and
    portfolio management services; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} issue payment, credit or charge cards and, in
    cooperation with others including other financial institutions,
    operate a payment, credit or charge card plan.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 409
- 2009, c. 2, s. 289(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-409-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 409"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional activities

- **[[410]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-410 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  addition, a company may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} act as an agent for vendors, purchasers,
    mortgagors, mortgagees, lessors or lessees of real property and
    provide consulting or appraisal services in respect of real
    property;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} hold, manage and otherwise deal with real property;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} outside Canada, or with the prior written approval
    of the Minister, in Canada, engage in any of the following
    activities, namely,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} collecting, manipulating and transmitting

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} information that is primarily financial or
        economic in nature,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} information that relates to the business of a
        permitted entity, as defined in subsection 449(1), or

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} any other information that the Minister may, by
        order, specify,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} providing advisory or other services in the
      design, development or implementation of information management
      systems,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} designing, developing or marketing computer
      software, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} designing, developing, manufacturing or selling,
      as an ancillary activity to any activity referred to in any of
      subparagraphs (i) to (iii) that the company is engaging in,
      computer equipment integral to the provision of information
      services related to the business of financial institutions or to
      the provision of financial services;

  - [(c.1)]{.lawlabel} with the prior written approval of the Minister,
    develop, design, hold, manage, manufacture, sell or otherwise deal
    with data transmission systems, information sites, communication
    devices or information platforms or portals that are used

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to provide information that is primarily
      financial or economic in nature,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to provide information that relates to the
      business of a permitted entity, as defined in subsection 449(1),
      or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} for a prescribed purpose or in prescribed
      circumstances;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in Canada, engage in such activities referred to in
    paragraph (c) that the company was engaged in prior to June 1, 1992;

  - [(d.1)]{.lawlabel} engage, under prescribed terms and conditions, if
    any are prescribed, in specialized business management or advisory
    services;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} promote merchandise and services to the holders of
    any payment, credit or charge card issued by the company;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} engage in the sale of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} tickets, including lottery tickets, on a
      non-profit public service basis in connection with special,
      temporary and infrequent non-commercial celebrations or projects
      that are of local, municipal, provincial or national interest,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} urban transit tickets, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} tickets in respect of a lottery sponsored by
      the federal government or a provincial or municipal government or
      an agency of any such government or governments;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} act as a custodian of property; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} provide identification, authentication or
    verification services.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Except as authorized by or under this Act, a company
  shall not deal in goods, wares or merchandise or engage in any trade
  or other business.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting what a company may or may not do with
    respect to the carrying on of the activities referred to in
    paragraphs (1)(c), (c.1) and (d.1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} imposing terms and conditions in respect of the
    provision of the services referred to in paragraphs (1)(a) and
    409(2)(c) and the carrying on of the activities referred to in
    paragraphs (1)(c), (c.1) and (d.1); and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting the circumstances in which companies may
    be exempted from the requirement to obtain the approval of the
    Minister before carrying on a particular activity referred to in
    paragraph (1)(c) or (c.1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 410
- 1993, c. 34, s. 126(F)
- 1997, c. 15, s. 375
- 2001, c. 9, s. 530
- 2018, c. 12, s. 310
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-410-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 410"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Networking

**[[411]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-411 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section 416,
a company may

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} act as agent for any person in respect of the
  provision of any service that is provided by a financial institution,
  a permitted entity as defined in subsection 449(1) or a prescribed
  entity and may enter into an arrangement with any person in respect of
  the provision of that service; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} refer any person to any such financial institution or
  entity.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 411
- 2001, c. 9, s. 531
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on fiduciary activities

**[[412]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-412 .sectionLabel}** No company, other than
a company that is a trust company pursuant to subsection 57(2), shall
act in Canada as

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an executor, administrator or official guardian or a
  guardian, tutor, curator, judicial adviser or committee of a mentally
  incompetent person; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a trustee for a trust.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on deposit taking

- **[[413]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-413 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company shall not accept deposits in Canada unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is a member institution, as defined in section 2
    of the [Canada Deposit Insurance Corporation Act](/eng/acts/C-3);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it has been authorized under subsection 26.03(1) of
    that Act to accept deposits without being a member institution, as
    defined in section 2 of that Act; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the order approving the commencement and carrying
    on of business in Canada by the company authorizes it to accept
    deposits solely in accordance with subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deposits that fall below \$150,000

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company referred to in paragraph (1)(b) or (c)
  shall ensure that, on each day that is at least 30 days after the
  company receives the authorization referred to in that paragraph,

  ::: {#456803 .Subsection}
  A/B ≤ 0.01

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the sum of all amounts each of which is the sum of all the
      deposits held by the company at the end of a day in the preceding
      30 days each of which deposits is less than \$150,000 and payable
      in Canada; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the sum of all amounts each of which is the sum of all deposits
      held by the company at the end of a day in those preceding 30 days
      and payable in Canada.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exchange rate

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsection (2), the rate of
  exchange to be applied on any day in determining the amount in
  Canadian dollars of a deposit in a currency of a country other than
  Canada is to be determined in accordance with rules prescribed under
  subsection 26.03(2) of the [Canada Deposit Insurance Corporation
  Act](/eng/acts/C-3).

- Definition of [[deposit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsection (2),
  [[deposit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning that would be given to
  that term by the schedule to the [Canada Deposit Insurance Corporation
  Act](/eng/acts/C-3) for the purposes of deposit insurance if that
  schedule were read without reference to subsections 2(2), (5) and (6)
  of that schedule, but does not include prescribed deposits.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the deposits referred to in subsection
    (4); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing terms and conditions with respect to
    the acceptance of those deposits.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 413
- 2007, c. 6, s. 358
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-413-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 413"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice before opening account or
providing prescribed product

- **[[413.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-413.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Before a company referred to in
  paragraph 413(1)(b) or (c) opens a deposit account in Canada or
  provides in Canada any prescribed product that relates to a deposit,
  the company shall, in the prescribed manner, give the person
  requesting the opening of the account or the provision of the product

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a notice in writing that deposits to the deposit
    account, or that the deposit that relates to the prescribed product,
    as the case may be, will not be insured by the Canada Deposit
    Insurance Corporation or, if the request is made by telephone, a
    verbal notice to that effect; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any other information that may be prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company referred to in paragraph 413(1)(b) or (c)
  shall, in accordance with any regulations that may be made,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} post notices at all of its branches, and at
    prescribed points of service, in Canada where deposits are accepted,
    and on all of its websites at which deposits are accepted in Canada,
    to inform the public that deposits with the company are not insured
    by the Canada Deposit Insurance Corporation; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} include in its advertisements notices to inform the
    public that deposits with the company are not insured by the Canada
    Deposit Insurance Corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the manner in which notices referred to
    in subsection (1) are to be given and the additional information to
    be contained in the notices; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting notices for the purpose of subsection
    (2).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 6, s. 358
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deposits less than \$150,000

- **[[413.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-413.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, a
  company referred to in paragraph 413(1)(b) or (c) may not, in respect
  of its business in Canada, act as agent for any person in the taking
  of a deposit that is less than \$150,000 and payable in Canada.

- Definition of [[deposit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[deposit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has
  the meaning assigned by subsection 413(4).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations
  respecting the circumstances in which, and the conditions under which,
  a company referred to in subsection (1) may act as agent for any
  person in the taking of a deposit that is less than \$150,000 and
  payable in Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 6, s. 358
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Shared premises

- **[[413.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-413.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, no
  company referred to in paragraph 413(1)(b) or (c) shall carry on
  business in Canada on premises that are shared with those of a member
  institution, within the meaning of section 2 of the [Canada Deposit
  Insurance Corporation Act](/eng/acts/C-3), that is affiliated with the
  company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) only applies in respect of premises or
  any portion of premises on which both the company and the member
  institution carry on business with the public and to which the public
  has access.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adjacent premises

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, no company referred to in
  paragraph 413(1)(b) or (c) shall carry on business in Canada on
  premises that are adjacent to a branch or office of a member
  institution, within the meaning of section 2 of the [Canada Deposit
  Insurance Corporation Act](/eng/acts/C-3), that is affiliated with the
  company, unless the company clearly indicates to its customers that
  its business and the premises on which it is carried on are separate
  and distinct from the business and premises of the affiliated member
  institution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting the circumstances in which, and the
    conditions under which, a company referred to in paragraph 413(1)(b)
    or (c) may carry on business in Canada on premises that are shared
    with those of a member institution referred to in subsection (1);
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting the circumstances in which, and the
    conditions under which, a company referred to in paragraph 413(1)(b)
    or (c) may carry on business in Canada on premises that are adjacent
    to a branch or office of a member institution referred to in
    subsection (3).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 6, s. 358
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on guarantees

- **[[414]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-414 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company shall not guarantee on behalf of any person the payment or
  repayment of any sum of money unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the sum of money is a fixed sum of money with or
    without interest thereon; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person on whose behalf the company has
    undertaken to guarantee the payment or repayment has an unqualified
    obligation to reimburse the company for the full amount of the
    payment or repayment to be guaranteed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(a) does not apply where the person on
  whose behalf the company has undertaken to guarantee a payment or
  repayment is a subsidiary of the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (1), a company may
  guarantee repayment of the principal or payment of the interest, or
  both, of any moneys entrusted to the company for investment, on such
  terms and conditions as are agreed on.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations imposing
  terms and conditions in respect of guarantees permitted by this
  section.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 414
- 1997, c. 15, s. 376
- 2001, c. 9, s. 532
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on securities activities

**[[415]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-415 .sectionLabel}** A company shall not
deal in Canada in securities to the extent prohibited or restricted by
such regulations as the Governor in Council may make for the purposes of
this section.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition

- **[[415.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-415.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} It is prohibited for a company to
  issue a debt obligation in relation to which the amounts of principal
  and interest owing are guaranteed to be paid from loans or other
  assets held by an entity that is created and organized for the
  principal purpose of holding those loans or other assets and with the
  intention of legally isolating those loans or other assets from the
  company, unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the debt obligation is a covered bond as defined in
    section 21.5 of the [National Housing Act](/eng/acts/N-11);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the company is a registered issuer as defined in
    section 21.5 of that Act other than one whose right to issue covered
    bonds has been suspended; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the debt obligation is issued under a registered
    program as defined in section 21.5 of that Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations
  exempting any type of debt obligation from the application of
  subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2012, c. 19, s. 361
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on insurance business

- **[[416]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-416 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company shall not undertake the business of insurance except to the
  extent permitted by this Act or the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on acting as agent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company shall not act in Canada as agent for any
  person in the placing of insurance and shall not lease or provide
  space in any branch in Canada of the company to any person engaged in
  the placing of insurance.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations
  respecting the matters referred to in subsection (1) and regulations
  respecting relations between companies and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} entities that undertake the business of insurance;
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} insurance agents or insurance brokers.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Saving

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Nothing in this section precludes a company from

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} requiring insurance to be placed by a borrower for
    the security of the company; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} obtaining group insurance for its employees or the
    employees of any bodies corporate in which it has a substantial
    investment pursuant to section 453.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No pressure

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} No company shall exercise pressure on a borrower to
  place insurance for the security of the company in any particular
  insurance company, but a company may require that an insurance company
  chosen by a borrower meet with its approval, which shall not be
  unreasonably withheld.

- Definition of [[business of insurance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[business of
  insurance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the issuance of any annuity if the liability in
    respect of the annuity is contingent on the death of a person; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the issuance of any debt obligation, any of whose
    terms and conditions are established on the basis of mortality
    considerations, under which the issuer is obliged to make periodic
    payments.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 416
- 2012, c. 19, s. 205
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-416-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 416"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on leasing

**[[417]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-417 .sectionLabel}** A company shall not
engage in Canada in any personal property leasing activity in which a
financial leasing entity, within the meaning of subsection 449(1), is
not permitted to engage.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 417
- 2001, c. 9, s. 533
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on residential mortgages

- **[[418]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-418 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company shall not make a loan in Canada on the security of residential
  property in Canada for the purpose of purchasing, renovating or
  improving that property, or refinance such a loan, if the amount of
  the loan, together with the amount then outstanding of any mortgage
  having an equal or prior claim against the property, would exceed 80
  per cent of the value of the property at the time of the loan.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a loan made or guaranteed under the [National
    Housing Act](/eng/acts/N-11) or any other Act of Parliament by or
    pursuant to which a different limit on the value of property on the
    security of which the company may make a loan is established;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a loan if repayment of the amount of the loan that
    exceeds the maximum amount set out in subsection (1) is guaranteed
    or insured by a government agency or a private insurer approved by
    the Superintendent;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the acquisition by the company from an entity of
    securities issued or guaranteed by the entity that are secured on
    any residential property, whether in favour of a trustee or
    otherwise, or the making of a loan by the company to the entity
    against the issue of such securities; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a loan secured by a mortgage where

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the mortgage is taken back by the company on a
      property disposed of by the company, including where the
      disposition is by way of a realization of a security interest, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the mortgage secures payment of an amount
      payable to the company for the property.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 418
- 1997, c. 15, s. 377
- 2007, c. 6, s. 359
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-418-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 418"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on charges to borrowers

- **[[418.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-418.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to any regulations made
  under subsection (2), a company that has obtained insurance or a
  guarantee against default on a loan made in Canada on the security of
  residential property shall not charge a borrower an amount for the
  insurance or guarantee that exceeds the actual cost to the company of
  the insurance or guarantee.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting the determination of the actual cost to
    a company for the purposes of subsection (1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting the circumstances in which a company is
    exempt from the application of subsection (1);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting, in relation to insurance or a guarantee
    against default on a loan made by a company in Canada on the
    security of residential property,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the arrangements into which the company, its
      representatives and its employees may or may not enter, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the payments or benefits that the company, its
      representatives and its employees may or may not accept from an
      insurer or the insurer's affiliates; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} respecting any other matters necessary to carry out
    the purposes of subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations --- disclosure

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations
  respecting the disclosure by a company of information relating to
  insurance or a guarantee against default on a loan made by the company
  in Canada on the security of residential property, including
  regulations respecting

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information that must be disclosed, including
    information relating to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person who benefits from the insurance or
      guarantee,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the arrangements between the company, its
      representatives or its employees and the insurer or the insurer's
      affiliates, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the payments and benefits that the company, its
      representatives and its employees accept from an insurer or the
      insurer's affiliates;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the time and place at which, the form and manner in
    which and the persons to whom information is to be disclosed; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the circumstances under which a company is not
    required to disclose information.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 2, s. 290
- 2012, c. 5, s. 167(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-418.1-20100701.html "Link to previous version of section 418.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Policies re security interests

- **[[419]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-419 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  directors of a company shall establish and the company shall adhere to
  policies regarding the creation of security interests in property of
  the company to secure obligations of the company and the acquisition
  by the company of beneficial interests in property that is subject to
  security interests.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order to amend policies

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent may, by order, direct a company to
  amend its policies as specified in the order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A company shall comply with an order made under
  subsection (2) within the time specified in the order.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 419
- 1999, c. 31, s. 219(E)
- 2001, c. 9, s. 534
- 2007, c. 6, s. 360(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-419-20040101.html "Link to previous version of section 419"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations and guidelines

**[[419.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-419.1 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in
Council may make regulations and the Superintendent may make guidelines
respecting the creation by a company of security interests in its
property to secure obligations of the company and the acquisition by the
company of beneficial interests in property that is subject to security
interests.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 534
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

**[[419.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-419.2 .sectionLabel}** Sections 419 and
419.1 do not apply in respect of a security interest created by a
company to secure an obligation of the company to the Bank of Canada or
the Canada Deposit Insurance Corporation.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 534
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on receivers

**[[420]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-420 .sectionLabel}** A company shall not
grant to a person the right to appoint a receiver or a receiver and
manager of the property or business of the company.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on partnerships

- **[[421]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-421
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except with the approval of the
  Superintendent, a company may not be a general partner in a limited
  partnership or a partner in a general partnership.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Meaning of [[general
  partnership]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), [[general
  partnership]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any partnership other than a
  limited partnership.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 421
- 2001, c. 9, s. 535
:::

#### [Fiduciary Activities]{.HTitleText3} {#h-456964 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Separate and distinct

- **[[422]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-422 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company shall keep money and other assets acquired or held in trust by
  the company separate and distinct from its own assets and shall keep a
  separate account for each trust.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Common trust fund

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Unless the instrument creating a trust otherwise
  provides, a company may invest money it holds in trust in one or more
  common trust funds.

#### [Deposit Acceptance]{.HTitleText3} {#h-456970 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deposit acceptance

- **[[423]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-423 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company may, without the intervention of any other person,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} accept a deposit from any person whether or not the
    person is qualified by law to enter into contracts; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} pay all or part of the principal of the deposit and
    all or part of the interest thereon to or to the order of that
    person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(b) does not apply if, before payment,
  the money deposited in the company pursuant to paragraph (1)(a) is
  claimed by some other person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in any action or proceeding to which the company is
    a party and in respect of which service of a writ or other process
    originating that action or proceeding has been made on the company,
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other action or proceeding pursuant to which
    an injunction or order made by the court requiring the company not
    to make payment of that money or make payment thereof to some person
    other than the depositor has been served on the company,

  and, in the case of any such claim so made, the money so deposited may
  be paid to the depositor with the consent of the claimant or to the
  claimant with the consent of the depositor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Guaranteed trust money

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A company that is a trust company pursuant to
  subsection 57(2) shall accept deposits only as guaranteed trust money.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Profit

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (3), a company that is a
  trust company pursuant to subsection 57(2) that accepts deposits may
  retain the interest and profit resulting from the investment thereof
  in excess of the amount of interest payable to its depositors in
  respect thereof.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assets to be identified

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where a company that is a trust company pursuant to
  subsection 57(2) accepts deposits, the company shall identify on its
  books assets to be held in respect thereof equal to the aggregate
  amount of the deposits.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Execution of trust

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A company is not bound to see to the execution of any
  trust to which any deposit made under the authority of this Act is
  subject, other than a trust of which the company is a trustee.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment when company has notice of
  trust

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (6) applies regardless of whether the
  trust is express or arises by the operation of law, and it applies
  even when the company has notice of the trust if it acts on the order
  of or under the authority of the holder or holders of the account into
  which the deposit is made.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 423
- 2001, c. 9, s. 536
:::

#### [Unclaimed Balances]{.HTitleText3} {#h-456993 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unclaimed balances

- **[[424]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-424
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a deposit has been made in Canada that is payable
    in Canada and in respect of which no transaction has taken place and
    no statement of account has been requested or acknowledged by the
    creditor during a period of 10 years

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a deposit made for a fixed period,
      from the day on which the fixed period terminated, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of any other deposit, from the day
      on which the last transaction took place or a statement of account
      was last requested or acknowledged by the creditor, whichever is
      later, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a cheque, draft or bill of exchange (including any
    of those instruments drawn by one branch of a company on another of
    its branches but not including an instrument issued in payment of a
    dividend on the capital of a company) payable in Canada has been
    issued, certified or accepted by a company in Canada and no payment
    has been made in respect of it for a period of 10 years after the
    date of issue, certification, acceptance or maturity, whichever is
    later,

  the company shall pay to the Bank of Canada not later than December 31
  in each year an amount equal to the principal amount of the deposit or
  instrument, plus interest, if any, calculated in accordance with the
  terms of the deposit or instrument, and payment accordingly discharges
  the company from all liability in respect of the deposit or
  instrument.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exchange rate

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} If a deposit referred to in paragraph (1)(a) or an
  instrument referred to in paragraph (1)(b) is not in Canadian
  currency, then, before paying to the Bank of Canada the amount
  required by subsection (1), the company shall convert the amount of
  the deposit or instrument to Canadian currency at a rate of exchange
  determined in accordance with rules prescribed under subsection
  26.03(2) of the [Canada Deposit Insurance Corporation
  Act](/eng/acts/C-3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provision of information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company shall, on making a payment under subsection
  (1), provide the Bank of Canada, for each deposit or instrument in
  respect of which the payment is made, with the following information
  current as of the day the payment is made, in so far as it is known to
  the company:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a deposit,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the name of the depositor in whose name the
      deposit is held, as well as their date of birth and Social
      Insurance Number, if they are a natural person,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the recorded address of the depositor,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the outstanding amount of the deposit, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the branch of the company at which the last
      transaction took place in respect of the deposit, and the date of
      that last transaction; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an instrument,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the name of the person to whom or at whose
      request the instrument was issued, certified or accepted, as well
      as their date of birth and Social Insurance Number, if they are a
      natural person,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the recorded address of that person,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the name of the payee of the instrument,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the amount and date of the instrument,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the name of the place where the instrument was
      payable, and

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the branch of the company at which the
      instrument was issued, certified or accepted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies of signature cards and signing
  authorities

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} A company shall provide the Bank of Canada with
  copies of signature cards and signing authorities relating to any
  deposit or instrument in respect of which it has made a payment under
  subsection (1). If it does not have any with respect to a deposit or
  instrument to which the request relates, it shall so inform the Bank
  of Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment to claimant

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 22 of the [Bank of Canada
  Act](/eng/acts/B-2), where payment has been made to the Bank of Canada
  under subsection (1) in respect of any deposit or instrument, and if
  payment is demanded or the instrument is presented at the Bank of
  Canada by the person who, but for that section, would be entitled to
  receive payment of the deposit or instrument, the Bank of Canada is
  liable to pay, at its agency in the province in which the deposit or
  instrument was payable, an amount equal to the amount so paid to it
  together with interest, if interest was payable under the terms of the
  deposit or instrument,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for a period not exceeding ten years from the day
    on which the payment was received by the Bank of Canada until the
    date of payment to the claimant; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at such rate and computed in such manner as the
    Minister determines.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Enforcing liability

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The liability of the Bank of Canada under
  subsection (3) may be enforced by action against the Bank of Canada in
  the court in the province in which the deposit or instrument was
  payable.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of subsection (1)

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) applies only in respect of deposits
  made, and cheques, drafts and bills of exchange issued, certified or
  accepted after May 31, 1990.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} This section shall not apply until the day that is
  eight years after the day on which this section comes into force.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 424
- 1993, c. 34, s. 127
- 2007, c. 6, s. 361
- [2021, c. 23, s. 143](#1307037-1417801){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1307037-1417801.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-424-20070420.html "Link to previous version of section 424"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of unpaid amount

- **[[425]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-425 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company shall send, by mail and by electronic means, to each person to
  whom a deposit referred to in paragraph 424(1)(a) is payable, or to
  each person to whom or at whose request an instrument referred to in
  paragraph 424(1)(b) was issued, certified or accepted, at their
  recorded address and electronic address in so far as they are known to
  the company, a notice stating that the deposit or instrument remains
  unpaid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When notice to be given

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A notice required by subsection (1) shall be given
  during the month of January next following the end of the first two
  year period, and also during the month of January next following the
  end of the first five year period,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a deposit made for a fixed period,
    after the fixed period has terminated;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of any other deposit, in respect of
    which no transaction has taken place and no statement of account has
    been requested or acknowledged by the creditor; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a cheque, draft or bill of exchange,
    in respect of which the instrument has remained unpaid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When notice to be sent

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The notice must be sent during the month of January
  next following the end of the first two-year period, during the month
  of January next following the end of the first five-year period and
  also during the month of January next following the end of the first
  nine-year period

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed before coming into force, 2008, c.
    20, s. 3\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of any other deposit, in respect of
    which no transaction has taken place and no statement of account has
    been requested or acknowledged by the creditor; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed before coming into force, 2008, c.
    20, s. 3\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 425
- 2007, c. 6, s. 362
- [2008, c. 20, s. 3](#441558-1337469){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="441558-1337469.html"
  role="button"}
- [2021, c. 23, s. 144](#1307050-1417803){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1307050-1417803.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-425-20220101.html "Link to previous version of section 425"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Accounts]{.HTitleText3} {#h-457048 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[425.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-425.1 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this section and in sections 431 to 434, 444.1 and
444.3.

[[member company]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[member company]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a company that is a
    member institution as defined in section 2 of the [Canada Deposit
    Insurance Corporation Act](/eng/acts/C-3). ([société
    membre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[personal deposit account]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[personal deposit account]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a deposit
    account in the name of one or more natural persons that is kept by
    that person or those persons for a purpose other than that of
    carrying on business. ([compte de dépôt personnel]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[retail deposit account]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[retail deposit account]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a personal
    deposit account that is opened with a deposit of less than \$150,000
    or any greater amount that may be prescribed. ([compte de dépôt de
    détail]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 538
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Account charges

**[[426]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-426 .sectionLabel}** A company shall not,
directly or indirectly, charge or receive any sum for the keeping of an
account unless the charge is made by express agreement between the
company and a customer or by order of a court.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure on opening account

- **[[427]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-427 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company shall not, after the day that is six months after the coming
  into force of this Part, open or maintain an interest-bearing deposit
  account in Canada in the name of any natural person unless the company
  discloses, in accordance with the regulations, to the person who
  requests the company to open the account, the rate of interest
  applicable to the account and how the amount of interest to be paid is
  to be calculated.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of an
  interest-bearing deposit account that is opened with a deposit in
  excess of \$150,000 or any greater amount that may be prescribed.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 427
- 2001, c. 9, s. 539
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure in advertisements

**[[428]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-428 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
authorize the publication, issue or appearance of any advertisement in
Canada that indicates the rate of interest offered by a company on an
interest-bearing deposit or a debt obligation unless the advertisement
discloses, in accordance with the regulations, how the amount of
interest is to be calculated.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure regulations

**[[429]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-429 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may make regulations respecting

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the time and place at which and the form and manner
  in which disclosure is to be made by a company of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} interest rates applicable to debts of the company
    and deposits with the company, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the manner in which the amount of interest paid is
    to be calculated;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the manner in which any charges for the keeping of an
  account are to be disclosed by a company to its customers and when the
  disclosure is to be made; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} such other matters or things as may be necessary to
  carry out the requirements of sections 426 to 428.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 429
- 2012, c. 5, s. 168
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-429-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 429"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[430]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-430 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2001, c.
9, s. 540\]]{.Repealed}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure required on opening a deposit
account

- **[[431]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-431
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) to (4), a
  company shall not open a deposit account in the name of a customer
  unless, at or before the time the account is opened, the company
  provides in writing to the individual who requests the opening of the
  account

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the account agreement with the company;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} information about all charges applicable to the
    account;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} information about how the customer will be notified
    of any increase in those charges and of any new charges applicable
    to the account;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} information about the company's procedures relating
    to complaints about the application of any charge applicable to the
    account; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} such other information as may be prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a deposit account is not a personal deposit
  account and the amount of a charge applicable to the account cannot be
  established at or before the time the account is opened, the company
  shall, as soon as is practicable after the amount is established,
  provide the customer in whose name the account is kept with a notice
  in writing of the amount of the charge.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a company has a deposit account in the name of a
  customer and the customer by telephone requests the opening of another
  deposit account in the name of the customer and the company has not
  complied with subsection (1) in respect of the opening of that other
  account, the company shall not open the account unless it provides the
  customer orally with any information prescribed at or before the time
  the account is opened.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure in writing

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a company opens an account under subsection (3),
  it shall, not later than seven business days after the account is
  opened, provide to the customer in writing the agreement and
  information referred to in subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to close account

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A customer may, within 14 business days after a
  deposit account is opened under subsection (3), close the account
  without charge and in such case is entitled to a refund of any charges
  related to the operation of the account, other than interest charges,
  incurred while the account was open.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (4), the Governor in
  Council may make regulations prescribing circumstances in which, and
  the time when, the agreement and information will be deemed to have
  been provided to the customer.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 431
- 1997, c. 15, s. 378
- 2001, c. 9, s. 541
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure of charges

**[[432]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-432 .sectionLabel}** A company shall
disclose to its customers and to the public, at the prescribed time and
place and in the prescribed form and manner, the charges applicable to
deposit accounts with the company and the usual amount, if any, charged
by the company for services normally provided by the company to its
customers and to the public.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 432
- 2012, c. 5, s. 169
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-432-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 432"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No increase or new charges without
disclosure

- **[[433]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-433 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company shall not increase any charge applicable to a personal deposit
  account with the company or introduce any new charge applicable to a
  personal deposit account with the company unless the company discloses
  the charge at the prescribed time and place and in the prescribed form
  and manner to the customer in whose name the account is kept.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mandatory disclosure

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} With respect to prescribed services in relation to
  deposit accounts, other than personal deposit accounts, a company
  shall not increase any charge for any such service in relation to a
  deposit account with the company or introduce any new charge for any
  such service in relation to a deposit account with the company unless
  the company discloses the charge at the prescribed time and place and
  in the prescribed form and manner to the customer in whose name the
  account is kept.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 433
- 2012, c. 5, s. 169
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-433-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 433"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

**[[434]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-434 .sectionLabel}** Sections 431 to 433
apply only in respect of charges applicable to deposit accounts with the
company in Canada and services provided by the company in Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 434
- 2001, c. 9, s. 542
:::

#### [Registered Products]{.HTitleText3} {#h-457112 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure required concerning registered
products

- **[[434.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-434.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), a company
  shall not open an account that is or forms part of a registered
  product in the name of a customer, or enter into an agreement with a
  customer for a prescribed product or service that is or forms part of
  a registered product, unless the company provides, in the prescribed
  manner, to the individual requesting the account or the prescribed
  product or service

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} information about all charges applicable to the
    registered product;
