  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} information about how the customer will be notified
    of any increase in those charges and of any new charges applicable
    to the registered product;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} information about the company's procedures relating
    to complaints about the application of any charge applicable to the
    registered product; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any other information that may be prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations
  specifying the circumstances under which a company need not provide
  the information.

- Definition of [[registered product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[registered
  product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a product that is defined to be a
  registered product by the regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 6, s. 363
:::

#### [Borrowing Costs]{.HTitleText3} {#h-457126 .Subheading}

Definition of [[cost of borrowing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

**[[435]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-435 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
this section and sections 435.1 to 442, [[cost of
borrowing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of a loan made by a
company,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the interest or discount applicable to the loan;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any amount charged in connection with the loan that
  is payable by the borrower to the company; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} any charge prescribed to be included in the cost of
  borrowing.

For those purposes, however, [[cost of borrowing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
does not include any charge prescribed to be excluded from the cost of
borrowing.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 435
- 1997, c. 15, s. 379
- 2001, c. 9, s. 543
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rebate of borrowing costs

- **[[435.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-435.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where a company makes a loan in
  respect of which the disclosure requirements of section 436 apply and
  the loan is not secured by a mortgage on real property and is required
  to be repaid either on a fixed future date or by instalments, the
  company shall, if there is a prepayment of the loan, rebate to the
  borrower a portion of the charges included in the cost of borrowing in
  respect of the loan.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The charges to be rebated do not include the interest
  or discount applicable to the loan.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations
  governing the rebate of charges under subsection (1). The rebate shall
  be made in accordance with those regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 15, s. 379
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosing borrowing costs

- **[[436]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-436 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company shall not make a loan to a natural person that is repayable in
  Canada unless the cost of borrowing, as calculated and expressed in
  accordance with section 437, and other prescribed information have
  been disclosed by the company to the borrower at the prescribed time
  and place and in the prescribed form and manner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of a loan
  that is of a prescribed class of loans.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 436
- 1997, c. 15, s. 379
- 2012, c. 5, s. 170
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-436-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 436"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calculating borrowing costs

**[[437]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-437 .sectionLabel}** The cost of borrowing
shall be calculated, in the prescribed manner, on the basis that all
obligations of the borrower are duly fulfilled and shall be expressed as
a rate per annum and, in prescribed circumstances, as an amount in
dollars and cents.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional disclosure

- **[[438]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-438
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where a company makes a loan in
  respect of which the disclosure requirements of section 436 are
  applicable and the loan is required to be repaid either on a fixed
  future date or by instalments, the company shall disclose to the
  borrower, in accordance with the regulations,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} whether the borrower has the right to repay the
    amount borrowed before the maturity of the loan and, if applicable,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any terms and conditions relating to that right,
      including the particulars of the circumstances in which the
      borrower may exercise that right, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} whether, in the event that the borrower
      exercises the right, any portion of the cost of borrowing is to be
      rebated, the manner in which any such rebate is to be calculated
      or, if a charge or penalty will be imposed on the borrower, the
      manner in which the charge or penalty is to be calculated;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the event that an amount borrowed is not repaid
    at maturity or, if applicable, an instalment is not paid on the day
    the instalment is due to be paid, particulars of the charges or
    penalties to be paid by the borrower because of the failure to repay
    or pay in accordance with the contract governing the loan;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} at the prescribed time and place and in the
    prescribed form and manner, any prescribed changes respecting the
    cost of borrowing or the loan agreement;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} particulars of any other rights and obligations of
    the borrower; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any other prescribed information, at the prescribed
    time and place and in the prescribed form and manner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure in credit card applications

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} A company shall, in accordance with the
  regulations, at the prescribed time and place and in the prescribed
  form and manner, provide prescribed information in any application
  forms or related documents that it prepares for the issuance of
  credit, payment or charge cards and provide prescribed information to
  any person applying to it for a credit, payment or charge card.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure re credit cards

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a company issues or has issued a credit,
  payment or charge card to a natural person, the company shall, in
  addition to disclosing the costs of borrowing in respect of any loan
  obtained through the use of the card, disclose to the person, in
  accordance with the regulations,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any charges or penalties described in paragraph
    (1)(b);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} particulars of the person's rights and obligations;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any charges for which the person becomes
    responsible by accepting or using the card;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} at the prescribed time and place and in the
    prescribed form and manner, any prescribed changes respecting the
    cost of borrowing or the loan agreement; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any other prescribed information, at the prescribed
    time and place and in the prescribed form and manner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional disclosure re other loans

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where a company enters into or has entered into an
  arrangement, including a line of credit, for the making of a loan in
  respect of which the disclosure requirements of section 436 apply and
  the loan is not a loan in respect of which subsection (1) or (2)
  applies, the company shall, in addition to disclosing the costs of
  borrowing, disclose to the person to whom the loan is made, in
  accordance with the regulations,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any charges or penalties described in paragraph
    (1)(b);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} particulars of the person's rights and obligations;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any charges for which the person is responsible
    under the arrangement;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} at the prescribed time and place and in the
    prescribed form and manner, any prescribed changes respecting the
    cost of borrowing under the arrangement; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any other prescribed information, at the prescribed
    time and place and in the prescribed form and manner.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 438
- 1997, c. 15, s. 380
- 2012, c. 5, s. 171
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-438-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 438"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Renewal statement

**[[438.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-438.1 .sectionLabel}** If a company makes
a loan in respect of which the disclosure requirements of section 436
apply and the loan is secured by a mortgage on real property, the
company shall disclose to the borrower, at the prescribed time and place
and in the prescribed form and manner, any information that is
prescribed respecting the renewal of the loan.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 15, s. 381
- 2012, c. 5, s. 172
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-438.1-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 438.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure in advertising

**[[439]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-439 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
authorize the publication, issue or appearance of any advertisement in
Canada relating to arrangements referred to in subsection 438(3), loans,
credit cards, payment cards or charge cards, offered to natural persons
by a company, and purporting to disclose prescribed information about
the cost of borrowing or about any other matter unless the advertisement
discloses prescribed information at the prescribed time and place and in
the prescribed form and manner.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 439
- 1997, c. 15, s. 381
- 2012, c. 5, s. 172
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-439-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 439"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations re borrowing costs

**[[440]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-440 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may make regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting the time and place at which, and the form
  and manner in which, a company is to disclose to a borrower

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the cost of borrowing,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any rebate of the cost of borrowing, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} any other information relating to a loan,
    arrangement, credit card, payment card or charge card referred to in
    section 438;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting the contents of any statement disclosing
  the cost of borrowing and other information required to be disclosed
  by a company to a borrower;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting the manner of calculating the cost of
  borrowing;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} respecting the circumstances under which the cost of
  borrowing is to be expressed as an amount in dollars and cents;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} specifying any class of loans that are not to be
  subject to section 435.1, subsection 436(1) or 438(1) or (3) or
  section 438.1 or 439 or the regulations or any specified provisions of
  the regulations;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} respecting the time and place at which, and the form
  and manner in which, any rights, obligations, charges or penalties
  referred to in sections 435.1 to 439 are to be disclosed;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} prohibiting the imposition of any charge or penalty
  referred to in section 438 or providing that the charge or penalty, if
  imposed, will not exceed a prescribed amount;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} respecting the nature or amount of any charge or
  penalty referred to in paragraph 438(1)(b), (2)(a) or (3)(a) and the
  costs of the company that may be included or excluded in the
  determination of the charge or penalty;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} respecting the method of calculating the amount of
  rebate of the cost of borrowing, or the portion of the cost of
  borrowing referred to in subparagraph 438(1)(a)(ii);

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} respecting advertisements made by a company regarding
  arrangements referred to in subsection 438(3), loans, credit cards,
  payment cards or charge cards;

- [(k)]{.lawlabel} respecting the renewal of loans; and

- [(l)]{.lawlabel} respecting such other matters or things as are
  necessary to carry out the purposes of sections 435.1 to 439.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 440
- 1997, c. 15, s. 381
- 2012, c. 5, s. 173
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-440-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 440"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Complaints]{.HTitleText3} {#h-457208 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Procedures for dealing with complaints

- **[[441]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-441 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} establish procedures for dealing with complaints
    made by persons having requested or received products or services in
    Canada from the company;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} designate an officer or employee of the company to
    be responsible for implementing those procedures; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} designate one or more officers or employees of the
    company to receive and deal with those complaints.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Procedures to be filed with
  Commissioner

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company shall file with the Commissioner a copy of
  its procedures established under paragraph (1)(a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}How procedures to be made available

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A company shall make its procedures established under
  paragraph (1)(a) available

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the form of a brochure, at its branches where
    products or services are offered in Canada;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} on its websites through which products or services
    are offered in Canada; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in written format to be sent to any person who
    requests them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information on contacting Agency

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A company shall also make prescribed information on
  how to contact the Agency available whenever it makes its procedures
  established under paragraph (1)(a) available under subsection (3).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 441
- 1997, c. 15, s. 382
- 2001, c. 9, s. 545
- 2007, c. 6, s. 364
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-441-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 441"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation to be member of complaints
body

**[[441.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-441.1 .sectionLabel}** In any province, if
there is no law of the province that makes a company subject to the
jurisdiction of an organization that deals with complaints made by
persons having requested or received products or services in the
province from a company, the company shall be a member of an
organization that is not controlled by it and that deals with those
complaints that have not been resolved to the satisfaction of the
persons under procedures established by companies under paragraph
441(1)(a).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 546
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information on contacting Agency

- **[[442]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-442 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company shall, in accordance with the regulations, at the prescribed
  time and place and in the prescribed form and manner, provide a person
  requesting or receiving a product or service from it with prescribed
  information on how to contact the Agency if the person has a complaint
  about a deposit account, an arrangement referred to in subsection
  438(3), a payment, credit or charge card, the disclosure of or manner
  of calculating the cost of borrowing in respect of a loan or about any
  other obligation of the company under a consumer provision.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner shall prepare a report, to be
  included in the report referred to in section 34 of the [Financial
  Consumer Agency of Canada Act](/eng/acts/F-11.1), respecting

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} procedures for dealing with complaints established
    by companies pursuant to paragraph 441(1)(a); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number and nature of complaints that have been
    brought to the attention of the Agency by persons who have requested
    or received a product or service from a company.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 442
- 1997, c. 15, s. 383
- 2001, c. 9, s. 547
- 2012, c. 5, s. 174
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-442-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 442"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Miscellaneous]{.HTitleText2} {#h-457239 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charges for prescribed products or
services

**[[442.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-442.1 .sectionLabel}** A company shall
not, directly or indirectly, charge or receive any sum for the provision
of any prescribed products or services unless the charge is made by
express agreement between it and a customer or by order of a court.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 6, s. 365
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prepayment protected

- **[[443]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-443 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company shall not make a loan to a natural person that is repayable in
  Canada, the terms of which prohibit prepayment of the money advanced
  or any instalment thereon before its due date.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minimum balance

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Except by express agreement between the company and
  the borrower, the making in Canada of a loan or advance by a company
  to a borrower shall not be subject to a condition that the borrower
  maintain a minimum credit balance with the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of subsection (1)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of a loan

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is secured by a mortgage on real property; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that is made for business purposes and the
    principal amount of which is more than \$100,000 or such other
    amount as may be prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Government cheques

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A company shall not make a charge

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for cashing a cheque or other instrument drawn on
    the Receiver General or on the Receiver General's account in the
    Bank of Canada, in a company or in any other deposit-taking Canadian
    financial institution incorporated by or under an Act of Parliament;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for cashing any other instrument issued as
    authority for the payment of money out of the Consolidated Revenue
    Fund; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of any cheque or other instrument that
    is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} drawn in favour of the Receiver General, the
      Government of Canada or any department thereof or any public
      officer acting in the capacity of a public officer, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} tendered for deposit to the credit of the
      Receiver General.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deposits of Government of Canada

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Nothing in subsection (4) precludes any arrangement
  between the Government of Canada and a company concerning

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} compensation for services performed by the company
    for the Government of Canada; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} interest to be paid on any or all deposits of the
    Government of Canada with the company.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 443
- 1997, c. 15, s. 384
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations respecting the holding of
funds

**[[443.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-443.1 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in
Council may make regulations respecting the maximum period during which
a company may hold funds in respect of specified classes of cheques or
other instruments that are deposited into an account at a branch or
prescribed point of service in Canada before permitting the customer in
whose name the account is kept to access the funds.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 6, s. 366
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations --- activities

**[[443.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-443.2 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in
Council may make regulations respecting any matters involving a
company's dealings, or its employees' or representatives' dealings, with
customers or the public, including

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} what a company may or may not do in carrying out any
  of the activities in which it is permitted to engage, or in providing
  any of the services that it may provide, under section 409 and any
  ancillary, related or incidental activities or services; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the time and place at which and the form and manner
  in which any of those activities are to be carried out or any of those
  services are to be provided.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 2, s. 291
- 2012, c. 5, s. 175
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-443.2-20090312.html "Link to previous version of section 443.2"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations re customer information

**[[444]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-444 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may make regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} requiring a company to establish procedures regarding
  the collection, retention, use and disclosure of any information about
  its customers or any class of customers;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} requiring a company to establish procedures for
  dealing with complaints made by a customer about the collection,
  retention, use or disclosure of information about the customer;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting the disclosure by a company of information
  relating to the procedures referred to in paragraphs (a) and (b);

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} requiring a company to designate the officers and
  employees of the company who are responsible for

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} implementing the procedures referred to in
    paragraph (b), and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} receiving and dealing with complaints made by a
    customer of the company about the collection, retention, use or
    disclosure of information about the customer;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} requiring a company to report information relating to

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} complaints made by customers of the company about
    the collection, retention, use or disclosure of information, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the actions taken by the company to deal with the
    complaints; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} defining "information", "collection" and "retention"
  for the purposes of paragraphs (a) to (e) and the regulations made
  under those paragraphs.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 444
- 1997, c. 15, s. 385
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of branch closure

- **[[444.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-444.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to regulations made under
  subsection (5), a member company with a branch in Canada at which it,
  through a natural person, opens retail deposit accounts and disburses
  cash to customers, shall give notice in accordance with those
  regulations before closing that branch or having it cease to carry on
  either of those activities.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pre-closure meeting

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} After notice is given but before the branch is closed
  or ceases to carry on the activities, the Commissioner shall, in
  prescribed situations, require the company to convene and hold a
  meeting between representatives of the company, representatives of the
  Agency and interested parties in the vicinity of the branch in order
  to exchange views about the closing or cessation of activities,
  including, but not limited to, alternative service delivery by the
  company and measures to help the branch's customers adjust to the
  closing or cessation of activities.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Meeting details

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner may establish rules for convening a
  meeting referred to in subsection (2) and for its conduct.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Not statutory instruments

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The [Statutory Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22) does
  not apply in respect of rules established under subsection (3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations
  prescribing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the time and place at which and the form and manner
    in which notice shall be given under subsection (1), the persons to
    whom it shall be given and the information to be included, the time,
    place, form and manner being permitted to vary according to
    circumstances specified in the regulations;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} circumstances in which a member company is not
    required to give notice under subsection (1), circumstances in which
    the Commissioner may exempt a member company from the requirement to
    give notice under that subsection, and circumstances in which the
    Commissioner may vary the time and place at which and the form and
    manner in which notice is required to be given under any regulation
    made under paragraph (a); and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} circumstances in which a meeting may be convened
    under subsection (2).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 548
- 2007, c. 6, s. 367
- 2012, c. 5, s. 176
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-444.1-20070420.html "Link to previous version of section 444.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Public accountability statements

- **[[444.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-444.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A company with equity of \$1 billion
  or more shall, in accordance with regulations made under subsection
  (4), annually publish a statement describing the contribution of the
  company and its prescribed affiliates to the Canadian economy and
  society.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Filing

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company shall, at the prescribed time and place and
  in the prescribed form and manner, file a copy of the statement with
  the Commissioner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provision of statement to public

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A company shall, at the prescribed time and place and
  in the prescribed form and manner, disclose the statement to its
  customers and to the public.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations
  prescribing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name, contents and form of a statement referred
    to in subsection (1) and the time within which, the place at which
    and the manner in which it must be prepared;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} affiliates of a company referred to in subsection
    (1);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the time and place at which and the form and manner
    in which a statement must be filed under subsection (2); and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the time and place at which and the form and manner
    in which a statement mentioned in subsection (3) is to be disclosed,
    respectively, to a company's customers and to the public.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 548
- 2012, c. 5, s. 177
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-444.2-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 444.2"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations re disclosure

**[[444.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-444.3 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in
Council may, subject to any other provisions of this Act relating to the
disclosure of information, make regulations respecting the disclosure of
information by companies or any prescribed class of companies, including
regulations respecting

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information that must be disclosed, including
  information relating to

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any product or service or prescribed class of
    products or services offered by them,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any of their policies, procedures or practices
    relating to the offer by them of any product or service or
    prescribed class of products or services,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} anything they are required to do or to refrain
    from doing under a consumer provision, and

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} any other matter that may affect their dealings,
    or their employees' or representatives' dealings, with customers or
    the public;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the time and place at which, the form and manner in
  which and the persons to whom information is to be disclosed; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the content and form of any advertisement by
  companies or any prescribed class of companies relating to any matter
  referred to in paragraph (a).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 548
- 2007, c. 6, s. 368
- 2012, c. 5, s. 178
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-444.3-20070420.html "Link to previous version of section 444.3"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Bank Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01) security

**[[445]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-445 .sectionLabel}** A bank that is
continued as a company under this Act that, immediately before that
continuance, held any outstanding security pursuant to section 426 or
427 of the [Bank Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01) may continue to hold the
security for the life of the loan to which the security relates and all
the provisions of the [Bank Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01) relating to the
security and its enforcement continue to apply to the company as though
it were a bank.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, ss. 445, 559
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transmission in case of death

- **[[446]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-446
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where the transmission of a debt
  owing by a company by reason of a deposit, of property held by a
  company as security or for safe-keeping or of rights with respect to a
  safety deposit box and property deposited therein takes place because
  of the death of a person, the delivery to the company of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an affidavit or declaration in writing in form
    satisfactory to the company signed by or on behalf of a person
    claiming by virtue of the transmission stating the nature and effect
    of the transmission, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} one of the following documents, namely,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} when the claim is based on a will or other
      testamentary instrument or on a grant of probate thereof or on
      such a grant and letters testamentary or other document of like
      import or on a grant of letters of administration or other
      document of like import, purporting to be issued by any court of
      authority in Canada or elsewhere, an authenticated copy or
      certificate thereof under the seal of the court or authority
      without proof of the authenticity of the seal or other proof, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} when the claim is based on a notarial will, an
      authenticated copy thereof,

  is sufficient justification and authority for giving effect to the
  transmission in accordance with the claim.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Nothing in subsection (1) shall be construed to
  prevent a company from refusing to give effect to a transmission until
  there has been delivered to the company such documentary or other
  evidence of or in connection with the transmission as it may deem
  requisite.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Branch of account with respect to
deposits

- **[[447]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-447 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of this Act, the branch of account with respect to a
  deposit account is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the branch the address or name of which appears on
    the specimen signature card or other signing authority signed by a
    depositor with respect to the deposit account or that is designated
    by agreement between the company and the depositor at the time of
    opening of the deposit account; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if no branch has been identified or agreed on as
    provided in paragraph (a), the branch that is designated as the
    branch of account with respect thereto by the company by notice in
    writing to the depositor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where debt payable

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The amount of any debt owing by a company by reason
  of a deposit in a deposit account in the company is payable to the
  person entitled thereto only at the branch of account and the person
  entitled thereto is not entitled to demand payment or to be paid at
  any other branch of the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (2), a company may permit,
  either occasionally or as a regular practice, the person to whom the
  company is indebted by reason of a deposit in a deposit account in the
  company to withdraw moneys owing by reason of that deposit at a branch
  of the company other than the branch of account or to draw cheques or
  other orders for the payment of such moneys at a branch other than the
  branch of account.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Situs of indebtedness

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The indebtedness of a company by reason of a deposit
  in a deposit account in the company shall be deemed for all purposes
  to be situated at the place where the branch of account is situated.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of writ, etc.

- **[[448]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-448
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (3) and (4),
  the following documents are binding on property belonging to a person
  and in the possession of a company, or on money owing to a person by
  reason of a deposit account in a company, only if the document or a
  notice of it is served at the branch of the company that has
  possession of the property or that is the branch of account in respect
  of the deposit account, as the case may be:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a writ or process originating a legal proceeding or
    issued in or pursuant to a legal proceeding;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an order or injunction made by a court;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an instrument purporting to assign, perfect or
    otherwise dispose of an interest in the property or the deposit
    account; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an enforcement notice in respect of a support order
    or support provision.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notices

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any notification sent to a company with respect to a
  customer of the company, other than a document referred to in
  subsection (1) or (3), constitutes notice to the company and fixes the
  company with knowledge of its contents only if sent to and received at
  the branch of the company that is the branch of account of an account
  held in the name of that customer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notices: Minister of National Revenue

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsections (1) and (2), a notice, demand,
  order or other document issued with respect to a customer of a company
  constitutes notice to the company and fixes the company with knowledge
  of its contents and, where applicable, is binding on property
  belonging to the customer and in the possession of the company or on
  money owing to the customer by reason of an account in the company, if
  it is sent to the branch of the company referred to in subsection (1)
  or (2), an office of the company referred to in paragraph (3)(a) or
  any other office agreed to by the company and the Minister of National
  Revenue and it relates to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the administration of an Act of Parliament by the
    Minister of National Revenue; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the administration of an Act of the legislature of
    a province or legislation made by an aboriginal government, where
    the Minister or the Minister of National Revenue has entered into a
    tax collection agreement under an Act of Parliament with the
    government of the province or the aboriginal government.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (2) do not apply in respect of an
  enforcement notice in respect of a support order or support provision
  if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the enforcement notice, accompanied by a written
    statement containing the information required by the regulations, is
    served at an office of a company designated in accordance with the
    regulations in respect of a province; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the order or provision can be enforced under the
    laws of that province.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time of application

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (3) does not apply in respect of an
  enforcement notice in respect of a support order or support provision
  until the second business day following the day of service referred to
  in that subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting the designation by a company of a place,
    for the purpose of subsection (3), in any province for the service
    of enforcement notices in respect of support orders and support
    provisions;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the manner in which a company shall
    publicize the locations of designated offices of the company; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting the information that must accompany
    enforcement notices in respect of support orders and support
    provisions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply in this section.

  [[designated office]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[designated office]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a place designated
      in accordance with regulations made for the purpose of subsection
      (3). ([bureau désigné]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[enforcement notice]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[enforcement notice]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a
      support order or support provision, means a garnishee summons or
      other instrument issued under the laws of a province for the
      enforcement of the support order or support provision. ([avis
      d'exécution]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[support order]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[support order]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an order or judgment
      or interim order or judgment for family financial
      support. ([ordonnance alimentaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[support provision]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[support provision]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a provision of an
      agreement relating to the payment of maintenance or family
      financial support. ([disposition alimentaire]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 448
- 2001, c. 9, s. 549
- 2005, c. 19, s. 64
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-448-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 448"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART IX]{.HLabel1}[Investments]{.HTitleText1} {#h-457389 .Part}

### [Definitions and Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-457390 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[449]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-449 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in this Part.

  [[business growth fund]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[business growth fund]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means Canadian
      Business Growth Fund (GP) Inc., a corporation incorporated under
      the [Canada Business Corporations Act](/eng/acts/C-44). ([fonds de
      croissance des entreprises]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[closed-end fund]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[closed-end fund]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an entity whose
      activities are limited to investing the funds of the entity so as
      to provide investment diversification and professional investment
      management to the holders of its securities, and whose securities
      are

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} fixed in number and distributed to the public
        in an offering under a preliminary prospectus, prospectus,
        short-form prospectus or similar document in accordance with the
        laws of a province or a foreign jurisdiction;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} traded on an exchange or an over-the-counter
        market; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} liquidated on a fixed future termination date,
        the proceeds of which are allocated to the holders of the
        securities on a proportional basis. ([fonds d'investissement à
        capital fixe]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[commercial loan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[commercial loan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any loan made or acquired by a company, other
        than

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a loan to a natural person in an amount of
          two hundred and fifty thousand dollars or less,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a loan to the Government of Canada, the
          government of a province, a municipality, or to any agency
          thereof, or to the government of a foreign country or any
          political subdivision thereof, or any agency thereof, or to a
          prescribed international agency,

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a loan that is guaranteed by, or fully
          secured by securities issued by, a government, a municipality
          or an agency referred to in subparagraph (ii),

        - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a loan that is secured by a mortgage on real
          property, if

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the mortgage is on residential property and
            the amount of the loan, together with the amount then
            outstanding of any mortgage having an equal or prior claim
            against the property, does not exceed 80% of the value of
            the property at the time the loan is made or acquired, or

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the mortgage is on real property other than
            residential property and

            - [(I)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the loan, together with the
              amount then outstanding of any mortgage having an equal or
              prior claim against the property, does not exceed 80% of
              the value of the property at the time the loan is made or
              acquired, and

            - [(II)]{.lawlabel} at the time the loan is made or
              acquired, the property provides an annual income
              sufficient to pay all annual expenses related to the
              property, including the payments owing under the mortgage
              and the mortgages having an equal or prior claim against
              the property,

        - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a loan that is secured by a mortgage on real
          property, if

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the mortgage is on residential property and

            - [(I)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the loan, together with the
              amount then outstanding of any mortgage having an equal or
              prior claim against the property, exceeds 80% of the value
              of the property at the time the loan is made or acquired,
              and

            - [(II)]{.lawlabel} repayment of the amount of the loan that
              exceeds 80% of the value of the property is guaranteed or
              insured by a government agency or private insurer approved
              by the Superintendent,

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the mortgage is on real property other than
            residential property and

            - [(I)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the loan, together with the
              amount then outstanding of any mortgage having an equal or
              prior claim against the property, exceeds 80% of the value
              of the property at the time the loan is made or acquired,

            - [(II)]{.lawlabel} repayment of the amount of the loan that
              exceeds 80% of the value of the property is guaranteed or
              insured by a government agency or private insurer approved
              by the Superintendent, and

            - [(III)]{.lawlabel} at the time the loan is made or
              acquired, the property provides an annual income
              sufficient to pay all annual expenses related to the
              property, including the payments owing under the mortgage
              and the mortgages having an equal or prior claim against
              the property, or

          - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the loan is one referred to in paragraph
            418(2)(d),

        - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} a loan that

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} consists of a deposit made by the company
            with another financial institution,

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} is fully secured by a deposit with any
            financial institution, including the company,

          - [(C)]{.lawlabel} is fully secured by debt obligations
            guaranteed by any financial institution other than the
            company, or

          - [(D)]{.lawlabel} is fully secured by a guarantee of a
            financial institution other than the company, or

        - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} a loan to an entity controlled by the
          company;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an investment in debt obligations, other than

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} debt obligations that are

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} guaranteed by any financial institution
            other than the company,

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} fully secured by deposits with any
            financial institution, including the company, or

          - [(C)]{.lawlabel} fully secured by debt obligations that are
            guaranteed by any financial institution other than the
            company,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} debt obligations issued by the Government of
          Canada, the government of a province, a municipality, or by
          any agency thereof, or by the government of a foreign country
          or any political subdivision thereof, or by any agency
          thereof, or by a prescribed international agency,

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} debt obligations that are guaranteed by, or
          fully secured by securities issued by, a government, a
          municipality or an agency referred to in subparagraph (ii),

        - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} debt obligations that are widely
          distributed, as that expression is defined by the regulations,
          or

        - [(v)]{.lawlabel} debt obligations of an entity controlled by
          the company; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an investment in shares of a body corporate or
        ownership interests in an unincorporated entity, other than

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} shares or ownership interests that are widely
          distributed, as that expression is defined by the regulations,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} shares or ownership interests of an entity
          controlled by the company, or

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} participating shares. ([prêt
          commercial]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[factoring entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[factoring entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a factoring entity
      as defined in the regulations. ([entité s'occupant
      d'affacturage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[finance entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[finance entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a finance entity as
      defined in the regulations. ([entité s'occupant de
      financement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[financial leasing entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[financial leasing entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an entity

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the activities of which are limited to the
        financial leasing of personal property and such related
        activities as are prescribed and whose activities conform to
        such restrictions and limitations thereon as are prescribed; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that, in conducting the activities referred to
        in paragraph (a) in Canada, does not

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} direct its customers or potential customers
          to particular dealers in the leased property or the property
          to be leased,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} enter into lease agreements with persons in
          respect of any motor vehicle having a gross vehicle weight, as
          that expression is defined by the regulations, of less than
          twenty-one tonnes, or

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} enter into lease agreements with natural
          persons in respect of personal household property, as that
          expression is defined by the regulations. ([entité s'occupant
          de crédit-bail]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[loan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[loan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes an acceptance, endorsement
      or other guarantee, a deposit, a financial lease, a conditional
      sales contract, a repurchase agreement and any other similar
      arrangement for obtaining funds or credit but does not include
      investments in securities. ([prêt]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou
      [emprunt]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[motor vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[motor vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a motorized vehicle
      designed to be used primarily on a public highway for the
      transportation of persons or things, but does not include

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a fire-engine, bus, ambulance or utility truck;
        or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any other special purpose motorized vehicle
        that contains significant special features that make it suitable
        for a specific purpose. ([véhicule à moteur]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[mutual fund distribution entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[mutual fund distribution entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
      entity whose principal activity is acting as a selling agent of
      units, shares or other interests in a mutual fund and acting as a
      collecting agent in the collection of payments for any such
      interests if

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the proceeds of the sales of any such
        interests, less any sales commissions and service fees, are paid
        to the mutual fund; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the existence of a sales commission and service
        fee in respect of the sale of any such interest is disclosed to
        the purchaser of the interest before the purchase of the
        interest. ([courtier de fonds mutuels]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[mutual fund entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[mutual fund entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an entity

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} whose activities are limited to the investing
        of the funds of the entity so as to provide investment
        diversification and professional investment management to the
        holders of its securities; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} whose securities entitle their holders to
        receive, on demand, or within a specified period after demand,
        an amount computed by reference to the value of a proportionate
        interest in the whole or in a part of its net assets, including
        a separate fund or trust account of the entity. ([entité
        s'occupant de fonds mutuels]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[participating share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[participating share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a share of a
      body corporate that carries the right to participate in the
      earnings of the body corporate to an unlimited degree and to
      participate in a distribution of the remaining property of the
      body corporate on dissolution. ([action
      participante]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[permitted entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[permitted entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an entity in which
      a company is permitted to acquire a substantial investment under
      section 453. ([entité admissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[prescribed subsidiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[prescribed subsidiary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a subsidiary
      that is one of a prescribed class of subsidiaries. ([filiale
      réglementaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[specialized financing entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[specialized financing entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      specialized financing entity as defined in the
      regulations. ([entité s'occupant de financement
      spécial]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Members of a company's group

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of this Part, a member of a company's
  group is any of the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an entity referred to in any of paragraphs
    453(1)(a) to (f) that controls the company;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a subsidiary of the company or of an entity
    referred to in any of paragraphs 453(1)(a) to (f) that controls the
    company;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an entity in which the company, or an entity
    referred to in any of paragraphs 453(1)(a) to (f) that controls the
    company, has a substantial investment; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed entity in relation to the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of Part

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} This Part does not apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} money or other assets held in trust by a company,
    other than guaranteed trust money and assets held in respect
    thereof;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the holding of a security interest in real
    property, unless the security interest is prescribed pursuant to
    paragraph 467(a) to be an interest in real property; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the holding of a security interest in securities of
    an entity.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, ss. 449, 560
- 1993, c. 34, s. 128(F)
- 1997, c. 15, s. 386
- 2001, c. 9, s. 550
- 2007, c. 6, s. 369
- 2008, c. 28, s. 163
- 2018, c. 27, s. 135
:::

### [General Constraints on Investments]{.HTitleText2} {#h-457472 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Investment standards

**[[450]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-450 .sectionLabel}** The directors of a
company shall establish and the company shall adhere to investment and
lending policies, standards and procedures that a reasonable and prudent
person would apply in respect of a portfolio of investments and loans to
avoid undue risk of loss and obtain a reasonable return.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 450
- 2001, c. 9, s. 550
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limit --- business growth fund

- **[[450.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-450.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The aggregate value of all ownership
  interests in the business growth fund and the entities that the
  business growth fund controls that a company and its subsidiaries hold
  must not exceed \$200,000,000.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), the value of an
  ownership interest is determined by the amount paid for it at the time
  of its issuance.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 27, s. 136
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on control and substantial
investments

- **[[451]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-451
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) to (4.4),
  no company shall acquire control of, or hold, acquire or increase a
  substantial investment in, any entity other than a permitted entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception: indirect investments

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company may, subject to Part XI, acquire control
  of, or acquire or increase a substantial investment in, an entity
  other than a permitted entity by way of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an acquisition of control of an entity referred to
    in any of paragraphs 453(1)(a) to (j), or of a prescribed entity,
    that controls or has a substantial investment in the entity; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an acquisition of shares or ownership interests in
    the entity by

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an entity referred to in any of paragraphs
      453(1)(a) to (j), or a prescribed entity, that is controlled by
      the company, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an entity controlled by an entity referred to in
      any of paragraphs 453(1)(a) to (j), or a prescribed entity, that
      is controlled by the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception: temporary investments,
  realizations and loan workouts

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A company may, subject to Part XI, acquire control
  of, or acquire or increase a substantial investment in, an entity by
  way of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a temporary investment permitted by section 456;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an acquisition of shares of a body corporate or of
    ownership interests in an unincorporated entity permitted by section
    457; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a realization of security permitted by section 458.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception: specialized financing
  regulations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A company may, subject to Part XI, acquire control
  of, or hold, acquire or increase a substantial investment in, an
  entity other than a permitted entity if it does so in accordance with
  regulations made under paragraph 452(d) concerning specialized
  financing.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Business growth fund

  [(4.1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 450.1, subsections (4.2) to
  (4.4) and Part XI, a company may hold, acquire or increase a
  substantial investment in the business growth fund or any entity that
  the business growth fund controls.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}For greater certainty

  [(4.2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, a company is prohibited from
  acquiring control of the business growth fund or any entity that the
  business growth fund controls.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- entity

  [(4.3)]{.lawlabel} A company is prohibited from holding or acquiring a
  substantial investment in the business growth fund or any entity that
  the business growth fund controls if the business growth fund or any
  entity that the business growth fund controls holds or acquires shares
  of, or other ownership interests in, any of the following entities, or
  in any entity that controls any of the following entities:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an entity referred to in any of paragraphs
    453(1)(a) to (j);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an entity that is primarily engaged in the leasing
    of motor vehicles in Canada for the purpose of extending credit to a
    customer or financing a customer's acquisition of a motor vehicle;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an entity that is primarily engaged in providing
    temporary possession of personal property, including motor vehicles,
    to customers in Canada for a purpose other than to finance the
    customer's acquisition of the property;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an entity that acts as an insurance broker or agent
    in Canada; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} an entity that is engaged in any prescribed
    activity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- capital and loans

  [(4.4)]{.lawlabel} A company is prohibited from holding or acquiring a
  substantial investment in the business growth fund or any entity that
  the business growth fund controls if the business growth fund or any
  entity that the business growth fund controls holds shares of, or
  other ownership interests in, an entity or holds a loan made to an
  entity and, in respect of that entity and its affiliates, the
  aggregate value of the following exceeds \$100,000,000:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all ownership interests that are held by the
    company, the company's subsidiaries, the business growth fund or the
    entities that the business growth fund controls, the value of those
    ownership interests as determined by the amount paid for them at the
    time each was first acquired by any of those entities; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the outstanding principal of all loans held by the
    business growth fund or the entities that the business growth fund
    controls.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception: uncontrolled event

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A company is deemed not to contravene subsection (1)
  if the company acquires control of, or acquires or increases a
  substantial investment in, an entity solely as the result of an event
  not within the control of the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of subsection (2)

  [(5.1)]{.lawlabel} No company shall, under subsection (2), acquire
  control of, or acquire or increase a substantial investment in, an
  entity referred to in paragraph 453(1)(j).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Holding

  [(5.2)]{.lawlabel} If a company holds a substantial investment in an
  entity referred to in paragraph 453(1)(j) that it acquired or
  increased under subsection (2) before the coming into force of
  subsection (5.1), the company may continue to hold that substantial
  investment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of other provision

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Despite having acquired control of, or a substantial
  investment in, an entity under a particular provision of this Part, a
  company may continue to control the entity or hold the substantial
  investment in the entity as though it had made the acquisition under
  another provision of this Part so long as the conditions of that other
  provision are met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Timing of deemed acquisition

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If a company decides to exercise its right under
  subsection (6), the company is deemed to be acquiring the control or
  the substantial investment under the other provision.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 451
- 1997, c. 15, s. 387
- 2001, c. 9, s. 550
- 2007, c. 6, s. 370
- 2013, c. 40, s. 167
- 2018, c. 27, s. 137
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-451-20131212.html "Link to previous version of section 451"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[452]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-452 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may make regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting the determination of the amount or value
  of loans, investments and interests for the purposes of this Part;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting the loans and investments, and the maximum
  aggregate amount of all loans and investments, that may be made or
  acquired by a company and its prescribed subsidiaries to or in a
  person and any persons connected with that person;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} specifying the classes of persons who are connected
  with any person for the purposes of paragraph (b); and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} concerning specialized financing for the purposes of
  subsection 451(4).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 452
- 2001, c. 9, s. 550
:::

### [Subsidiaries and Equity Investments]{.HTitleText2} {#h-457535 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permitted investments

- **[[453]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-453
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (4) to (6)
  and Part XI, a company may acquire control of, or acquire or increase
  a substantial investment in

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a company;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a bank;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a bank holding company;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an association to which the [Cooperative Credit
    Associations Act](/eng/acts/C-41.01) applies;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} an insurance company or a fraternal benefit society
    incorporated or formed under the [Insurance Companies
    Act](/eng/acts/I-11.8);

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} an insurance holding company;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a trust, loan or insurance corporation incorporated
    or formed by or under an Act of the legislature of a province;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a cooperative credit society incorporated or
    formed, and regulated, by or under an Act of the legislature of a
    province;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an entity that is incorporated or formed by or
    under an Act of Parliament or of the legislature of a province and
    that is primarily engaged in dealing in securities; or

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} an entity that is incorporated or formed, and
    regulated, otherwise than by or under an Act of Parliament or of the
    legislature of a province and that is primarily engaged outside
    Canada in a business that, if carried on in Canada, would be the
    business of banking, the business of a cooperative credit society,
    the business of insurance, the business of providing fiduciary
    services or the business of dealing in securities.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Permitted investments

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (3) to (6) and Part XI, a
  company may acquire control of, or acquire or increase a substantial
  investment in, an entity, other than an entity referred to in any of
  paragraphs (1)(a) to (j), whose business is limited to one or more of
  the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} engaging in any financial service activity or in
    any other activity that a company is permitted to engage in under
    any of paragraphs 409(2)(b) to (d) or section 410 or 411;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} acquiring or holding shares of, or ownership
    interests in, entities in which a company is permitted under this
    Part to hold or acquire;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} engaging in the provision of any services
    exclusively to any or all of the following, so long as the entity is
    providing those services to the company or any member of the
    company's group:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the company,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any member of the company's group,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} any entity that is primarily engaged in the
      business of providing financial services,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} any permitted entity in which an entity referred
      to in subparagraph (iii) has a substantial investment, or

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} any prescribed person, if it is doing so under
      prescribed terms and conditions, if any are prescribed;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} engaging in any activity that a company is
    permitted to engage in, other than an activity referred to in
    paragraph (a) or (e), that relates to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the promotion, sale, delivery or distribution of
      a financial product or financial service that is provided by the
      company or any member of the company's group, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if a significant portion of the business of the
      entity involves an activity referred to in subparagraph (i), the
      promotion, sale, delivery or distribution of a financial product
      or financial service that is provided by any other entity that is
      primarily engaged in the business of providing financial services;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} engaging in the activities referred to in the
    definition [[closed-end fund]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[mutual fund
    distribution entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or [[mutual fund
    entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection 449(1); and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} engaging in prescribed activities, under prescribed
    terms and conditions, if any are prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A company may not acquire control of, or acquire or
  increase a substantial investment in, an entity whose business
  includes any activity referred to in any of paragraphs (2)(a) to (e)
  if the entity engages in the business of accepting deposit liabilities
  or if the activities of the entity include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} activities that a company is not permitted to
    engage in under any of sections 417 and 418;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} dealing in securities, except as may be permitted
    under paragraph (2)(e) or as may be permitted to a company under
    paragraph 409(2)(c);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} acting as an executor, administrator or official
    guardian or as a guardian, tutor, curator, judicial adviser or
    committee of a mentally incompetent person;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} being a trustee for a trust;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} activities that a company is not permitted to
    engage in under any regulation made under section 416 if the entity
    engages in the activities of a finance entity or of any other entity
    as may be prescribed;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} acquiring control of or acquiring or holding a
    substantial investment in another entity unless

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an entity that is controlled by
      the company, the company itself would be permitted under this Part
      to acquire a substantial investment in the other entity, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an entity that is not controlled
      by the company, the company itself would be permitted to acquire a
      substantial investment in the other entity under subsection (1)
      or (2) or 451(2), paragraph 451(3)(b) or (c) or subsection 451(4);
      or

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} any prescribed activity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (3)(d), a company may acquire
  control of, or acquire or increase a substantial investment in, any
  entity that acts as a trustee for a trust if the entity has been
  permitted under the laws of a province to act as a trustee for a trust
  and the following conditions are satisfied:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the entity acts as a trustee only with respect to a
    closed-end fund or mutual fund entity; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the entity engages in other business, that
    business is limited to engaging in one or more of the following:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the activities of a mutual fund distribution
      entity,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any activity that a company is permitted to
      engage in under paragraph 410(1)(d.1), and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the provision of investment counselling
      services and portfolio management services.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Control

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (8) and the regulations, a
  company may not acquire control of, or acquire or increase a
  substantial investment in,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an entity referred to in any of paragraphs (1)(a)
    to (j), unless

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the company controls, within the meaning of
      paragraph 3(1)(d), the entity, or would thereby acquire control,
      within the meaning of that paragraph, of the entity, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the company is permitted by regulations made
      under paragraph 459(a) to acquire or increase the substantial
      investment;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an entity whose business includes one or more of
    the activities referred to in paragraph (2)(a) and that engages, as
    part of its business, in any financial intermediary activity that
    exposes the entity to material market or credit risk, including a
    finance entity, a factoring entity and a financial leasing entity,
    unless

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the company controls, within the meaning of
      paragraph 3(1)(d), the entity, or would thereby acquire control,
      within the meaning of that paragraph, of the entity, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the company is permitted by regulations made
      under paragraph 459(a) to acquire or increase the substantial
      investment; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an entity whose business includes an activity
    referred to in paragraph (2)(b), including a specialized financing
    entity, unless

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the company controls, within the meaning of
      paragraph 3(1)(d), the entity, or would thereby acquire control,
      within the meaning of that paragraph, of the entity,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the company is permitted by regulations made
      under paragraph 459(a) to acquire or increase the substantial
      investment, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} subject to prescribed terms and conditions, if
      any are prescribed, the activities of the entity do not include
      the acquisition or holding of control of, or the acquisition or
      holding of shares or other ownership interests in, an entity
      referred to in paragraph (a) or (b) or an entity that is not a
      permitted entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister's approval

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, a company may not,
  without the prior written approval of the Minister,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} acquire control of an entity referred to in
    paragraphs (1)(g) to (i) from a person who is not a member of the
    company's group;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} acquire control of an entity referred to in
    paragraph (1)(j) or (4)(b), other than an entity whose activities
    are limited to the activities of one or more of the following
    entities, if the control is acquired from an entity referred to in
    any of paragraphs (1)(a) to (f) that is not a member of the
    company's group:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a factoring entity, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a financial leasing entity;

  - [(b.1)]{.lawlabel} acquire control of an entity referred to in
    paragraph (1)(j) if the company is a company with equity of two
    billion dollars or more and

    ::: {#457601 .Paragraph}
    A + B \> C

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the value of the entity's consolidated assets, as it would
        have been reported in the entity's annual financial statements
        if those statements had been prepared immediately before the
        acquisition,

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is the aggregate of the values of the consolidated assets of all
        other entities referred to in paragraph (1)(j) that the company
        has acquired control of within the preceding 12 months, as the
        value for each entity would have been reported in its annual
        financial statements if those statements had been prepared
        immediately before the acquisition of control of that entity,
        and

    [C]{.dfn}
    :   is 10% of the value of the company's consolidated assets, as
        shown in the company's last annual statement that was prepared
        before its first acquisition of control of an entity referred to
        in paragraph (1)(j) within the preceding 12 months;
    :::

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} acquire control of, or acquire or increase a
    substantial investment in, an entity whose business includes one or
    more of the activities referred to in paragraph (2)(d);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} acquire control of, or acquire or increase a
    substantial investment in, an entity that engages in Canada in an
    activity described in paragraph 410(1)(c);

  - [(d.1)]{.lawlabel} acquire control of, or acquire or increase a
    substantial investment in, an entity that engages in an activity
    described in paragraph 410(1)(c.1); or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} acquire control of, or acquire or increase a
    substantial investment in, an entity engaging in an activity
    prescribed for the purposes of paragraph (2)(f).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Matters for consideration

  [(5.1)]{.lawlabel} In addition to any matters or conditions provided
  for in this Act that are relevant to the granting of an approval, the
  Minister may, in considering whether to grant the approval under
  paragraph (5)(b.1), take into account all matters that he or she
  considers relevant in the circumstances, including

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the stability of the financial system in Canada;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the best interests of the financial system in
    Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Superintendent's approval

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (7) and the regulations, a
  company may not acquire control of, or acquire or increase a
  substantial investment in, an entity referred to in any of paragraphs
  (1)(g) to (j) and (4)(b) and (c) unless the company obtains the
  approval of the Superintendent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (6) does not apply in respect of a
  particular transaction if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the company is acquiring control of an entity,
    other than a specialized financing entity, and the only reason for
    which the company would, but for this subsection, require approval
    for the acquisition is that the entity carries on activities
    referred to in paragraph (2)(b);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the company is acquiring control of an entity whose
    activities are limited to the activities of a factoring entity or a
    financial leasing entity;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has approved the transaction under
    subsection (5) or is deemed to have approved it under subsection
    454(1);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (7.1), the company is
    acquiring control of an entity (referred to in this paragraph as the
    "target entity") referred to in paragraph (4)(b) or (c) and

    ::: {#457622 .Paragraph}
    A/B \< C

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the aggregate of the values, as they would have been reported
        in the company's annual financial statements if those statements
        were prepared on the day of the acquisition of control of the
        target entity, of
        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the target entity's consolidated assets,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the assets of the company and of any
          subsidiary of the company that were acquired, at any time
          within the 12 months preceding the acquisition of control of
          the target entity, from any entity that, at that time, held
          any of the assets referred to in subparagraph (i), and

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the consolidated assets of any entity
          referred to in paragraph (4)(b) or (c) the control of which is
          acquired by the company at the same time as the acquisition of
          control of the target entity --- or within the 12 months
          preceding the acquisition of control of the target entity if,
          at any time within those 12 months, that entity and the target
          entity were affiliates --- excluding any assets referred to in
          subparagraph (i) or (ii) and the consolidated assets of an
          entity in respect of which no approval of the Superintendent
          is required under any of paragraphs (a) to (c),

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is the value of the company's consolidated assets, as shown in
        its last annual statement prepared before the acquisition of
        control of the target entity, and

    [C]{.dfn}
    :   is
        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} 0.01, in the case of a company with equity of
          12 billion dollars or more, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} 0.02, in the case of any other company; or
    :::

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the company is acquiring or increasing a
    substantial investment in an entity (referred to in this paragraph
    as the "target entity") without acquiring control of it, and

    ::: {#457633 .Paragraph}
    A/B \< C

    where

    [A]{.dfn}
    :   is the aggregate of the values, as they would have been reported
        in the company's annual financial statements if those statements
        were prepared on the day of the acquisition or increase of the
        substantial investment in the target entity, of
        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the shares of, or other ownership interests
          in, the target entity that the company or a subsidiary of the
          company is acquiring in the transaction that results in the
          acquisition or increase of a substantial investment in the
          target entity, and the shares of, or other ownership interests
          in, the target entity that are held by an entity the control
          of which the company is acquiring in the transaction that
          results in the acquisition or increase of a substantial
          investment in the target entity,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the shares of, or other ownership interests
          in, the target entity that are held by the company or a
          subsidiary of the company and that were acquired by the
          company or the subsidiary within the 12 months preceding the
          transaction referred to in subparagraph (i), and

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the shares of, or other ownership interests
          in, the target entity that are held by a subsidiary of the
          company the control of which was acquired by the company
          within the 12 months preceding the transaction referred to in
          subparagraph (i), excluding any shares or other ownership
          interests referred to in subparagraph (ii),

    [B]{.dfn}
    :   is the value of the company's consolidated assets, as shown in
        its last annual statement prepared before the transaction that
        results in the acquisition or increase of the substantial
        investment in the target entity, and

    [C]{.dfn}
    :   is
