        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} 0.005, in the case of a company with equity
          of 12 billion dollars or more, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} 0.01, in the case of any other company.
    :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No exception for deemed acquisition

  [(7.1)]{.lawlabel} The exception in paragraph (7)(d) does not apply
  with respect to a deemed acquisition of control under subsection
  451(7).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Control not required

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} A company need not control an entity referred to in
  paragraph (1)(j), or an entity that is incorporated or formed
  otherwise than by or under an Act of Parliament or of the legislature
  of a province, if the laws or customary business practices of the
  country under the laws of which the entity was incorporated or formed
  do not permit the company to control the entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition on giving up control in
  fact

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} A company that, under subsection (4), controls an
  entity may not, without the prior written approval of the Minister,
  give up control, within the meaning of paragraph 3(1)(d), of the
  entity while it continues to control the entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Giving up control

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} A company that, under subsection (4), controls an
  entity may, with the prior written approval of the Superintendent,
  give up control of the entity while keeping a substantial investment
  in the entity if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the company is permitted to do so by regulations
    made under paragraph 459(c); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the entity meets the conditions referred to in
    subparagraph (4)(c)(iii).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsections do not apply

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} If a company controls, within the meaning of
  paragraph 3(1)(a), (b) or (c), an entity, subsections (5) and (6) do
  not apply in respect of any subsequent increases by the company of its
  substantial investment in the entity so long as the company continues
  to control the entity.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 453
- 1997, c. 15, s. 388
- 1999, c. 28, s. 141
- 2001, c. 9, s. 550
- 2007, c. 6, s. 371
- 2012, c. 5, s. 179
- 2018, c. 27, s. 130
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-453-20120524.html "Link to previous version of section 453"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval for indirect investments

- **[[454]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-454 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  company obtains the approval of the Minister under subsection 453(5)
  to acquire control of, or to acquire or increase a substantial
  investment in, an entity and, through that acquisition or increase,
  the company indirectly acquires control of, or acquires or increases a
  substantial investment in, another entity that would require the
  approval of the Minister under subsection 453(5) or the Superintendent
  under subsection 453(6) and that indirect acquisition or increase is
  disclosed to the Minister in writing before the approval is obtained,
  the company is deemed to have obtained the approval of the Minister or
  the Superintendent for that indirect acquisition or increase.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval for indirect investments

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a company obtains the approval of the
  Superintendent under subsection 453(6) to acquire control of, or to
  acquire or increase a substantial investment in, an entity and,
  through that acquisition or increase the company indirectly acquires
  control of, or acquires or increases a substantial investment in,
  another entity that would require the approval of the Superintendent
  under that subsection and that indirect acquisition or increase is
  disclosed to the Superintendent in writing before the approval is
  obtained, the company is deemed to have obtained the approval of the
  Superintendent for that indirect acquisition or increase.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 454
- 2001, c. 9, s. 550
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Undertakings

- **[[455]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-455 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  company controls a permitted entity, other than an entity referred to
  in any of paragraphs 453(1)(a) to (f), the company shall provide the
  Superintendent with any undertakings that the Superintendent may
  require regarding

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the activities of the entity; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} access to information about the entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Undertakings

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a company acquires control of an entity referred
  to in any of paragraphs 453(1)(g) to (j), the company shall provide
  the Superintendent with any undertakings concerning the entity that
  the Superintendent may require.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agreements with other jurisdictions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent may enter into an agreement with
  the appropriate official or public body responsible for the
  supervision of an entity referred to in any of paragraphs 453(1)(g)
  to (j) in each province or in any other jurisdiction concerning any
  matters referred to in paragraphs (1)(a) and (b) or any other matter
  the Superintendent considers appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access to records

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite any other provision of this Part, a company
  shall not control a permitted entity, other than an entity referred to
  in any of paragraphs 453(1)(a) to (f), unless, in the course of the
  acquisition of control or within a reasonable time after the control
  is acquired, the company obtains from the permitted entity an
  undertaking to provide the Superintendent with reasonable access to
  the records of the permitted entity.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 455
- 2001, c. 9, s. 550
:::

### [Exceptions and Exclusions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-457677 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Temporary investments in entity

- **[[456]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-456
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), a company
  may, by way of a temporary investment, acquire control of, or acquire
  or increase a substantial investment in, an entity but, within two
  years, or any other period that may be specified or approved by the
  Superintendent, after acquiring control or after acquiring or
  increasing the substantial investment, as the case may be, it shall do
  all things necessary to ensure that it no longer controls the entity
  or has a substantial investment in the entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), if a company that was in
  existence immediately before June 1, 1992 had an investment in an
  entity on September 27, 1990 that is a substantial investment within
  the meaning of section 10 and the company subsequently increases that
  substantial investment by way of a temporary investment, the company
  shall, within two years, or any other period that is specified or
  approved by the Superintendent, after increasing the substantial
  investment, do all things necessary to ensure that its substantial
  investment in the entity is no greater than it was on September 27,
  1990.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent may, in the case of any particular
  company that makes an application under this subsection, extend the
  period of two years, or the other period specified or approved by the
  Superintendent, that is referred to in subsection (1) or (2) for any
  further period or periods, and on any terms and conditions, that the
  Superintendent considers necessary.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Temporary investment

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a company, by way of temporary investment,
  acquires control of, or acquires or increases a substantial investment
  in, an entity for which the approval of the Minister would have been
  required under subsection 453(5) if the company had acquired the
  control, or acquired or increased the substantial investment, under
  section 453, the company must, within 90 days after acquiring control
  or after acquiring or increasing the substantial investment,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} apply to the Minister for approval to retain
    control of the entity or to continue to hold the substantial
    investment in the entity for a period specified by the Minister or
    for an indeterminate period on any terms and conditions that the
    Minister considers appropriate; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} do all things necessary to ensure that, on the
    expiry of the 90 days, it no longer controls the entity or does not
    have a substantial investment in the entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Indeterminate extension

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a company, by way of temporary investment,
  acquires control of, or acquires or increases a substantial investment
  in, an entity for which the approval of the Superintendent would have
  been required under subsection 453(6) if the company had acquired the
  control, or acquired or increased the substantial investment, under
  section 453, the Superintendent may, on application, permit the
  company to retain control of the entity or to continue to hold the
  substantial investment in the entity for an indeterminate period, on
  any terms and conditions that the Superintendent considers
  appropriate.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 456
- 2001, c. 9, s. 550
- 2007, c. 6, s. 372
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-456-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 456"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Loan workouts

- **[[457]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-457
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite anything in this Part, if a
  company or any of its subsidiaries has made a loan to an entity and,
  under the terms of the agreement between the company, or any of its
  subsidiaries, and the entity with respect to the loan and any other
  documents governing the terms of the loan, a default has occurred, the
  company may acquire

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the entity is a body corporate, all or any of
    the shares of the body corporate;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the entity is an unincorporated entity, all or
    any of the ownership interests in the entity;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} all or any of the shares or all or any of the
    ownership interests in any entity that is an affiliate of the
    entity;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} all or any of the shares of a body corporate that
    is primarily engaged in holding shares of, ownership interests in or
    assets acquired from the entity or any of its affiliates; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} all or any of the ownership interests in any entity
    that is primarily engaged in holding shares of, ownership interests
    in or assets acquired from the entity or any of its affiliates.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation of company

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a company acquires shares or ownership interests
  in an entity under subsection (1), the company shall, within five
  years after acquiring them do all things necessary to ensure that the
  company does not control the entity or have a substantial investment
  in the entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), if a company that was in
  existence immediately before June 1, 1992 had an investment in an
  entity on September 27, 1990 that is a substantial investment within
  the meaning of section 10 and the company later increases that
  substantial investment by way of an investment made under subsection
  (1), the company shall, within five years after increasing the
  substantial investment, do all things necessary to ensure that its
  substantial investment in the entity is no greater than it was on
  September 27, 1990.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent may, in the case of any particular
  company that makes an application under this subsection, extend the
  period of five years referred to in subsection (2) or (3) for any
  further period or periods, and on any terms and conditions, that the
  Superintendent considers necessary.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- entities controlled by
  foreign governments

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Despite anything in this Part, if a company has made
  a loan to, or holds a debt obligation of, the government of a foreign
  country or an entity controlled by the government of a foreign country
  and, under the terms of the agreement between the company and that
  government or the entity, as the case may be, and any other documents
  governing the terms of the loan or debt obligation, a default has
  occurred, the company may acquire all or any of the shares of, or
  ownership interests in, that entity or in any other entity designated
  by that government, if the acquisition is part of a debt restructuring
  program of that government.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time for holding shares

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a company acquires any shares or ownership
  interests under subsection (5), the company may, on any terms and
  conditions that the Superintendent considers appropriate, hold those
  shares or ownership interests for an indeterminate period or for any
  other period that the Superintendent may specify.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If, under subsection (1), a company acquires control
  of, or acquires or increases a substantial investment in, an entity
  that it would otherwise be permitted to acquire or increase under
  section 453, the company may retain control of the entity or continue
  to hold the substantial investment for an indeterminate period if the
  approval in writing of the Minister is obtained before the end of the
  period referred to in subsection (2) or (3), including any extension
  of it granted under subsection (4).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 457
- 1997, c. 15, s. 389
- 2001, c. 9, s. 550
- 2007, c. 6, s. 373
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-457-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 457"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Realizations

- **[[458]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-458
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite anything in this Act, a
  company may acquire

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an investment in a body corporate,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an interest in an unincorporated entity, or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an interest in real property,

  if the investment or interest is acquired through the realization of a
  security interest held by the company or any of its subsidiaries.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disposition

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection 76(2), if a company acquires
  control of, or acquires a substantial investment in, an entity by way
  of the realization of a security interest held by the company or any
  of its subsidiaries, the company shall, within five years after the
  day on which control or the substantial investment is acquired, do all
  things necessary, or cause its subsidiary to do all things necessary,
  as the case may be, to ensure that the company no longer controls the
  entity or has a substantial investment in the entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2), if a company that was in
  existence immediately before June 1, 1992 had an investment in an
  entity on September 27, 1990 that is a substantial investment within
  the meaning of section 10 and the company later increases that
  substantial investment by way of a realization of a security interest
  under subsection (1), the company shall, within five years after
  increasing the substantial investment, do all things necessary to
  ensure that its substantial investment in the entity is no greater
  than it was on September 27, 1990.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent may, in the case of any particular
  company that makes an application under this subsection, extend the
  period of five years referred to in subsection (2) or (3) for any
  further period or periods, and on any terms and conditions, that the
  Superintendent considers necessary.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If, under subsection (1), a company acquires control
  of, or acquires or increases a substantial investment in, an entity
  that it would otherwise be permitted to acquire or increase under
  section 453, the company may retain control of the entity or continue
  to hold the substantial investment for an indeterminate period if the
  approval in writing of the Minister is obtained before the end of the
  period referred to in subsection (2) or (3), including any extension
  of it granted under subsection (4).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 458
- 1997, c. 15, s. 390
- 2001, c. 9, s. 550
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations restricting ownership

**[[459]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-459 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in Council
may make regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of subsection 453(4), permitting the
  acquisition of control or the acquisition or increase of substantial
  investments, or prescribing the circumstances under which that
  subsection does not apply or the companies or other entities in
  respect of which that subsection does not apply, including prescribing
  companies or other entities on the basis of the activities they engage
  in;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of subsection 453(5) or (6),
  permitting the acquisition of control or the acquisition or increase
  of substantial investments, or prescribing the circumstances under
  which either of those subsections does not apply or the companies or
  other entities in respect of which either of those subsections does
  not apply, including prescribing companies or other entities on the
  basis of the activities they engage in;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of subsection 453(10), permitting a
  company to give up control of an entity; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} restricting the ownership by a company of shares of a
  body corporate or of ownership interests in an unincorporated entity
  under sections 453 to 458 and imposing terms and conditions applicable
  to companies that own such shares or interests.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 459
- 1997, c. 15, s. 391
- 2001, c. 9, s. 550
:::

### [Portfolio Limits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-457740 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exclusion from portfolio limits

- **[[460]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-460
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), the value
  of all loans, investments and interests acquired by a company and any
  of its prescribed subsidiaries under section 457 or as a result of a
  realization of a security interest is not to be included in
  calculating the value of loans, investments and interests of the
  company and its prescribed subsidiaries under sections 461 to 466

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for a period of twelve years following the day on
    which the interest was acquired, in the case of an interest in real
    property; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for a period of five years after the day on which
    the loan, investment or interest was acquired, in the case of a
    loan, investment or interest, other than an interest in real
    property.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent may, in the case of any particular
  company, extend any period referred to in subsection (1) for any
  further period or periods, and on any terms and conditions, that the
  Superintendent considers necessary.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to an investment or
  interest described in that subsection if the investment or interest is
  defined by a regulation made under section 467 to be an interest in
  real property and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the company or the subsidiary acquired the
    investment or interest as a result of the realization of a security
    interest securing a loan that was defined by a regulation made under
    section 467 to be an interest in real property; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the company or the subsidiary acquired the
    investment or interest under section 457 as a result of a default
    referred to in that section in respect of a loan that was defined by
    a regulation made under section 467 to be an interest in real
    property.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 460
- 1997, c. 15, s. 392
- 2001, c. 9, s. 550
:::

### [Commercial Loans]{.HTitleText2} {#h-457754 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Lending limit: companies with regulatory
capital of \$25 million or less

**[[461]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-461 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section 462,
a company that has twenty-five million dollars or less of regulatory
capital shall not, and shall not permit its prescribed subsidiaries to,
make or acquire a commercial loan or acquire control of a permitted
entity that holds commercial loans if the aggregate value of all
commercial loans held by the company and its prescribed subsidiaries
exceeds, or the making or acquisition of the commercial loan or
acquisition of control of the entity would cause the aggregate value of
all commercial loans held by the company and its prescribed subsidiaries
to exceed, 5 per cent of the total assets of the company.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 461
- 1999, c. 28, s. 142
- 2001, c. 9, s. 550
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Lending limit: regulatory capital over
\$25 million

**[[462]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-462 .sectionLabel}** A company that has
twenty-five million dollars or less of regulatory capital that is
controlled by a financial institution that has the equivalent of more
than twenty-five million dollars of regulatory capital or a company that
has more than twenty-five million dollars of regulatory capital may make
or acquire commercial loans or acquire control of a permitted entity
that holds commercial loans if the aggregate value of all commercial
loans held by the company and its prescribed subsidiaries would thereby
exceed the limit set out in section 461 only with the prior approval in
writing of the Superintendent and in accordance with any terms and
conditions that the Superintendent may specify.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 462
- 1999, c. 28, s. 143
- 2001, c. 9, s. 550
:::

Meaning of [[total assets]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

**[[463]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-463 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
sections 461 and 462, [[total assets]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect
of a company, has the meaning given to that expression by the
regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 463
- 2001, c. 9, s. 550
:::

### [Real Property]{.HTitleText2} {#h-457767 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limit on total property interest

**[[464]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-464 .sectionLabel}** A company shall not,
and shall not permit its prescribed subsidiaries to, purchase or
otherwise acquire an interest in real property or make an improvement to
any real property in which the company or any of its prescribed
subsidiaries has an interest if the aggregate value of all interests of
the company in real property exceeds, or the acquisition of the interest
or the making of the improvement would cause that aggregate value to
exceed the prescribed percentage of the regulatory capital of the
company.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 464
- 2001, c. 9, s. 550
:::

### [Equities]{.HTitleText2} {#h-457772 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limits on equity acquisitions

**[[465]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-465 .sectionLabel}** A company shall not,
and shall not permit its prescribed subsidiaries to,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} purchase or otherwise acquire any participating
  shares of any body corporate or any ownership interests in any
  unincorporated entity, other than those of a permitted entity in which
  the company has, or by virtue of the acquisition would have, a
  substantial investment, or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} acquire control of an entity that holds shares or
  ownership interests referred to in paragraph (a),

if the aggregate value of

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} all participating shares, excluding participating
  shares of permitted entities in which the company has a substantial
  investment, and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} all ownership interests in unincorporated entities,
  other than ownership interests in permitted entities in which the
  company has a substantial investment,

beneficially owned by the company and its prescribed subsidiaries
exceeds, or the purchase or acquisition would cause that aggregate value
to exceed, the prescribed percentage of the regulatory capital of the
company.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 465
- 2001, c. 9, s. 550
:::

### [Aggregate Limit]{.HTitleText2} {#h-457783 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Aggregate limit

**[[466]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-466 .sectionLabel}** A company shall not,
and shall not permit its prescribed subsidiaries to,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} purchase or otherwise acquire

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} participating shares of a body corporate, other
    than those of a permitted entity in which the company has, or by
    virtue of the acquisition would have, a substantial investment,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} ownership interests in an unincorporated entity,
    other than ownership interests in a permitted entity in which the
    company has, or by virtue of the acquisition would have, a
    substantial investment, or

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} interests in real property, or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} make an improvement to real property in which the
  company or any of its prescribed subsidiaries has an interest

if the aggregate value of

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} all participating shares and ownership interests
  referred to in subparagraphs (a)(i) and (ii) that are beneficially
  owned by the company and its prescribed subsidiaries,

and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} all interests of the company in real property
  referred to in subparagraph (a)(iii)

exceeds, or the acquisition or the making of the improvement would cause
that aggregate value to exceed, the prescribed percentage of the
regulatory capital of the company.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 466
- 1997, c. 15, s. 393
- 2001, c. 9, s. 550
:::

### [Miscellaneous]{.HTitleText2} {#h-457798 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[467]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-467 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
this Part, the Governor in Council may make regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} defining the interests of a company in real property;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} determining the method of valuing those interests; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} exempting classes of companies from the application
  of sections 464, 465 and 466.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 467
- 1997, c. 15, s. 394
- 2001, c. 9, s. 550
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Divestment order

- **[[468]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-468 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Superintendent may, by order, direct a company to dispose of, within
  any period that the Superintendent considers reasonable, any loan,
  investment or interest made or acquired in contravention of this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Divestment order

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If, in the opinion of the Superintendent,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an investment by a company or any entity it
    controls in shares of a body corporate or in ownership interests in
    an unincorporated entity enables the company to control the body
    corporate or the unincorporated entity, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the company or any entity it controls has entered
    into an arrangement whereby it or its nominee may veto any proposal
    put before

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the board of directors of a body corporate, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a similar group or committee of an
      unincorporated entity,

    or whereby no proposal may be approved except with the consent of
    the company, the entity it controls or the nominee,

  the Superintendent may, by order, require the company, within any
  period that the Superintendent considers reasonable, to do all things
  necessary to ensure that the company no longer controls the body
  corporate or unincorporated entity or has the ability to veto or
  otherwise defeat any proposal referred to in paragraph (b).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Divestment order

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a company

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} fails to provide or obtain within a reasonable
      time the undertakings referred to in subsection 455(1), (2) or
      (4), or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is in default of an undertaking referred to in
      subsection 455(1) or (2) and the default is not remedied within
      ninety days after the day of receipt by the company of a notice
      from the Superintendent of the default, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a permitted entity referred to in subsection 455(4)
    is in default of an undertaking referred to in that subsection and
    the default is not remedied within ninety days after the day of
    receipt by the company of a notice from the Superintendent of the
    default,

  the Superintendent may, by order, require the company, within any
  period that the Superintendent considers reasonable, to do all things
  necessary to ensure that the company no longer has a substantial
  investment in the entity to which the undertaking relates.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) does not apply in respect of an entity
  in which a company has a substantial investment permitted by this
  Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 468
- 2001, c. 9, s. 550
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed temporary investment

**[[469]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-469 .sectionLabel}** If a company controls
or has a substantial investment in an entity as permitted by this Part
and the company becomes aware of a change in the business or affairs of
the entity that, if the change had taken place before the acquisition of
control or of the substantial investment, would have caused the entity
not to be a permitted entity or would have been such that approval for
the acquisition would have been required under subsection 453(5) or (6),
the company is deemed to have acquired, on the day the company becomes
aware of the change, a temporary investment in respect of which section
456 applies.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 469
- 1997, c. 15, s. 395
- 2001, c. 9, s. 550
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Asset transactions

- **[[470]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-470 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company shall not, and shall not permit its subsidiaries to, without
  the approval of the Superintendent, acquire assets from a person or
  transfer assets to a person if

  ::: {#457835 .Subsection}
  A + B \> C

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the value of the assets;

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the total value of all assets that the company and its
      subsidiaries acquired from or transferred to that person in the
      twelve months ending immediately before the acquisition or
      transfer; and

  [C]{.dfn}
  :   is ten per cent of the total value of the assets of the company,
      as shown in the last annual statement of the company prepared
      before the acquisition or transfer.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval of series of transactions

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent may, for the purposes of
  subsection (1), approve a transaction or series of transactions
  relating to the acquisition or transfer of assets that may be entered
  into with a person, or with persons of any class of persons,
  regardless of whether those persons are known at the time of the
  granting of the approval or not.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an asset that is a debt obligation referred to in
    subparagraphs (b)(i) to (v) of the definition [[commercial
    loan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection 449(1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} assets acquired or transferred under a transaction
    or series of transactions by the company with another financial
    institution as a result of the company's participation in one or
    more syndicated loans with that financial institution;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} assets purchased or sold under a sale agreement
    that is approved by the Minister under section 241;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} shares of, or ownership interests in, an entity for
    which the approval of the Minister under Part VII or subsection
    453(5) is required or the approval of the Superintendent under
    subsection 453(6) is required;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} assets that are acquired or transferred under a
    transaction that has been approved by the Minister under subsection
    678(1) of the [Bank Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01) or subsection 715(1) of
    the [Insurance Companies Act](/eng/acts/I-11.8);

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} assets, other than real property, acquired or
    disposed of under an arrangement that has been approved by the
    Superintendent under subsection 482(3); or

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} assets acquired or disposed of with the approval of
    the Superintendent under subsection 482(4).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2007, c. 6, s. 374\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Value of assets

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of "A" in subsection (1), the value
  of the assets is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of assets that are acquired, the
    purchase price of the assets or, if the assets are shares of, or
    ownership interests in, an entity the assets of which will be
    included in the annual statement of the company after the
    acquisition, the fair market value of the assets; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of assets that are transferred, the
    value of the assets as reported in the last annual statement of the
    company prepared before the transfer or, if the value of the assets
    is not reported in that annual statement, the value of the assets as
    it would be reported in the annual statement of the company if the
    annual statement had been prepared, in accordance with the
    accounting principles referred to in subsection 313(4), immediately
    before the transfer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Total value of all assets

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), the total value
  of all assets that the company or any of its subsidiaries has acquired
  during the period of twelve months referred to in subsection (1) is
  the purchase price of the assets or, if the assets are shares of, or
  ownership interests in, an entity the assets of which immediately
  after the acquisition were included in the annual statement of the
  company, the fair market value of the assets of the entity at the date
  of the acquisition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Total value of all assets

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), the total value
  of all assets that the company or any of its subsidiaries has
  transferred during the 12-month period referred to in subsection (1)
  is the total of the value of each of those assets as reported in the
  last annual statement of the company prepared before the transfer of
  the asset or, if the value of any of those assets is not reported in
  that annual statement, as it would be reported in the annual statement
  of the company if the annual statement had been prepared, in
  accordance with the accounting principles referred to in subsection
  313(4), immediately before the transfer of the asset.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 470
- 1997, c. 15, s. 396
- 2001, c. 9, s. 550
- 2007, c. 6, s. 374
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-470-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 470"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional

**[[471]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-471 .sectionLabel}** Nothing in this Part
requires

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the termination of a loan made before February 7,
  2001;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the termination of a loan made after that date as a
  result of a commitment made before that date;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the disposal of an investment made before that date;
  or

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the disposal of an investment made after that date as
  a result of a commitment made before that date.

But if the loan or investment would be precluded or limited by this
Part, the amount of the loan or investment may not, except as provided
in subsections 456(2), 457(3) and 458(3), be increased after that date.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 471
- 2001, c. 9, s. 550
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Saving

**[[472]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-472 .sectionLabel}** A loan or investment
referred to in section 471 is deemed not to be prohibited by the
provisions of this Part.

## [PART X]{.HLabel1}[Adequacy of Capital and Liquidity]{.HTitleText1} {#h-457873 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adequacy of capital and liquidity

- **[[473]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-473 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company shall, in relation to its operations, maintain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} adequate capital, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} adequate and appropriate forms of liquidity,

  and shall comply with any regulations in relation thereto.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations and guidelines

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations and the
  Superintendent may make guidelines respecting the maintenance by
  companies of adequate capital and adequate and appropriate forms of
  liquidity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Directives

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding that a company is complying with
  regulations or guidelines made under subsection (2), the
  Superintendent may, by order, direct the company

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to increase its capital; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to provide additional liquidity in such forms and
    amounts as the Superintendent may require.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A company shall comply with an order made under
  subsection (3) within such time as the Superintendent specifies
  therein.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of value

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where an appraisal of any asset held by a company or
  any of its subsidiaries has been made by the Superintendent and the
  value determined by the Superintendent to be the appropriate value of
  the asset varies materially from the value placed by the company or
  subsidiary on the asset, the Superintendent shall send to the company,
  the auditor of the company and the audit committee of the company a
  written notice of the appropriate value of the asset as determined by
  the Superintendent.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 473
- 1996, c. 6, s. 120
:::

## [PART XI]{.HLabel1}[Self-dealing]{.HTitleText1} {#h-457892 .Part}

### [Interpretation and Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-457893 .Subheading}

Definition of [[senior officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

**[[473.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-473.1 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
this Part, a [[senior officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of a body corporate
is a person who is

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a director of the body corporate who is a full-time
  employee of the body corporate;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the chief executive officer, chief operating officer,
  president, secretary, treasurer, controller, chief financial officer,
  chief accountant, chief auditor or chief actuary of the body
  corporate;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a natural person who performs functions for the body
  corporate similar to those performed by a person referred to in
  paragraph (b);

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the head of the strategic planning unit of the body
  corporate;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the head of the unit of the body corporate that
  provides legal services or human resources services to the body
  corporate; or

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} any other officer reporting directly to the body
  corporate's board of directors, chief executive officer or chief
  operating officer.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 15, s. 397
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Related party of company

- **[[474]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-474 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of this Part, a person is a related party of a company
  where the person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is a person who has a significant interest in a
    class of shares of the company;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is a director or senior officer of the company or
    of a body corporate that controls the company or is acting in a
    similar capacity in respect of an unincorporated entity that
    controls the company;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is the spouse or common-law partner, or a child who
    is less than eighteen years of age, of a person described in
    paragraph (a) or (b);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} is an entity that is controlled by a person
    referred to in any of paragraphs (a) to (c);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} is an entity in which a person who controls the
    company has a substantial investment;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} is an entity in which the spouse or common-law
    partner, or a child who is less than eighteen years of age, of a
    person who controls the company has a substantial investment; or

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} is a person, or a member of a class of persons,
    designated under subsection (3) or (4) as, or deemed under
    subsection (5) to be, a related party of the company.

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 1997, c. 15, s. 398\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- subsidiaries and
  substantial investments of companies

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an entity in which a company has a substantial
  investment would, but for this subsection, be a related party of the
  company only because a person who controls the company controls the
  entity or has a substantial investment in the entity, and the person
  does not control the entity or have a substantial investment in the
  entity otherwise than through the person's controlling interest in the
  company, the entity is not a related party of the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designated related party

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, the Superintendent
  may, with respect to a particular company, designate as a related
  party of the company

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any person or class of persons whose direct or
    indirect interest in or relationship with the company or a related
    party of the company might reasonably be expected to affect the
    exercise of the best judgment of the company in respect of a
    transaction; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any person who is a party to any agreement,
    commitment or understanding referred to in section 9 if the company
    referred to in that section is the particular company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where a person is designated as a related party of a
  company pursuant to subsection (3), the Superintendent may also
  designate any entity in which the person has a substantial investment
  and any entity controlled by such an entity to be a related party of
  the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed related party

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where, in contemplation of a person becoming a
  related party of a company, the company enters into a transaction with
  the person, the person is deemed for the purposes of this Part to be a
  related party of the company in respect of that transaction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Holders of exempted shares

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent may, by order, designate a class
  of non-voting shares of a company for the purpose of this subsection.
  If a class of non-voting shares of a company is so designated, a
  person is deemed, notwithstanding paragraph (1)(a), not to be a
  related party of the company if the person would otherwise be a
  related party of the company only because the person has a significant
  interest in that class.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination of substantial investment

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of determining whether an entity or a
  person has a substantial investment for the purposes of paragraph
  (1)(e) or (f), the references to "control" and "controlled" in section
  10 shall be construed as references to "control, within the meaning of
  section 3, determined without regard to paragraph 3(1)(d)" and
  "controlled, within the meaning of section 3, determined without
  regard to paragraph 3(1)(d)", respectively.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination of control

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (1)(d), "controlled"
  means "controlled, within the meaning of section 3, determined without
  regard to paragraph 3(1)(d)".

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 474
- 1997, c. 15, s. 398
- 2000, c. 12, s. 302
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of Part

- **[[475]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-475 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This
  Part does not apply in respect of any transaction entered into prior
  to the coming into force of this Part but, after the coming into force
  of this Part, any modification of, addition to, or renewal or
  extension of a prior transaction is subject to this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} This Part does not apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} money or other assets held in trust, other than
    guaranteed trust money and assets held in respect thereof;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the issue of shares of any class of shares of a
    company when fully paid for in money or when issued

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with any provisions for the
      conversion of other issued and outstanding securities of the
      company into shares of that class of shares,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} as a share dividend,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in exchange for shares of a body corporate that
      has been continued as a company under Part III,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with the terms of an amalgamation
      under Part VI,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} by way of consideration in accordance with the
      terms of a sale agreement under Part VI, or

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} with the approval in writing of the
      Superintendent, in exchange for shares of another body corporate;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the payment of dividends by a company;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} transactions that consist of the payment or
    provision by a company to persons who are related parties of the
    company of salaries, fees, stock options, pension benefits,
    incentive benefits or other benefits or remuneration in their
    capacity as directors, officers or employees of the company;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} transactions approved by the Minister under
    subsection 678(1) of the [Bank Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01) or subsection
    715(1) of the [Insurance Companies Act](/eng/acts/I-11.8); or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if a company is controlled by a widely held bank
    holding company or a widely held insurance holding company,
    transactions approved by the Superintendent that are entered as part
    of, or in the course of, a restructuring of the holding company or
    of any entity controlled by it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Nothing in paragraph (2)(d) exempts from the
  application of this Part the payment by a company of fees or other
  remuneration to a person for

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the provision of services referred to in paragraph
    483(1)(a); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} duties outside the ordinary course of business of
    the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception for holding body corporate

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A holding body corporate of a company is not a
  related party of a company if the holding body corporate is a Canadian
  financial institution that is referred to in any of paragraphs (a)
  to (d) of the definition "financial institution" in section 2.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Substantial investment --- related
  party exception

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where a holding body corporate of a company is,
  because of subsection (4), not a related party of the company, any
  entity in which the holding body corporate has a substantial
  investment is not a related party of the company if no related party
  of the company has a substantial investment in the entity otherwise
  than through the control of the holding body corporate.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 475, c. 48, s. 493
- 1997, c. 15, s. 399
- 2001, c. 9, s. 551
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Meaning of "transaction"

- **[[476]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-476 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of this Part, entering into a transaction with a related
  party of a company includes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} making a guarantee on behalf of the related party;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} making an investment in any securities of the
    related party;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} taking an assignment of or otherwise acquiring a
    loan made by a third party to the related party; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} taking a security interest in the securities of the
    related party.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, the fulfilment of an
  obligation under the terms of any transaction, including the payment
  of interest on a loan or deposit, is part of the transaction, and not
  a separate transaction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Meaning of
  [[loan]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part,
  [[loan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a deposit, a financial lease, a
  conditional sales contract, a repurchase agreement and any other
  similar arrangement for obtaining funds or credit, but does not
  include investments in securities or the making of an acceptance,
  endorsement or other guarantee.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security of a related party

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part,
  [[security]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of a related party includes an
  option, transferable by delivery, to demand delivery of a specified
  number or amount of shares of the related party at a fixed price
  within a specified time.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 476
- 2007, c. 6, s. 375
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-476-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 476"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Prohibited Related Party Transactions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-457975 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibited transactions

- **[[477]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-477
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except as provided in this Part, a
  company shall not, directly or indirectly, enter into any transaction
  with a related party of the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transaction of entity

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Without limiting the generality of subsection (1), a
  company is deemed to have indirectly entered into a transaction in
  respect of which this Part applies where the transaction is entered
  into by an entity that is controlled by the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) does not apply where an entity that is
  controlled by a company is a financial institution incorporated or
  formed under the laws of a province and is subject to regulation and
  supervision, satisfactory to the Minister, regarding transactions with
  related parties of the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) does not apply in respect of
  transactions entered into by an entity that is controlled by a company
  if the transaction is a prescribed transaction or is one of a class of
  prescribed transactions.

### [Permitted Related Party Transactions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-457985 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Nominal value transactions

**[[478]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-478 .sectionLabel}** Notwithstanding
anything in this Part, a company may enter into a transaction with a
related party of the company if the value of the transaction is nominal
or immaterial to the company when measured by criteria that have been
established by the conduct review committee of the company and approved
in writing by the Superintendent.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Secured loans

**[[479]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-479 .sectionLabel}** A company may make a
loan to or a guarantee on behalf of a related party of the company or
take an assignment of or otherwise acquire a loan to a related party of
the company if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the loan or guarantee is fully secured by securities
  of or guaranteed by the Government of Canada or the government of a
  province; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the loan is a loan permitted by section 418 made to a
  related party who is a natural person on the security of a mortgage of
  the principal residence of that related party.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deposits

**[[480]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-480 .sectionLabel}** A company may enter
into a transaction with a related party of the company if the
transaction consists of a deposit by the company with a financial
institution that is a direct clearer or a member of a clearing group
under the by-laws of the Canadian Payments Association and the deposit
is made for clearing purposes.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Borrowing, etc., from related party

**[[481]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-481 .sectionLabel}** A company may borrow
money from, take deposits from, or issue debt obligations to, a related
party of the company.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition of assets

- **[[482]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-482 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company may purchase or otherwise acquire from a related party of the
  company

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} securities of, or securities guaranteed by, the
    Government of Canada or the government of a province;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} assets fully secured by securities of, or
    securities guaranteed by, the Government of Canada or the government
    of a province; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} goods for use in the ordinary course of business.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sale of assets

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 470, a company may sell any assets
  of the company to a related party of the company if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the consideration for the assets is fully paid in
    money; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} there is an active market for those assets.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Asset transactions with financial
  institutions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding any of the provisions of
  subsections (1) and (2), a company may, in the normal course of
  business and pursuant to arrangements that have been approved by the
  Superintendent in writing, acquire or dispose of any assets, other
  than real property, from or to a related party of the company that is
  a financial institution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Asset transactions in restructuring

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding any of the provisions of
  subsections (1) and (2), a company may acquire any assets from, or
  dispose of any assets to, a related party of the company as part of,
  or in the course of, a restructuring, if the acquisition or
  disposition has been approved in writing by the Superintendent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Goods or space for use in business

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A company may lease assets

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} from a related party of the company for use in the
    ordinary course of business of the company, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to a related party of the company,

  if the lease payments are made in money.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval under section 241

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A company may acquire any assets from, or dispose of
  any assets to, a related party of the company under a sale agreement
  that is approved by the Minister under section 241.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 482
- 2007, c. 6, s. 376
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-482-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 482"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Services

- **[[483]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-483 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company may enter into a transaction with a related party of the
  company if the transaction

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (2), consists of a written
    contract for the purchase by the company of services used in the
    ordinary course of business;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (4), involves the provision
    by the company of services, other than loans or guarantees, normally
    offered to the public by the company in the ordinary course of
    business;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} consists of a written contract with a financial
    institution or an entity in which the company is permitted to have a
    substantial investment pursuant to section 453 that is a related
    party of the company

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for the networking of any services provided by
      the company or the financial institution or entity, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for the referral of any person by the company to
      the financial institution or entity, or for the referral of any
      person by the financial institution or entity to the company;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} consists of a written contract for such pension or
    benefit plans or their management or administration as are
    incidental to directorships or to the employment of officers or
    employees of the company or its subsidiaries; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} involves the provision by the company of
    management, advisory, accounting, information processing or other
    services in relation to any business of the related party.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order concerning management by
  employees

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a company has entered into a contract pursuant
  to paragraph (1)(a) and the contract, when taken together with all
  other such contracts entered into by the company, results in all or
  substantially all of the management functions of the company being
  exercised by persons who are not employees of the company, the
  Superintendent may, by order, if the Superintendent considers that
  result to be inappropriate, require the company, within such time as
  may be specified in the order, to take all steps necessary to ensure
  that management functions that are integral to the carrying on of
  business by the company are exercised by employees of the company to
  the extent specified in the order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection 477(2), a company is deemed not to
  have indirectly entered into a transaction in respect of which this
  Part applies if the transaction is entered into by an entity that is
  controlled by the company and the business of which is limited to the
  activity referred to in 453(2)(c) and the transaction is on terms and
  conditions at least as favourable to the company as market terms and
  conditions, as defined in subsection 489(2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Loans or guarantees not included

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The provision of services, for the purposes of
  paragraph (1)(b), does not include the making of loans or guarantees.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 483
- 1997, c. 15, s. 400
- 2007, c. 6, s. 377
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-483-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 483"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transactions with holding companies

- **[[483.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-483.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2) and
  sections 483.2 and 483.3, if a widely held insurance holding company
  or a widely held bank holding company has a significant interest in
  any class of shares of a company, the company may enter into any
  transaction with the holding company or with any other related party
  of the company that is an entity in which the holding company has a
  substantial investment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Policies and procedures

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The company shall adhere to policies and procedures
  established under subsection 199(3) when entering into the
  transaction.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 552
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction

- **[[483.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-483.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a company enters into a
  transaction with a related party of the company with whom the company
  may enter into transactions under subsection 483.1(1) and that is not
  a federal financial institution, the company shall not directly or
  indirectly make, take an assignment of or otherwise acquire a loan to
  the related party, make an acceptance, endorsement or other guarantee
  on behalf of the related party or make an investment in the securities
  of the related party if, immediately following the transaction, the
  aggregate financial exposure, as that expression is defined by the
  regulations, of the company would exceed

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of all transactions of the company with
    the related party, the prescribed percentage of the company's
    regulatory capital or, if no percentage is prescribed, five per cent
    of the company's regulatory capital; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of all transactions of the company with
    such related parties of the company, the prescribed percentage of
    the company's regulatory capital or, if no percentage is prescribed,
    ten per cent of the company's regulatory capital.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Superintendent is of the opinion that it is
  necessary for the protection of the interests of the depositors and
  creditors of a company, the Superintendent may, by order,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} reduce the limit in paragraph (1)(a) or (b) that
    would otherwise apply to the company; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} impose limits on transactions by the company with
    related parties with whom the company may enter into transactions
    under subsection 483.1(1) that are federal financial institutions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent may, by order, increase the limit
  in paragraph (1)(a) or (b) that would otherwise apply to a company on
  transactions by the company with related parties that are financial
  institutions that are regulated in a manner acceptable to the
  Superintendent.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 552
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Assets transactions

- **[[483.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-483.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection 482(3), a company
  shall not, without the approval of the Superintendent and its conduct
  review committee, directly or indirectly acquire assets from a related
  party of the company with whom the company may enter into transactions
  under subsection 483.1(1) that is not a federal financial institution,
  or directly or indirectly transfer assets to such a related party if

  ::: {#458060 .Subsection}
  A + B \> C

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the value of the assets;

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the total value of all assets that the company directly or
      indirectly acquired from, or directly or indirectly transferred
      to, that related party in the 12 months ending immediately before
      the acquisition or transfer, other than assets acquired by or
      transferred to the company under transactions permitted by section
      478; and

  [C]{.dfn}
  :   is five per cent, or the percentage that may be prescribed, of the
      total value of the assets of the company, as shown in the last
      annual statement of the company prepared before the acquisition or
      transfer.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The prohibition in subsection (1) does not apply in
  respect of assets purchased or otherwise acquired under subsection
  482(1), assets sold under subsection 482(2) or any other assets as may
  be prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The approval of the Superintendent is not required if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the company purchases or sells assets under a sale
    agreement that is approved by the Minister under section 241; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the company or its subsidiary acquires shares of,
    or ownership interests in, an entity for which the approval of the
    Minister under Part VII or subsection 453(5) is required or the
    approval of the Superintendent under subsection 453(6) is required.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Value of assets

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of "A" in subsection (1), the value
  of the assets is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of assets that are acquired, the
    purchase price of the assets or, if the assets are shares of, or
    ownership interests in, an entity the assets of which will be
    included in the annual statement of the company after the
    acquisition, the fair market value of the assets; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of assets that are transferred, the
    book value of the assets as stated in the last annual statement of
    the company prepared before the transfer or, if the assets are
    shares of, or ownership interests in, an entity the assets of which
    were included in the last annual statement of the company before the
    transfer, the value of the assets as stated in the annual statement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Total value of all assets

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), the total value
  of all assets that the company or any of its subsidiaries has acquired
  during the period of twelve months referred to in subsection (1) is
  the purchase price of the assets or, if the assets are shares of, or
  ownership interests in, an entity the assets of which immediately
  after the acquisition were included in the annual statement of the
  company, the fair market value of the assets of the entity at the date
  of the acquisition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Total value of all assets

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), the total value
  of all assets that the company or any of its subsidiaries has
  transferred during the period of twelve months referred to in
  subsection (1) is the book value of the assets as stated in the last
  annual statement of the company prepared before the transfer or, if
  the assets are shares of, or ownership interests in, an entity the
  assets of which were included in the last annual statement of the
  company before the transfer, the value of the assets of the entity as
  stated in the annual statement.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 552
- 2007, c. 6, s. 378
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-483.3-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 483.3"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Directors and officers and their
interests

- **[[484]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-484
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2) and
  sections 485 and 486, a company may enter into any transaction with a
  related party of the company if the related party is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a natural person who is a related party of the
    company only because the person is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a director or senior officer of the company or of
      an entity that controls the company, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the spouse or common-law partner, or a child who
      is less than eighteen years of age, of a director or senior
      officer of the company or of an entity that controls the company;
      or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an entity that is a related party of the company
    only because the entity is controlled by

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a director or senior officer of the company or of
      an entity that controls the company, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the spouse or common-law partner, or a child who
      is less than eighteen years of age, of a director or senior
      officer referred to in subparagraph (i).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Loans to full-time senior officers

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company may, with respect to a related party of the
  company referred to in subsection (1) who is a full-time senior
  officer of the company, make, take an assignment of or otherwise
  acquire a loan to the related party only if the aggregate principal
  amount of all outstanding loans to the related party that are held by
  the company and its subsidiaries, together with the principal amount
  of the proposed loan, does not exceed the greater of twice the annual
  salary of the related party and \$100,000.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) does not apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} loans referred to in paragraph 479(b), and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} margin loans referred to in section 486,

  and the amount of any such loans to a related party of a company shall
  not be included in determining, for the purposes of subsection (2),
  the aggregate principal amount of all outstanding loans made by the
  company to the related party.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Preferred terms --- loan to senior
  officer

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding section 489, a company may make a
  loan, other than a margin loan, to a senior officer of the company on
  terms and conditions more favourable to the officer than those offered
  to the public by the company if those terms and conditions have been
  approved by the conduct review committee of the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Preferred terms --- loan to spouse or
  common-law partner

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding section 489, a company may make a
  loan referred to in paragraph 479(b) to the spouse or common-law
  partner of a senior officer of the company on terms and conditions
  more favourable than those offered to the public by the company if
  those terms and conditions have been approved by the conduct review
  committee of the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Preferred terms --- other financial
  services

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding section 489, a company may offer
  financial services, other than loans or guarantees, to a senior
  officer of the company, or to the spouse or common-law partner, or a
  child who is less than eighteen years of age, of a senior officer of
  the company, on terms and conditions more favourable than those
  offered to the public by the company if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the financial services are offered by the company
    to employees of the company on those favourable terms and
    conditions; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the conduct review committee of the company has
    approved the practice of making those financial services available
    on those favourable terms and conditions to senior officers of the
    company or to the spouses or common-law partners, or the children
    under eighteen years of age, of senior officers of the company.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 484
- 1997, c. 15, s. 401
- 2000, c. 12, ss. 300, 302
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Board approval required

- **[[485]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-485
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except with the concurrence of at
  least two thirds of the directors present at a meeting of the board of
  directors of the company, a company shall not, with respect to a
  related party of the company referred to in subsection 484(1),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} make, take an assignment of or otherwise acquire a
    loan to the related party, including a margin loan referred to in
    section 486,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} make a guarantee on behalf of the related party, or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} make an investment in the securities of the related
    party

  if, immediately following the transaction, the aggregate of

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the principal amount of all outstanding loans to
    the related party that are held by the company and its subsidiaries,
    other than

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} loans referred to in paragraph 479(b), and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the related party is a full-time senior
      officer of the company, loans to the related party that are
      permitted by subsection 484(2),

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the sum of all outstanding amounts guaranteed by
    the company and its subsidiaries on behalf of the related party, and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} where the related party is an entity, the book
    value of all investments by the company and its subsidiaries in the
    securities of the entity

  would exceed 2 per cent of the regulatory capital of the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limit on transactions with directors,
  officers and their interests

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company shall not, with respect to a related party
  of the company referred to in subsection 484(1),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} make, take an assignment of or otherwise acquire a
    loan to the related party, including a margin loan referred to in
    section 486,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} make a guarantee on behalf of the related party, or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} make an investment in the securities of the related
    party

  if, immediately following the transaction, the aggregate of

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the principal amount of all outstanding loans to
    all related parties of the company referred to in subsection 484(1)
    that are held by the company and its subsidiaries, other than

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} loans referred to in section 479, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} loans permitted by subsection 484(2),

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the sum of all outstanding amounts guaranteed by
    the company and its subsidiaries on behalf of all related parties of
    the company referred to in subsection 484(1), and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the book value of all investments by the company
    and its subsidiaries in the securities of all entities that are
    related parties of the company referred to in subsection 484(1)

  would exceed 50 per cent of the regulatory capital of the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exclusion of *de minimis* transactions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Loans, guarantees and investments that are referred
  to in section 478 shall not be included in calculating the aggregate
  of loans, guarantees and investments referred to in subsections (1)
  and (2).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 485
- 1997, c. 15, s. 402
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Margin loans

**[[486]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-486 .sectionLabel}** The Superintendent may
establish terms and conditions with respect to the making by a company
of margin loans to a director or senior officer of the company.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 486
- 1997, c. 15, s. 403
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption by order

- **[[487]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-487 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company may enter into a transaction with a related party of the
  company if the Superintendent, by order, has exempted the transaction
  from the provisions of section 477.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for order

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent shall not make an order referred
  to in subsection (1) unless the Superintendent is satisfied that the
  decision of the company to enter into the transaction has not been and
  is not likely to be influenced in any significant way by a related
  party of the company and does not involve in any significant way the
  interests of a related party of the company.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 487
- 1996, c. 6, s. 121
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed transactions

**[[488]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-488 .sectionLabel}** A company may enter
into a transaction with a related party of the company if the
transaction is a prescribed transaction or one of a class of prescribed
transactions.

### [Restrictions on Permitted Transactions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-458149 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Market terms and conditions

- **[[489]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-489
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except as provided in subsections
  484(4) to (6), any transaction entered into with a related party of
  the company shall be on terms and conditions that are at least as
  favourable to the company as market terms and conditions.

- Meaning of [[market terms and conditions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), [[market terms
  and conditions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a service or a loan facility or a
    deposit facility offered to the public by the company in the
    ordinary course of business, terms and conditions that are no more
    or less favourable than those offered to the public by the company
    in the ordinary course of business; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of any other transaction,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} terms and conditions, including those relating to
      price, rent or interest rate, that might reasonably be expected to
      apply in a similar transaction in an open market under conditions
      requisite to a fair transaction between parties who are at arm's
      length and who are acting prudently, knowledgeably and willingly,
      or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the transaction is one that would not
      reasonably be expected to occur in an open market between parties
      who are at arm's length, terms and conditions, including those
      relating to price, rent or interest rate, that would reasonably be
      expected to provide the company with fair value, having regard to
      all the circumstances of the transaction, and that would be
      consistent with the parties to the transaction acting prudently,
      knowledgeably and willingly.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 489
- 2001, c. 9, s. 553
:::

**[[490 and 491]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-490_and_491
.sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 1997, c. 15, s. 404\]]{.Repealed}

### [Disclosure]{.HTitleText2} {#h-458162 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Company obligation

- **[[492]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-492
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where, in respect of any proposed
  transaction permitted by this Part, other than those referred to in
  section 478, a company has reason to believe that the other party to
  the transaction is a related party of the company, the company shall
  take all reasonable steps to obtain from the other party full
  disclosure, in writing, of any interest or relationship, direct or
  indirect, that would make the other party a related party of the
  company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reliance on information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company and any person who is a director or an
  officer, employee or agent of the company may rely on any information
  contained in any disclosure received by the company pursuant to
  subsection (1) or any information otherwise acquired in respect of any
  matter that might be the subject of such a disclosure and no action
  lies against the company or any such person for anything done or
  omitted in good faith in reliance on any such information.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to Superintendent

**[[493]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-493 .sectionLabel}** Where a company has
entered into a transaction that the company is prohibited by this Part
from entering into, or where a company has entered into a transaction
for which approval is required under subsection 485(1) without having
obtained the approval, the company shall, on becoming aware of that
fact, notify the Superintendent without delay.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 493
- 1997, c. 15, s. 405
:::

### [Remedial Actions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-458172 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order to void contract or to grant other
remedy

- **[[494]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-494 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  company enters into a transaction that it is prohibited from entering
  into by this Part, the company or the Superintendent may apply to a
