  court for an order setting aside the transaction or for any other
  appropriate remedy, including an order directing that the related
  party of the company involved in the transaction account to the
  company for any profit or gain realized or that any director or senior
  officer of the company who authorized the transaction compensate the
  company for any loss or damage incurred by the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time limit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An application under subsection (1) in respect of a
  particular transaction may only be made within the period of three
  months following the day the notice referred to in section 493 in
  respect of the transaction is given to the Superintendent or, if no
  such notice is given, the day the Superintendent becomes aware of the
  transaction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (2), a document
  purporting to have been issued by the Superintendent, certifying the
  day on which the Superintendent became aware of the transaction,
  shall, in the absence of evidence to the contrary, be received in
  evidence as conclusive proof of that fact without proof of the
  signature or of the official character of the person appearing to have
  signed the document and without further proof.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 494
- 2001, c. 9, s. 554
:::

## [PART XII]{.HLabel1}[Regulation of Companies --- Superintendent]{.HTitleText1} {#h-458182 .Part}

### [Supervision]{.HTitleText2} {#h-458183 .Subheading}

#### [Returns]{.HTitleText3} {#h-458184 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Required information

**[[495]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-495 .sectionLabel}** A company shall provide
the Superintendent with such information, at such times and in such form
as the Superintendent may require.

**[[496]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-496 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2007, c.
6, s. 379\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-496-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 496"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[497]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-497 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2007, c.
6, s. 379\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-497-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 497"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[498]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-498 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2007, c.
6, s. 379\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-498-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 498"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Names of directors and auditors

- **[[499]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-499 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  company shall, within thirty days after each annual meeting of the
  company, provide the Superintendent with a return showing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name, residence and citizenship of each
    director holding office immediately following the meeting;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the mailing address of each director holding office
    immediately following the meeting;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the bodies corporate of which each director
    referred to in paragraph (a) is an officer or director and the firms
    of which each director is a member;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the affiliation, within the meaning of section 166,
    with the company of each director referred to in paragraph (a);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the names of the directors referred to in
    paragraph (a) who are officers or employees of the company or any
    affiliate of the company, and the positions they occupy;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the name of each committee of the company on which
    each director referred to in paragraph (a) serves;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the date of expiration of the term of each director
    referred to in paragraph (a); and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the name, address and date of appointment of the
    auditor of the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Changes

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any information relating to a director or auditor
    of a company shown in the latest return made to the Superintendent
    under subsection (1), other than information referred to in
    paragraph (1)(c) or (d), becomes inaccurate or incomplete,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a vacancy in the position of auditor of the company
    occurs or is filled by another person, or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a vacancy on the board of directors of the company
    occurs or is filled,

  the company shall forthwith provide the Superintendent with such
  information as is required to maintain the return in a complete and
  accurate form.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copy of by-laws

**[[500]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-500 .sectionLabel}** A company shall send to
the Superintendent, within thirty days after the coming into effect of a
by-law or an amendment to a by-law, a copy of the by-law or amendment.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 500
- 2001, c. 9, s. 556
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Register of companies

- **[[501]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-501 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Superintendent shall, in respect of each company for which an order
  approving the commencement and carrying on of business has been made,
  cause a register to be maintained containing a copy of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the incorporating instrument of the company; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the information referred to in paragraphs
    499(1)(a), (c) and (e) to (h) contained in the latest return sent to
    the Superintendent under section 499.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The register may be maintained in

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a bound or loose-leaf form or in a photographic
    film form; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a system of mechanical or electronic data
    processing or any other information storage device that is capable
    of reproducing any required information in intelligible written form
    within a reasonable time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Persons are entitled to reasonable access to the
  register and may make copies of or take extracts from the information
  in it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Evidence

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A statement containing information in the register
  and purporting to be certified by the Superintendent is admissible in
  evidence in all courts as proof, in the absence of evidence to the
  contrary, of the facts stated in the statement without proof of the
  appointment or signature of the Superintendent.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 501
- 2001, c. 9, s. 556
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Production of information and documents

- **[[502]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-502 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Superintendent may, by order, direct a person who controls a company
  or any entity that is affiliated with a company to provide the
  Superintendent with any information or documents that may be specified
  in the order if the Superintendent believes that the production of the
  information or documents is necessary in order to be satisfied that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the provisions of this Act are being duly observed
    and that the company is in a sound financial condition; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the company has adequate policies and procedures to
    protect itself against threats to its integrity or security.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any person to whom a direction has been issued under
  subsection (1) shall provide the information or documents specified in
  the order within the time specified in the order and, where the order
  does not specify a time, the person shall provide the information or
  documents within a reasonable time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of an entity
  that controls a company or is affiliated with a company where that
  entity is a financial institution regulated

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} by or under an Act of Parliament; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} by or under an Act of the legislature of a province
    where the Superintendent has entered into an agreement with the
    appropriate official or public body responsible for the supervision
    of financial institutions in that province concerning the sharing of
    information on such financial institutions.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 502
- [2023, c. 26, s. 522](#1410895-1417196){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410895-1417196.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-502-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 502"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Confidential information

- **[[503]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-503
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 504.1, all
  information regarding the business or affairs of a company, or
  regarding a person dealing with a company, that is obtained by the
  Superintendent, or by any person acting under the direction of the
  Superintendent, as a result of the administration or enforcement of
  any Act of Parliament, and all information prepared from that
  information, is confidential and shall be treated accordingly.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure permitted

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Nothing in subsection (1) prevents the Superintendent
  from disclosing any information

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to any government agency or body that regulates or
    supervises financial institutions, for purposes related to that
    regulation or supervision,

  - [(a.01)]{.lawlabel} to any other agency or body that regulates or
    supervises financial institutions, for purposes related to that
    regulation or supervision,

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} to the Canada Deposit Insurance Corporation for
    purposes related to its operation; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to the Deputy Minister of Finance or any officer of
    the Department of Finance authorized in writing by the Deputy
    Minister of Finance or to the Governor of the Bank of Canada or any
    officer of the Bank of Canada authorized in writing by the Governor
    of the Bank of Canada, for the purposes of policy analysis related
    to the regulation of financial institutions,

  if the Superintendent is satisfied that the information will be
  treated as confidential by the agency, body or person to whom it is
  disclosed.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 503
- 1996, c. 6, s. 122
- 1997, c. 15, s. 406
- 2001, c. 9, s. 557
- 2007, c. 6, s. 380
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-503-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 503"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[503.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-503.1 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in
Council may make regulations prohibiting, limiting or restricting the
disclosure by companies of prescribed supervisory information.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1999, c. 28, s. 144
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Evidentiary privilege

- **[[504]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-504
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Prescribed supervisory information
  shall not be used as evidence in any civil proceedings and is
  privileged for that purpose.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No testimony or production

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall by an order of any court, tribunal or
  other body be required in any civil proceedings to give oral testimony
  or to produce any document relating to any prescribed supervisory
  information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions to subsection (1)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Minister, the Superintendent or the Attorney
    General of Canada may, in accordance with the regulations, if any,
    use prescribed supervisory information as evidence in any
    proceedings; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a company may, in accordance with the regulations,
    if any, use prescribed supervisory information as evidence in any
    proceedings in relation to the administration or enforcement of this
    Act or the [Winding-up and Restructuring Act](/eng/acts/W-11) that
    are commenced by the company, the Minister, the Superintendent or
    the Attorney General of Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions to subsections (1) and (2)

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsections (1) and (2) and section 39.1 of
  the [Office of the Superintendent of Financial Institutions
  Act](/eng/acts/O-2.7), a court, tribunal or other body may, by order,
  require the Minister, the Superintendent or a company to give oral
  testimony or to produce any document relating to any prescribed
  supervisory information in any civil proceedings in relation to the
  administration or enforcement of this Act that are commenced by the
  Minister, the Superintendent, the Attorney General of Canada or the
  company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No waiver

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The disclosure of any prescribed supervisory
  information, other than under subsection (3) or (4), does not
  constitute a waiver of the privilege referred to in subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, for the purposes of
  subsection (3), make regulations respecting the circumstances in which
  prescribed supervisory information may be used as evidence.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 504
- 1996, c. 6, s. 123
- 2007, c. 6, s. 381
- 2015, c. 36, s. 232
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-504-20070420.html "Link to previous version of section 504"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No waiver

- **[[504.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-504.01
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the
  disclosure by a company --- or by a person who controls a company or
  by an entity that is affiliated with a company --- to the
  Superintendent of any information that is subject to a privilege under
  the law of evidence, solicitor-client privilege or the professional
  secrecy of advocates and notaries or to litigation privilege does not
  constitute a waiver of any of those privileges or that secrecy.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No disclosure

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent shall not disclose any information
  referred to in subsection (1) to any person whose powers, duties or
  functions include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the investigation or prosecution of an offence
    under any Act of Parliament or of the legislature of a province; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the investigation of, or conduct of proceedings in
    respect of, a violation under an Act referred to in paragraph (a).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 27, s. 168
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure by Superintendent

- **[[504.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-504.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent shall disclose at
  such times and in such manner as the Minister may determine, such
  information obtained by the Superintendent under this Act as the
  Minister considers ought to be disclosed for the purposes of the
  analysis of the financial condition of a company and that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is contained in returns filed pursuant to the
    Superintendent's financial regulatory reporting requirements in
    respect of companies; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has been obtained as a result of an industry-wide
    or sectoral survey conducted by the Superintendent in relation to an
    issue or circumstances that could have an impact on the financial
    condition of companies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prior consultation required

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall consult with the Superintendent
  before making any determination under subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 6, s. 124
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure by a company

- **[[504.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-504.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A company shall make available to
  the public such information concerning

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the compensation of its executives, as that
    expression is defined by the regulations, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} its business and affairs for the purpose of the
    analysis of its financial condition,

  in such form and manner and at such times as may be required by or
  pursuant to such regulations as the Governor in Council may make for
  the purpose.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption by regulation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(a) does not apply to a company that is
  within such class or classes of companies as may be prescribed.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 6, s. 124
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions to disclosure

**[[504.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-504.3 .sectionLabel}** Subject to any
regulations made under section 444, no information obtained by a company
regarding any of its customers shall be disclosed or made available
under subsection 504.1(1) or section 504.2.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 6, s. 124
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report respecting disclosure

**[[504.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-504.4 .sectionLabel}** The Superintendent
shall prepare a report, to be included in the report referred to in
section 40 of the [Office of the Superintendent of Financial
Institutions Act](/eng/acts/O-2.7), respecting the disclosure of
information by companies and describing the state of progress made in
enhancing the disclosure of information in the financial services
industry.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 6, s. 124
- 2001, c. 9, s. 558
:::

#### [Inspection of Companies]{.HTitleText3} {#h-458304 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Examination of companies

- **[[505]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-505 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Superintendent, from time to time, but at least once in each calendar
  year, shall make or cause to be made any examination and inquiry into
  the business and affairs of each company that the Superintendent
  considers to be necessary or expedient to determine whether the
  company is complying with the provisions of this Act, whether the
  company is in a sound financial condition and whether the company has
  adequate policies and procedures to protect itself against threats to
  its integrity or security. After the conclusion of each examination
  and inquiry, the Superintendent shall report on it to the Minister.

- [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2023, c. 26, s. 523\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access to records of company

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent or a person acting under the
  Superintendent's direction

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has a right of access to any records, cash, assets
    and security held by or on behalf of a company; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} may require the directors, officers and auditor of
    a company to provide information and explanations, to the extent
    that they are reasonably able to do so, in respect of the condition
    and affairs of the company or any entity in which the company has a
    substantial investment.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 505
- 2001, c. 9, s. 559
- 2012, c. 5, s. 180
- [2023, c. 26, s. 523](#1410897-1417199){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410897-1417199.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-505-20230622.html "Link to previous version of section 505"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power of Superintendent on inquiry

**[[506]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-506 .sectionLabel}** The Superintendent has
all the powers of a person appointed as a commissioner under Part II of
the [Inquiries Act](/eng/acts/I-11) for the purpose of obtaining
evidence under oath, and may delegate those powers to any person acting
under the Superintendent's direction.

### [Remedial Powers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-458316 .Subheading}

#### [Prudential Agreements]{.HTitleText3} {#h-458317 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prudential agreement

**[[506.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-506.1 .sectionLabel}** The Superintendent
may enter into an agreement, called a "prudential agreement", with a
company for the purposes of implementing any measure designed to
maintain or improve its safety and soundness or establishing adequate
policies and procedures to protect it against threats to its integrity
or security.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 560
- [2023, c. 26, s. 524](#1410903-1417202){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410903-1417202.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-506.1-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 506.1"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Directions of Compliance]{.HTitleText3} {#h-458322 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Superintendent's directions to company

- **[[507]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-507
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where, in the opinion of the
  Superintendent, a company, or a person with respect to a company, is
  committing, or is about to commit, an act that is an unsafe or unsound
  practice in conducting the business of the company, or is pursuing or
  is about to pursue any course of conduct that is an unsafe or unsound
  practice in conducting the business of the company, the Superintendent
  may direct the company or person to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} cease or refrain from committing the act or
    pursuing the course of conduct; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} perform such acts as in the opinion of the
    Superintendent are necessary to remedy the situation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Directions --- policies and procedures

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} If, in the opinion of the Superintendent, a company
  does not have adequate policies and procedures to protect itself
  against threats to its integrity or security, the Superintendent may
  direct the company to take any measures that in the opinion of the
  Superintendent are necessary to remedy the situation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Opportunity for representations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), no direction shall be
  issued to a company or person under subsection (1) or (1.1) unless the
  company or person is provided with a reasonable opportunity to make
  representations in respect of the matter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Temporary direction

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If, in the opinion of the Superintendent, the length
  of time required for representations to be made under subsection (2)
  might be prejudicial to the public interest, the Superintendent may
  make a temporary direction with respect to the matters referred to in
  paragraphs (1)(a) and (b) or subsection (1.1) having effect for a
  period of not more than 15 days.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 508, a temporary direction under
  subsection (3) continues to have effect after the expiration of the
  fifteen day period referred to in that subsection if no
  representations are made to the Superintendent within that period or,
  if representations have been made, the Superintendent notifies the
  company or person that the Superintendent is not satisfied that there
  are sufficient grounds for revoking the direction.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 507
- [2023, c. 26, s. 525](#1410905-1417211){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410905-1417211.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-507-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 507"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[508]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-508 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 1996, c.
6, s. 125\]]{.Repealed}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Court enforcement

- **[[509]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-509
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where a company or person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is contravening or has failed to comply with a
    prudential agreement entered into under section 506.1 or a direction
    of the Superintendent issued to the company or person under
    subsection 507(1), (1.1) or (3),

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is contravening this Act, or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} has omitted to do any thing under this Act that is
    required to be done by or on the part of the company or person,

  the Superintendent may, in addition to any other action that may be
  taken under this Act, apply to a court for an order requiring the
  company or person to comply with the prudential agreement or
  direction, cease the contravention or do any thing that is required to
  be done, and on such application the court may so order and make any
  other order it thinks fit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An appeal from a decision of a court under
  subsection (1) lies in the same manner, and to the same court, as an
  appeal from any other order of the court.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 509
- 2001, c. 9, s. 561
- [2023, c. 26, s. 526](#1410908-1417213){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410908-1417213.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-509-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 509"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Disqualification and Removal of Directors or Senior Officers]{.HTitleText3} {#h-458346 .Subheading}

Meaning of [[senior officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

**[[509.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-509.01 .sectionLabel}** In sections 509.1
and 509.2, [[senior officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the chief
executive officer, secretary, treasurer or controller of a company or
any other officer reporting directly to the company's board of directors
or chief executive officer.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 562
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

- **[[509.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-509.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This section applies only in respect
  of a company

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that has been notified by the Superintendent that
    this section applies to it where the company is subject to measures
    designed to maintain or improve its safety and soundness, which
    measures

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} have been specified by the Superintendent by way
      of conditions or limitations in respect of the order approving the
      commencement and carrying on of the company's business, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} are contained in a prudential agreement entered
      into under section 506.1 or an undertaking given by the company to
      the Superintendent; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that is the subject of a direction made under
    section 507 or an order made under subsection 473(3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information to be provided

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A company shall provide the Superintendent with the
  name of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} each person who has been nominated for election or
    appointment as a member of its board of directors,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} each person who has been selected by the company
    for appointment as a senior officer, and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} each person who is newly elected as a director of
    the company at a meeting of shareholders and who was not proposed
    for election by anyone involved in the management of the company,

  together with such other information about the background, business
  record and experience of the person as the Superintendent may require.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When information to be provided

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The information required by subsection (2) shall be
  provided to the Superintendent

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at least thirty days prior to the date or proposed
    date of the election or appointment or within such shorter period as
    the Superintendent may allow; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a person referred to in paragraph
    (2)(c), within fifteen days after the date of the election of the
    person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disqualification or removal

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the Superintendent is of the opinion that, on the
  basis of the competence, business record, experience, conduct or
  character of a person, he or she is not suitable to hold that
  position, the Superintendent may, by order

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a person referred to in paragraph
    (2)(a) or (b), disqualify the person from being elected or appointed
    as a director of a company or from being appointed as a senior
    officer; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a person referred to in paragraph
    (2)(c), remove the person from office as a director of the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Risk of prejudice

  [(4.1)]{.lawlabel} In forming an opinion under subsection (4), the
  Superintendent must consider whether the interests of the depositors
  and creditors of the company would likely be prejudiced if the person
  were to take office or continue to hold office, as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations may be made

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent must in writing notify the person
  concerned and the company of any action that the Superintendent
  proposes to take under subsection (4) and must afford them an
  opportunity within 15 days after the date of the notice, or within any
  longer period that the Superintendent allows, to make representations
  to the Superintendent in relation to the matter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Where an order has been made under subsection (4)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} disqualifying a person from being elected or
    appointed to a position, the person shall not be, and the company
    shall not permit the person to be, elected or appointed to the
    position; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} removing a director from office, the person shall
    not continue to hold, and the company shall not permit the person to
    continue to hold, office as a director.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 6, s. 126
- 2001, c. 9, s. 563
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Removal of directors or senior officers

- **[[509.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-509.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent may, by order,
  remove a person from office as a director or senior officer of a
  company if the Superintendent is of the opinion that the person is not
  suitable to hold that office

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on the basis of the competence, business record,
    experience, conduct or character of the person; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} because the person has contravened or, by action or
    negligence, has contributed to the contravention of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} this Act or the regulations made under it,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a direction made under section 507,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} an order made under subsection 473(3),

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a condition or limitation in respect of the
      order approving the commencement and carrying on the company's
      business, or

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a prudential agreement entered into under section
      506.1 or an undertaking given by the company to the
      Superintendent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Risk of prejudice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In forming an opinion under subsection (1), the
  Superintendent must consider whether the interests of the depositors
  and creditors of the company have been or are likely to be prejudiced
  by the person's holding office as a director or senior officer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations may be made

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent must in writing notify the person
  concerned and the company of any removal order that the Superintendent
  proposes to make under subsection (1) and must afford them an
  opportunity within 15 days after the date of the notice, or within any
  longer period that the Superintendent allows, to make representations
  to the Superintendent in relation to the matter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the Superintendent is of the opinion that the
  public interest may be prejudiced by the director or senior officer
  continuing to exercise the powers or carry out the duties and
  functions of that office during the period for making representations,
  the Superintendent may make an order suspending the director or senior
  officer. The suspension may not extend beyond 10 days after the
  expiration of that period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of order

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent shall, without delay, notify the
  director or senior officer, as the case may be, and the company of a
  removal order or suspension order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consequences of removal order

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The director or senior officer, as the case may be,
  ceases to hold that office as of the date the removal order is made or
  any later date specified in the order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The director or senior officer, as the case may be,
  or the company may, within 30 days after the date of receipt of notice
  of the removal order under subsection (5), or within any longer period
  that the Court allows, appeal the matter to the Federal Court.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of Federal Court

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} The Federal Court, in the case of an appeal, may
  dismiss the appeal or set aside the removal order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order not stayed by appeal

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} A removal order is not stayed by an appeal.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 564
:::

#### [Supervisory Intervention]{.HTitleText3} {#h-458410 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Superintendent may take control

- **[[510]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-510
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Act, where any of
  the circumstances described in subsection (1.1) exist in respect of a
  company, the Superintendent may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} take control, for a period not exceeding sixteen
    days, of the assets of the company and the assets held in trust by
    or under the administration of the company; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} unless the Minister advises the Superintendent that
    the Minister is of the opinion that it is not in the public interest
    to do so,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} take control, for a period exceeding sixteen
      days, of the assets of the company and the assets held in trust by
      or under the administration of the company,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} where control of assets has been taken under
      paragraph (a), continue the control beyond the sixteen days
      referred to in that paragraph, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} take control of the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Circumstances for taking control

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Control by the Superintendent under subsection (1)
  may be taken in respect of a company where

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the company has failed to pay its liabilities or,
    in the opinion of the Superintendent, will not be able to pay its
    liabilities as they become due and payable;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2001, c. 9, s. 565\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the assets of the company are not, in the opinion
    of the Superintendent, sufficient to give adequate protection to the
    company's depositors and creditors;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any asset appearing on the books or records of the
    company or held in trust by or under the administration of the
    company is not, in the opinion of the Superintendent, satisfactorily
    accounted for;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the regulatory capital of the company has, in the
    opinion of the Superintendent, reached a level or is eroding in a
    manner that may detrimentally affect the company's depositors or
    creditors;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the company has failed to comply with an order of
    the Superintendent under paragraph 473(3)(a);

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the company's deposit insurance has been terminated
    by the Canada Deposit Insurance Corporation;

  - [(g.1)]{.lawlabel} in the opinion of the Superintendent, the
    company's depositors and creditors may be detrimentally affected
    because all of the common shares of the company must be disposed of
    under a direction made by the Minister or because there is a
    prohibition under this Act in respect of the exercise of the right
    to vote attached to all of the common shares of the company;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} in the opinion of the Superintendent, any other
    state of affairs exists in respect of the company that may be
    materially prejudicial to the interests of the company's depositors
    or creditors or the beneficiaries of any trust under the company's
    administration, including where proceedings under a law relating to
    bankruptcy or insolvency have been commenced in Canada or elsewhere
    in respect of the holding body corporate of the company;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the opinion of the Superintendent, the continued
    operation of the company by the directors of the company or by the
    officers of the company responsible for its management would be
    materially prejudicial to its integrity or security; or

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} in the opinion of the Superintendent, the continued
    operation of the company by the directors of the company or by the
    officers of the company responsible for its management would pose a
    risk to national security.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister's powers

  [(1.11)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Act, the Minister may, for reasons
  related to national security, direct the Superintendent to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} take control, for a period not exceeding 16 days,
    of the assets of the company and the assets held in trust by or
    under the administration of the company;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} take control, for a period exceeding 16 days, of
    the assets of the company and the assets held in trust by or under
    the administration of the company;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if control of assets has been taken under paragraph
    (a), continue the control beyond the 16 days referred to in that
    paragraph; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} take control of the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of proposed action

  [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent must notify a company of any
  action proposed to be taken in respect of it under paragraph (1)(b)
  and of its right to make written representations to the Superintendent
  within the time specified in the notice not exceeding ten days after
  it receives the notice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice --- up to 16 days

  [(1.3)]{.lawlabel} If the Superintendent takes control of the assets
  of a company under paragraph (1.11)(a), the Superintendent shall
  notify the company that control has been taken at the direction of the
  Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice --- more than 16 days

  [(1.4)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister is considering whether to exercise
  the powers under any of paragraphs (1.11)(b) to (d), the
  Superintendent shall notify the company of the action that is being
  considered and of its right to make written representations to the
  Minister within the time specified in the notice, not exceeding 10
  days after it receives the notice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice --- Committee and Review Agency

  [(1.5)]{.lawlabel} Within 30 days after exercising any of the powers
  under subsection (1.11), the Minister shall notify

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the [[Committee]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined
    in section 2 of the [National Security and Intelligence Committee of
    Parliamentarians Act](/eng/acts/N-16.6); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the [[Review Agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as
    defined in section 2 of the [National Security and Intelligence
    Review Agency Act](/eng/acts/N-16.62).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Objectives of Superintendent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Superintendent has control under
  subsection (1) or (1.11) of the assets of a company referred to in
  that subsection, the Superintendent may do all things necessary or
  expedient to protect the rights and interests of the depositors and
  creditors of the company or the beneficiaries of any trust under the
  administration of the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of Superintendent

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the Superintendent has control under
  subsection (1) or (1.11) of the assets of a company referred to in
  that subsection,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the company shall not make, acquire or transfer any
    loan or make any purchase, sale or exchange of securities or any
    disbursement or transfer of cash of any kind without the prior
    approval of the Superintendent or a representative designated by the
    Superintendent; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} no director, officer or employee of the company
    shall have access to any cash or securities held by or under the
    administration of the company unless

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a representative of the Superintendent
      accompanies the director, officer or employee, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the access is previously authorized by the
      Superintendent or the Superintendent's representative.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 510
- 1996, c. 6, s. 127
- 2001, c. 9, s. 565
- [2023, c. 26, s. 527](#1410910-1417236){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410910-1417236.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-510-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 510"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[511 to 513]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-511_to_513
.sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 1996, c. 6, s. 128\]]{.Repealed}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of directors and officers
suspended

- **[[514]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-514 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the Superintendent takes control of a company under subparagraph
  510(1)(b)(iii) or paragraph 510(1.11)(d), the powers, duties,
  functions, rights and privileges of the directors of the company and
  of the officers of the company responsible for its management are
  suspended.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Superintendent to manage company

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Superintendent takes control of a company
  under subparagraph 510(1)(b)(iii) or paragraph 510(1.11)(d), the
  Superintendent shall manage the business and affairs of the company
  and in so doing the Superintendent

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} may perform any of the duties and functions that
    the persons referred to in subsection (1) were performing prior to
    the taking of control; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has and may exercise any power, right or privilege
    that any such person had or could have exercised prior to the taking
    of control.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Persons to assist

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the Superintendent takes control of a company
  under subparagraph 510(1)(b)(iii) or paragraph 510(1.11)(d), the
  Superintendent may appoint one or more persons to assist in the
  management of the company.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 514
- 1996, c. 6, s. 129
- [2023, c. 26, s. 528(E)](#1410921-1417241){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410921-1417241.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-514-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 514"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Expiration of control

- **[[515]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-515
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Control by the Superintendent under
  subsection 510(1) of a company or of the assets of a company and the
  assets held in trust by or under the administration of the company
  expires on the day on which a notice by the Superintendent is sent to
  the directors and officers who conducted the business and affairs of
  the company stating that the Superintendent is of the opinion that the
  circumstances leading to the taking of control by the Superintendent
  have been substantially rectified and that the company can resume
  control of its business and affairs.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Expiration of control --- Minister's
  direction

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Control by the Superintendent under subsection
  510(1.11) of a company or of the assets of a company and the assets
  held in trust by or under the administration of the company expires on
  the day on which a notice by the Superintendent is sent to the
  directors and officers who conducted the business and affairs of the
  company stating that the Minister is of the opinion, on the
  recommendation of the Superintendant, that corrective measures have
  been taken in response to the reasons related to national security and
  that the company can resume control of its business and affairs.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 515
- 1996, c. 6, s. 129
- [2023, c. 26, s. 529](#1410928-1417247){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410928-1417247.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-515-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 515"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Superintendent may request winding-up

**[[515.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-515.1 .sectionLabel}** The Superintendent
may, at any time before the receipt of a request under section 516 to
relinquish control of a company or of the assets of a company and the
assets held in trust by or under the administration of the company,
request the Attorney General of Canada to apply for a winding-up order
under section 10.1 of the [Winding-up and Restructuring
Act](/eng/acts/W-11) in respect of the company where

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the assets of the company and the assets held in
  trust by or under the administration of the company are under the
  control of the Superintendent under subparagraph 510(1)(b)(i) or (ii)
  or paragraph 510(1.11)(b) or (c); or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the company is under the control of the
  Superintendent under subparagraph 510(1)(b)(iii) or paragraph
  510(1.11)(d).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 6, s. 129
- [2023, c. 26, s. 530](#1410930-1417250){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410930-1417250.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-515.1-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 515.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to relinquish control

**[[516]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-516 .sectionLabel}** If no action has been
taken by the Superintendent under section 515.1 and, after 30 days
following the taking of control by the Superintendent under subsection
510(1) or (1.11) of a company or of the assets of a company and the
assets held in trust by or under the administration of the company, the
Superintendent receives from its board of directors a notice in writing
requesting the Superintendent to relinquish control, the Superintendent
shall, not later than 12 days after receipt of the notice,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} comply with the request; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} request the Attorney General of Canada to apply for a
  winding-up order under section 10.1 of the [Winding-up and
  Restructuring Act](/eng/acts/W-11) in respect of the company.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 516
- 1996, c. 6, s. 129
- [2023, c. 26, s. 531(E)](#1410932-1417252){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410932-1417252.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-516-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 516"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Advisory committee

**[[517]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-517 .sectionLabel}** The Superintendent may,
from among the companies that are subject to an assessment under section
23 of the [Office of the Superintendent of Financial Institutions
Act](/eng/acts/O-2.7) and required to share in the expenses resulting
from the taking of control of a company under subsection 510(1) or
(1.11), appoint a committee of not more than six members to advise the
Superintendent in respect of assets, management and all other matters
pertinent to the duties and responsibilities of the Superintendent in
exercising control of the company.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 517
- 1996, c. 6, s. 129
- [2023, c. 26, s. 532(E)](#1410934-1417255){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410934-1417255.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-517-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 517"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Expenses payable by company

- **[[518]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-518 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the Superintendent has taken control of a company under subparagraph
  510(1)(b)(iii) or paragraph 510(1.11)(d) and the control expires or is
  relinquished under section 515 or paragraph 516(a), the Superintendent
  may direct that the company be liable for repayment of all or part of
  the expenses resulting from the taking of control of the company and
  assessed against and paid by other companies under section 23 of the
  [Office of the Superintendent of Financial Institutions
  Act](/eng/acts/O-2.7), together with any interest in respect of the
  expenses at any rate that is specified by the Superintendent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Debt due to Her Majesty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where any direction is made under subsection (1), the
  amount for which the company is liable is a debt due to Her Majesty in
  right of Canada payable on demand and is recoverable in the Federal
  Court or any other court of competent jurisdiction.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 518
- 1996, c. 6, s. 130
- [2023, c. 26, s. 533(E)](#1410936-1417257){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410936-1417257.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-518-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 518"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Priority of claim in liquidation

**[[519]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-519 .sectionLabel}** In the case of the
winding-up of a company, the expenses resulting from the taking of
control of the company under subsection 510(1) or (1.11) and assessed
against and paid by other companies under section 23 of the [Office of
the Superintendent of Financial Institutions Act](/eng/acts/O-2.7), and
interest in respect of the expenses at any rate that is specified by the
Superintendent, constitute a claim of His Majesty in right of Canada
against the assets of the company that ranks after all other claims but
prior to any claim in respect of the shares of the company.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 519
- 1996, c. 6, s. 131(E)
- [2023, c. 26, s. 534(E)](#1410938-1417260){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410938-1417260.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-519-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 519"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of assessment

**[[520]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-520 .sectionLabel}** Any amount recovered
pursuant to section 518 or 519 shall be applied to reduce the total
amount of expenses incurred for or in connection with the administration
of this Act.

## [PART XII.1]{.HLabel1}[Regulation of Companies --- Commissioner]{.HTitleText1} {#h-458486 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Required information

**[[520.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-520.1 .sectionLabel}** A company shall
provide the Commissioner with the information at the times and in the
form that the Commissioner may require for the purposes of the
administration of the [Financial Consumer Agency of Canada
Act](/eng/acts/F-11.1) and the consumer provisions.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 566
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Confidential information

- **[[520.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-520.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2),
  information regarding the business or affairs of a company or
  regarding persons dealing with one that is obtained by the
  Commissioner or by any person acting under the direction of the
  Commissioner, in the course of the exercise or performance of powers,
  duties and functions referred to in subsection 5(1) of the [Financial
  Consumer Agency of Canada Act](/eng/acts/F-11.1), and any information
  prepared from that information, is confidential and shall be treated
  accordingly.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure permitted

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Commissioner is satisfied that the information
  will be treated as confidential by the agency, body or person to whom
  it is disclosed, subsection (1) does not prevent the Commissioner from
  disclosing it

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to any government agency or body that regulates or
    supervises financial institutions, for purposes related to that
    regulation or supervision;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to any other agency or body that regulates or
    supervises financial institutions, for purposes related to that
    regulation or supervision;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to the Canada Deposit Insurance Corporation for
    purposes related to its operation; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} to the Deputy Minister of Finance or any officer of
    the Department of Finance authorized in writing by the Deputy
    Minister of Finance or to the Governor of the Bank of Canada or any
    officer of the Bank of Canada authorized in writing by the Governor
    of the Bank of Canada, for the purposes of policy analysis related
    to the regulation of financial institutions.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 566
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Examination

- **[[520.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-520.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner, from time to time,
  but at least once in each calendar year, shall make or cause to be
  made any examination and inquiry that the Commissioner considers
  necessary for the purposes of satisfying the Commissioner that the
  applicable consumer provisions are being complied with and, after the
  conclusion of each examination and inquiry, shall report on it to the
  Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access to records of company

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner or a person acting under the
  Commissioner's direction in carrying out his or her duties under
  subsection (1)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has a right of access to any records, including
    electronic records, of a company; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} may require the directors or officers of a company
    to provide information and explanations, to the extent that they are
    reasonably able to do so, in respect of any matter subject to
    examination or inquiry under subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 566
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power of Commissioner on inquiry

**[[520.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-520.4 .sectionLabel}** The Commissioner,
in carrying out his or her duties in relation to consumer provisions,
has all the powers of a person appointed as a commissioner under Part II
of the [Inquiries Act](/eng/acts/I-11) for the purpose of obtaining
evidence under oath, and may delegate those powers to any person acting
under the Commissioner's direction.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 566
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance agreement

**[[520.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-520.5 .sectionLabel}** The Commissioner
may enter into an agreement, called a "compliance agreement", with a
company for the purposes of implementing any measure designed to further
compliance by it with the consumer provisions.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 566
:::

## [PART XIII]{.HLabel1}[Administration]{.HTitleText1} {#h-458519 .Part}

### [Notices and Other Documents]{.HTitleText2} {#h-458520 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Execution of documents

**[[520.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-520.6 .sectionLabel}** Any by-law, notice,
resolution, requisition, statement or other document required or
permitted to be executed or signed by more than one person for the
purposes of this Act may be executed or signed in several documents of
like form, each of which is executed or signed by one or more of the
persons. The documents if duly executed or signed by all persons
required or permitted to sign them are deemed to constitute one document
for the purposes of this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 54, s. 446
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to directors and shareholders

**[[521]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-521 .sectionLabel}** A notice or document
required by this Act or the regulations or by the incorporating
instrument or by-laws of a company to be sent to a shareholder or
director of a company may be sent by prepaid mail addressed to, or may
be delivered personally to,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the shareholder at the shareholder's latest address
  as shown in the records of the company or its transfer agent; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the director at the director's latest address as
  shown in the records of the company or in the latest return made under
  section 499.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presumption from return

**[[522]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-522 .sectionLabel}** A director named in the
latest return sent by a company to the Superintendent under section 499
is presumed for the purposes of this Act to be a director of the company
referred to in the return.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presumption of receipt

- **[[523]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-523 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  notice or document sent by mail in accordance with section 521 to a
  shareholder or director is deemed to be received by the shareholder or
  director at the time it would be delivered in the ordinary course of
  mail unless there are reasonable grounds for believing that the
  shareholder or director did not receive the notice or document at that
  time or at all.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Undelivered notices

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a company sends a notice or document to a
  shareholder in accordance with section 521 and it is returned on two
  consecutive occasions because the shareholder cannot be found, the
  company is not required to send any further notices or documents to
  the shareholder until it is informed in writing of their new address.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 523
- 2005, c. 54, s. 447
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-523-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 523"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Service on a company

**[[524]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-524 .sectionLabel}** A notice or document
required by this Act to be sent to or served on a company may be sent by
registered mail to the head office of the company and, if so sent, is
deemed to be received or served at the time it would be delivered in the
ordinary course of mail unless there are reasonable grounds for
believing that the company did not receive the notice or document at
that time or at all.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of company

- **[[525]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-525 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  certificate issued on behalf of a company stating any fact that is set
  out in the incorporating instrument, the by-laws, the minutes of the
  meetings of the directors, a committee of directors or the
  shareholders, or in a contract to which the company is a party, may be
  signed by a director or an officer of the company.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof of certain cases

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When introduced as evidence in any civil, criminal or
  administrative action or proceeding,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a fact stated in a certificate referred to in
    subsection (1),

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a certified extract from a securities register of a
    company, or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a certified copy of, or an extract from, minutes of
    a meeting of shareholders, directors or a committee of directors of
    a company

  is, in the absence of evidence to the contrary, proof of the facts so
  certified without proof of the signature or official character of the
  person appearing to have signed the certificate.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Entry in securities register

**[[526]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-526 .sectionLabel}** An entry in the
securities register of, or on a security certificate issued by, a
company is evidence that the person in whose name the security is
registered is the owner of the securities described in the register or
in the certificate.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 526
- 2005, c. 54, s. 448(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-526-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 526"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Verification of documents or fact

- **[[527]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-527 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Superintendent may require that a document or a fact stated in a
  document that is required by or under this Act to be sent to the
  Superintendent or to the Minister be verified in accordance with
  subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form of proof

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A document or fact required by this Act or by the
  Superintendent to be verified may be verified by affidavit made under
  oath or by statutory declaration under the [Canada Evidence
  Act](/eng/acts/C-5) before any commissioner for oaths or for taking
  affidavits.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternative means of publication

- **[[527.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-527.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Anything that is required by a
  provision of this Act to be published in the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) or to be published in any other
  way may, instead of being published in that way, be published in any
  manner that may be prescribed for the purpose of that provision.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternative means of publishing
  summaries

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Anything that is required by a provision of this Act
  to be summarized in a publication may instead be summarized and
  published in any manner that may be prescribed for the purpose of that
  provision.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication conditions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Any condition under a provision of this Act that
  something be published in the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) or in any other way is satisfied
  if that thing is published instead in any manner that may be
  prescribed for the purpose of that provision.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other consequences

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where a provision of this Act provides for
  consequences to follow the publication of something in the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) or in any other manner, the same
  consequences follow the publication of that thing in any other manner
  that may be prescribed for the purpose of that provision.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1997, c. 15, s. 407
:::

### [Approvals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-458569 .Subheading}

Definition of [[approval]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

**[[527.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-527.2 .sectionLabel}** In sections 527.3
to 527.8, [[approval]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes any consent,
designation, order, exemption, extension or other permission granted by
the Minister or the Superintendent under this Act, and includes the
issuance of letters patent.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 567
- 2007, c. 6, s. 382
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-527.2-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 527.2"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Matters to take into account --- Minister

- **[[527.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-527.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In addition to any matters or
  conditions provided for in this Act that are relevant to the granting
  of an approval, the Minister may, in considering whether to grant the
  approval, take into account all matters that he or she considers
  relevant in the circumstances, including

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} national security; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Canada's international relations and its
    international legal obligations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Matters to take into account ---
  Superintendent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In addition to any matters or conditions provided for
  in this Act that are relevant to the granting of an approval and to
  any prudential considerations that the Superintendent considers
  relevant in the circumstances, the Superintendent may, in considering
  whether to grant the approval, take into account

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} national security; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Canada's international relations and its
    international legal obligations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 6, s. 382
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister --- terms, conditions and
undertakings

- **[[527.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-527.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In addition to any other action that
  may be taken under this Act, the Minister may, in granting an
  approval, impose any terms and conditions or require any undertaking
  that the Minister considers appropriate, including any terms,
  conditions or undertakings specified by the Superintendent to maintain
  or improve the safety and soundness of any financial institution
  regulated under an Act of Parliament to which the approval relates or
  that might be affected by it or to ensure that such a financial
  institution has adequate policies and procedures to protect itself
  against threats to its integrity or security.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Commissioner --- supervision of terms,
  conditions and undertakings

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister specifies that the Commissioner is
  to supervise a company to determine if it is complying with any terms
  and conditions that are imposed, or undertakings that are required, by
  the Minister for the protection of the company's customers, the
  Commissioner may take the same measures that the Commissioner could
  take if the terms and conditions or undertaking were a consumer
  provision.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Superintendent --- terms, conditions
  and undertakings

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In addition to any other action that may be taken
  under this Act, the Superintendent may, in granting an approval,
  impose any terms and conditions or require any undertaking that the
  Superintendent considers appropriate.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 6, s. 382
- 2010, c. 12, s. 1861
- [2023, c. 26, s. 535](#1410940-1417262){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410940-1417262.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-527.4-20100712.html "Link to previous version of section 527.4"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation, suspension or amendment of
approval --- Minister

- **[[527.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-527.5
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may revoke, suspend or
  amend any approval granted by the Minister if he or she considers it
  appropriate to do so. In deciding whether to take any of those
  actions, the Minister may take into account all matters that he or she
  considers relevant in the circumstances, including

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} national security; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Canada's international relations and its
    international legal obligations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation, suspension or amendment of
  approval --- Superintendent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent may revoke, suspend or amend any
  approval granted by the Superintendent if he or she considers it
  appropriate to do so. In deciding whether to take any of those
  actions, the Superintendent may take into account any prudential
  considerations that he or she considers relevant in the circumstances
  and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} national security; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Canada's international relations and its
    international legal obligations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Before taking any action under subsection (1) or (2),
  the Minister or the Superintendent, as the case may be, shall give the
  person concerned a reasonable opportunity to make representations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Temporary suspension or amendment

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If, in the Minister's opinion, the length of time
  required for representations to be made under subsection (3) might be
  prejudicial to the public interest, the Minister may temporarily
  suspend or amend any approval granted by the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cessation of effect

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A temporary suspension or amendment of an approval
  ceases to have effect on the earlier of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the expiry of 30 days after the day on which it
    takes effect or of a shorter period that is specified by the
    Minister, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the approval is revoked, suspended or amended
    under subsection (1), the day on which the revocation, suspension or
    amendment takes effect.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 6, s. 382
- [2023, c. 26, s. 536](#1410942-1417271){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410942-1417271.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-527.5-20070420.html "Link to previous version of section 527.5"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Confidential undertaking

- **[[527.51]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-527.51
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If, in the Minister's opinion, the
  disclosure of information about an undertaking required under
  subsection 527.4(1) or 527.5(1) or (4), or information that could
  reveal the existence of the undertaking, could pose a threat to the
  integrity or security of the financial institution to which the
  undertaking relates or could be injurious to national security, the
  Minister may specify that the information is confidential and shall be
  treated accordingly.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite anything in this Act but subject to
  subsection (3), it is prohibited to disclose any confidential
  information referred to in subsection (1) except in accordance with
  any terms or conditions that the Minister may specify in the
  undertaking.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice --- Committee and Review Agency

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister specifies under subsection (1) that
  information referred to in that subsection is confidential for reasons
  related to national security, the Minister shall, within 30 days after
  the day on which the undertaking in question is required, notify

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the [[Committee]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined
    in section 2 of the [National Security and Intelligence Committee of
    Parliamentarians Act](/eng/acts/N-16.6); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the [[Review Agency]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as
    defined in section 2 of the [National Security and Intelligence
    Review Agency Act](/eng/acts/N-16.62).

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 26, s. 537](#1410944-1417282){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410944-1417282.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of non-compliance on approval

- **[[527.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-527.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless otherwise expressly provided
  in this Act, a failure to comply with a term, condition or undertaking
  imposed or required under any provision of this Act does not
  invalidate the approval to which the term, condition or undertaking
  relates.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-compliance

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In addition to any other action that may be taken
  under this Act, in the case of non-compliance by a person with a term,
  condition or undertaking imposed or required under any provision of
  this Act, the Minister or the Superintendent, as the case may be, may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} revoke, suspend or amend the approval to which the
    term, condition or undertaking relates; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} apply to a court for an order directing the person
    to comply with the term, condition or undertaking, and on such an
    application the court may make the order and any other order that it
    thinks fit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Before taking any action under subsection (2), the
  Minister or the Superintendent, as the case may be, shall give the
  person concerned a reasonable opportunity to make representations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation, suspension or amendment

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} At the request of the person concerned, the Minister
  or the Superintendent, as the case may be, may revoke, suspend or
  amend any terms or conditions imposed by him or her and may revoke or
  suspend an undertaking given to him or her or approve its amendment.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 6, s. 382
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Multiple approval --- other approvals

**[[527.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-527.7 .sectionLabel}** The Minister or the
Superintendent may grant more than one approval, other than letters
patent, in a single instrument if he or she considers it appropriate to
do so, and if the Minister or Superintendent does so, he or she may
specify different effective dates for each of the approvals.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 6, s. 382
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption in relation to notices of
intention

**[[527.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-527.8 .sectionLabel}** The Superintendent
may, on application, exempt an applicant or applicants from the
provisions of this Act respecting the publication of a notice of
intention in respect of applications for approvals and impose any terms
and conditions respecting the publication of the notice of intention
that he or she considers appropriate.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 6, s. 382
:::

### [Exceptions to Generally Accepted Accounting Principles]{.HTitleText2} {#h-458628 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calculations --- generally accepted
accounting principles

- **[[527.81]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-527.81
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If, as a result of a change to the
  accounting principles referred to in subsection 313(4) --- whether the
  change is made before or after this section comes into force --- the
  Superintendent considers, given any prudential considerations that he
  or she considers relevant, that any amount, calculation or valuation
  under this Act or the regulations is not appropriate, the
  Superintendent may specify the amount that is to be used or the
  calculation or valuation that is to be performed instead.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Superintendent shall cause a notice of the
  specification to be published in the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) within 60 days after the day on
  which the specification has effect.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Five-year limit

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The specification ceases to have effect on the day
  indicated in the notice, which may be no later than five years after
  the day on which the specification is made.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2012, c. 5, s. 181
:::

### [Orders to Exempt or Adapt]{.HTitleText2} {#h-458638 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order

- **[[527.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-527.9
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On the recommendation of the
  Minister, the Governor in Council may, by order,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} provide that any provision of this Act or the
    regulations shall not apply to a company, to Her Majesty in right of
    Canada or an agent or agency of Her Majesty or to any other person
    otherwise subject to the provision; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} provide that any provision of this Act or the
    regulations applies to a company, to Her Majesty in right of Canada
    or Her Majesty's agent or agency or to any other person subject to
    the provision only in the manner and to the extent provided for in
    the order, and adapt the provision for the purposes of that
    application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister's recommendation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may make a recommendation under
  subsection (1) only if the Minister

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is of the opinion that the order would relate to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the acquisition, holding, sale or other
      disposition of, or other dealing with, shares of a company by, or
      the transfer or issue of shares of a company to, Her Majesty in
      right of Canada or Her Majesty's agent or agency, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the management of the business and affairs or
      the regulation and supervision of a company during the time that
      Her Majesty or Her Majesty's agent or agency is acquiring,
      holding, selling or otherwise disposing of, or otherwise dealing
      with, shares of the company, or during the time that shares of the
      company are transferred or issued to Her Majesty or Her Majesty's
      agent or agency; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is of the opinion --- after considering measures
    other than an order under that subsection and after consulting with
    the Superintendent, the Governor of the Bank of Canada and the Chief
    Executive Officer of the Canada Deposit Insurance Corporation ---
    that the order will promote the stability of the financial system in
    Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Terms and conditions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On the recommendation of the Minister, the Governor
  in Council may, by order, impose any terms and conditions relating to
  the acquisition of shares of a company by, or transfer or issue of
  shares of a company to, Her Majesty in right of Canada or Her
  Majesty's agent or agency.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Repeal of order under subsection (1)

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may recommend the repeal of an order
  made under subsection (1) without regard to subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Terms, conditions and undertakings

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} From the time that Her Majesty in right of Canada or
  an agent or agency of Her Majesty acquires shares of a company to the
  time that the shares are sold or otherwise disposed of, the Minister
  may, by order, impose any terms and conditions on --- or require any
  undertaking from --- the company that the Minister considers
  appropriate, including any terms and conditions or undertakings
  relating to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the remuneration of the company's senior officers,
    as defined in section 509.01, and directors;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the appointment or removal of the company's senior
    officers, as defined in section 509.01, and directors;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the payment of dividends by the company; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the company's lending policies and practices.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acquisition

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Despite Part X of the [Financial Administration
  Act](/eng/acts/F-11), the Minister or an agent or agency of Her
  Majesty in right of Canada may, on any terms and conditions imposed
  under subsection (3), acquire and hold shares of a company on behalf
  of or in trust for Her Majesty if, as a result of an order under
  subsection (1), the company may record in its securities register the
  transfer or issue of shares to Her Majesty or an agent or agency of
  Her Majesty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment out of C.R.F.

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} On the requisition of the Minister, there may be paid
  out of the Consolidated Revenue Fund the amount that the Minister or
  an agent or agency of Her Majesty in right of Canada is required to
  pay for the acquisition of shares under subsection (6) and any costs
  and expenses incurred in connection with the acquisition, holding,
  sale or other disposition of, or other dealing with, the shares.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration of shares

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} Shares acquired under subsection (6) by the Minister
  or an agent or agency of Her Majesty in right of Canada shall be
  registered in the name of the Minister, agent or agency, as the case
  may be, in the company's securities register if they are capable of
  being registered in it, and the shares shall be held by the Minister,
  agent or agency, as the case may be, on behalf of or in trust for Her
  Majesty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disposition by Minister

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, at any time, sell or otherwise
  dispose of shares acquired under subsection (6). The [Surplus Crown
  Assets Act](/eng/acts/S-27) and section 61 of the [Financial
