  Administration Act](/eng/acts/F-11) do not apply to the sale or
  disposition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disposition by agent or agency

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} An agent or agency of Her Majesty in right of Canada
  --- at the request of the Minister, which may be made at any time ---
  shall sell or otherwise dispose of shares acquired under subsection
  (6). The [Surplus Crown Assets Act](/eng/acts/S-27) and section 61 of
  the [Financial Administration Act](/eng/acts/F-11) do not apply to the
  sale or disposition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consideration by Minister

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister or an agent or agency of Her Majesty
  in right of Canada is holding shares of a company on behalf of or in
  trust for Her Majesty on the day that is two years after the day on
  which the shares were acquired, the Minister shall consider whether
  holding the shares continues to promote the stability of the financial
  system in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mandatory disposition

  [(12)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister, under subsection (11), considers
  that holding shares acquired under subsection (6) no longer continues
  to promote the stability of the financial system in Canada, the
  Minister --- or, at the request of the Minister, the agent or agency
  of Her Majesty in right of Canada --- shall take the measures that the
  Minister considers practicable in the circumstances to sell or
  otherwise dispose of the shares. The [Surplus Crown Assets
  Act](/eng/acts/S-27) and section 61 of the [Financial Administration
  Act](/eng/acts/F-11) do not apply to the sale or disposition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Not a Crown corporation

  [(13)]{.lawlabel} Even if the acquisition of a company's shares under
  subsection (6) would otherwise cause the company to be a Crown
  corporation, as defined in subsection 83(1) of the [Financial
  Administration Act](/eng/acts/F-11), the company is not a Crown
  corporation for the purposes of that Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Statutory Instruments
  Act](/eng/acts/S-22)

  [(14)]{.lawlabel} The [Statutory Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22) does
  not apply to an order made under this section.

- Definition of [[shares]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(15)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section,
  [[shares]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes any conversion or exchange
  privilege, option or right to acquire shares.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 2, s. 292
- 2016, c. 7, s. 174
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-527.9-20090312.html "Link to previous version of section 527.9"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Orders and Directives]{.HTitleText2} {#h-458682 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Not statutory instruments

**[[528]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-528 .sectionLabel}** An instrument issued or
made under this Act and directed to a single company or person, other
than an order referred to in section 487, is not a statutory instrument
for the purposes of the [Statutory Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form

**[[529]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-529 .sectionLabel}** The Superintendent may,
by order, establish the form of any application to be made to the
Minister or the Superintendent under this Act.

### [Applications to Superintendent]{.HTitleText2} {#h-458687 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of applications

- **[[529.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-529.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The following applications to the
  Superintendent must contain the information, material and evidence
  that the Superintendent may require:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} applications for approval under subsection 68(1),
    75(2), 78(4), 82(5), 83(1), 174(1), 222(3), 421(1), 453(6) or (10),
    456(1) or (2) or 470(1), subparagraph 475(2)(b)(vi), section 478 or
    subsection 482(3) or (4) or 483.3(1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} applications for consent under subsection 74(1);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} applications for exemptions under subsection
    160.05(3); and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} applications for extensions of time under
    subsection 456(3) or (5), 457(4) or 458(4).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Receipt

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Without delay after receiving the application, the
  Superintendent shall send a receipt to the applicant certifying the
  date on which it was received.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of decision to applicant

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), the Superintendent shall,
  within a period of thirty days after the receipt of the application,
  send to the applicant

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a notice approving the application, subject to any
    terms and conditions that the Superintendent considers appropriate;
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the Superintendent is not satisfied that it
    should be approved, a notice to that effect.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of period

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the Superintendent is unable to complete the
  consideration of the application within the period referred to in
  subsection (3), the Superintendent shall, within that period, send a
  notice to the applicant informing the applicant that the
  Superintendent has extended the period for a further period set out in
  the notice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed approval

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the applicant does not receive the notice required
  by subsection (3) and, where applicable, subsection (4), within the
  required period, the Superintendent is deemed to have approved the
  application and granted the approval, consent, extension or exemption
  to which the application relates, regardless of whether the approval,
  consent, extension or exemption is to be in writing or not.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 568
- 2007, c. 6, s. 383
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-529.1-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 529.1"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Applications for Certain Approvals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-458707 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for certain approvals

- **[[529.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-529.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An application for the prior written
  approval of the Minister in respect of paragraph 410(1)(c) or (c.1) or
  453(5)(b.1), (c), (d) or (d.1) must be filed with the Superintendent
  and contain the information, material and evidence that the
  Superintendent may require.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certification of receipt of application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If, in the opinion of the Superintendent, the
  application contains all the required information, the Superintendent
  must refer it to the Minister, together with his or her analysis in
  relation to the application, and send a receipt to the applicant
  certifying the date on which the application was referred to the
  Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incomplete application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If, in the opinion of the Superintendent, the
  application is incomplete, the Superintendent must send a notice to
  the applicant specifying the information required by the
  Superintendent to complete it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of decision

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (5), the Minister must, within
  30 days after the certified date referred to in subsection (2), send
  to the applicant

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a notice approving the application; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the Minister is not satisfied that the
    application should be approved, a notice to that effect.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of period

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister is unable to complete the
  consideration of an application within the 30-day period, the Minister
  must, within that period, send a notice to the applicant informing the
  applicant that the Minister has extended the period for a further
  period set out in the notice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed approval

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister does not send the notice referred to
  in subsection (4) or, where applicable, subsection (5), within the
  required period, the Minister is deemed to have approved the
  application.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2007, c. 6, s. 384
- 2012, c. 5, s. 182
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-529.2-20070420.html "Link to previous version of section 529.2"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Appeals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-458725 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal to Federal Court

- **[[530]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-530 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  appeal lies to the Federal Court from any direction of the Minister
  made under subsection 396(7), 401(1) or 401.1(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Federal Court may, in an appeal under subsection
  (1),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} dismiss the appeal;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} set aside the direction or decision; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} set aside the direction or decision and refer the
    matter back for re-determination.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of an appeal under subsection (1),
  the Minister shall, at the request of the company or person making the
  appeal, provide the company or person with a certificate in writing
  setting out the direction or decision appealed from and the reasons
  why the direction or decision was made.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, ss. 530, 540
- 1996, c. 6, s. 132
- 2012, c. 31, s. 108
- [2023, c. 26, s. 538](#1410946-1417284){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1410946-1417284.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-530-20121214.html "Link to previous version of section 530"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Regulations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-458738 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power to make regulations

- **[[531]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-531 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing anything that is required or authorized
    by this Act to be prescribed;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the way in which anything that is
    required or authorized by this Act to be prescribed shall be
    determined;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} defining words and expressions to be defined for
    the purposes of this Act;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} requiring the payment of a fee in respect of the
    filing, examining or issuing of any document or in respect of any
    action that the Superintendent is required or authorized to take
    under this Act, and fixing the amount thereof or the manner of
    determining the amount thereof;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} respecting the regulatory capital and total assets
    of a company;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} respecting the retention, in Canada, of assets of a
    company;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} respecting the value of assets of a company to be
    held in Canada and the manner in which those assets are to be held;

  - [(f.1)]{.lawlabel} respecting, for any purpose of any provision of
    the Act, the determination of the equity of a company;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} respecting the protection and maintenance of assets
    of a company and assets held in trust by a company, including
    regulations respecting the bonding of directors, officers and
    employees of a company;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} respecting the holding of shares and ownership
    interests for the purposes of sections 73 and 77;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} respecting information, in addition to the
    information required by section 501, to be maintained in the
    register referred to in that section; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} generally for carrying out the purposes and
    provisions of this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incorporation by reference

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The regulations may incorporate any material by
  reference regardless of its source and either as it exists on a
  particular date or as amended from time to time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incorporated material is not a
  regulation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Material does not become a regulation for the
  purposes of the [Statutory Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22) because it
  is incorporated by reference.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 531
- 1997, c. 15, s. 408
- 1999, c. 31, s. 220(F)
- 2001, c. 9, s. 569
- 2005, c. 54, s. 449
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-531-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 531"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Delegation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-458760 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delegation

**[[532]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-532 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may
delegate any of the Minister's powers, duties and functions under this
Act to any Minister of State appointed pursuant to the [Ministries and
Ministers of State Act](/eng/acts/M-8) to assist the Minister.

## [PART XIV]{.HLabel1}[Sanctions]{.HTitleText1} {#h-458763 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offence

- **[[533]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-533
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who, without reasonable
  cause, contravenes any provision of this Act or the regulations is
  guilty of an offence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}False or misleading information

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who knowingly provides false or
  misleading information in relation to any matter under this Act or the
  regulations is guilty of an offence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Undue preference to creditor

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every director, officer or employee of a company who
  wilfully gives or concurs in giving to any creditor of the company any
  fraudulent, undue or unfair preference over other creditors, by giving
  security to the creditor, by changing the nature of the creditor's
  claim or otherwise, is guilty of an offence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Failure to provide information

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every person who, without reasonable cause, refuses
  or fails to comply with a requirement made under paragraph 505(2)(b)
  is guilty of an offence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of name

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Except to the extent permitted by the regulations,
  every person who uses the name of a company in a prospectus, offering
  memorandum, takeover bid circular, advertisement for a transaction
  related to securities or in any other document in connection with a
  transaction related to securities is guilty of an offence.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 533
- 2007, c. 6, s. 385
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-533-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 533"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Punishment

- **[[534]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-534
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who is guilty of an
  offence under any of subsections 533(1) to (4) is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a natural person, liable

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} on summary conviction, to a fine not exceeding
      \$100,000 or to imprisonment for a term not exceeding twelve
      months, or to both, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} on conviction on indictment, to a fine not
      exceeding \$1,000,000 or to imprisonment for a term not exceeding
      five years, or to both; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an entity, liable

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} on summary conviction, to a fine not exceeding
      \$500,000, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} on conviction on indictment, to a fine not
      exceeding \$5,000,000.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order to comply

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a person has been convicted of an offence under
  this Act, the court may, in addition to any punishment it may
  otherwise impose, order the person to comply with the provisions of
  this Act or the regulations in respect of which the person was
  convicted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional fine

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a person has been convicted of an offence under
  this Act, the court may, if it is satisfied that as a result of the
  commission of the offence the convicted person acquired any monetary
  benefits or that monetary benefits accrued to the convicted person or
  their spouse, common-law partner or other dependant, order the
  convicted person to pay, despite the maximum amount of any fine that
  may otherwise be imposed under this Act, an additional fine in an
  amount equal to three times the court's estimation of the amount of
  those monetary benefits.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 534
- 1997, c. 15, s. 409
- 2000, c. 12, s. 301
- 2005, c. 54, s. 450
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-534-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 534"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability of officers, directors, etc.

**[[535]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-535 .sectionLabel}** Where an entity commits
an offence under this Act, any officer, director or agent of the entity
who directed, authorized, assented to, acquiesced in or participated in
the commission of the offence is a party to and guilty of the offence
and liable on summary conviction or on conviction on indictment to the
punishment provided under paragraph 534(1)(a) for the offence whether or
not the entity has been prosecuted or convicted.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 535
- 1997, c. 15, s. 410
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation period

- **[[535.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-535.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Proceedings by way of summary
  conviction in respect of an offence under a provision of this Act may
  be commenced at any time within, but not later than, two years after
  the day on which the subject-matter of the proceedings became known,
  in the case of an offence under a consumer provision, to the
  Commissioner and, in any other case, to the Superintendent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of Superintendent or
  Commissioner

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A document appearing to have been issued by the
  Superintendent or Commissioner, as the case may be, certifying the day
  on which the subject-matter of any proceedings became known to the
  Superintendent or Commissioner is admissible in evidence without proof
  of the signature or official character of the person appearing to have
  signed it and is, in the absence of evidence to the contrary, proof of
  the matter asserted in it.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 9, s. 570
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of offence on contracts

**[[536]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-536 .sectionLabel}** Unless otherwise
expressly provided in this Act, a contravention of any provision of this
Act or the regulations does not invalidate any contract entered into in
contravention of the provision.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restraining or compliance order

- **[[537]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-537 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  company or any director, officer, employee or agent of a company does
  not comply with any provision of this Act or the regulations other
  than a consumer provision, or of the incorporating instrument or any
  by-law of the company, the Superintendent, any complainant or any
  creditor of the company may, in addition to any other right that
  person has, apply to a court for an order directing the company,
  director, officer, employee or agent to comply with --- or restraining
  the company, director, officer, employee or agent from acting in
  breach of --- the provision and, on the application, the court may so
  order and make any further order it thinks fit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance or restraining order ---
  consumer provisions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a company or any director, officer, employee or
  agent of a company does not comply with any applicable consumer
  provision, the Commissioner or any complainant may, in addition to any
  other right that person has, apply to a court for an order directing
  the company, director, officer, employee or agent to comply with ---
  or restraining the company, director, officer, employee or agent from
  acting in breach of --- the consumer provision and, on the
  application, the court may so order and make any further order it
  thinks fit.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 537
- 2001, c. 9, s. 571
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal of final order

- **[[538]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-538 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  appeal lies to the court of appeal of a province from any final order
  made by a court of that province under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal with leave

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An appeal lies to the court of appeal of a province
  from any order, other than a final order made by a court of that
  province, only with leave of the court of appeal in accordance with
  the rules applicable to that court.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1991, c. 45, s. 538
- 2005, c. 54, s. 451
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-538-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 538"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recovery and application of fines

**[[539]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-539 .sectionLabel}** All fines payable under
this Act are recoverable and enforceable, with costs, at the suit of Her
Majesty in right of Canada, instituted by the Attorney General of
Canada, and, when recovered, belong to Her Majesty in right of Canada.

## [PART XIV.1]{.HLabel1}[Documents in Electronic or Other Form]{.HTitleText1} {#h-458821 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[539.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-539.01 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Part.

[[electronic document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[electronic document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, except in section
    539.1, any form of representation of information or concepts that is
    fixed in any medium in or by electronic, optical or other similar
    means and that can be read or perceived by a person or by any
    means. ([document électronique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[information system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[information system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a system used to
    generate, send, receive, store or otherwise process an electronic
    document. ([système de traitement de l'information]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 54, s. 452
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

**[[539.02]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-539.02 .sectionLabel}** This Part other
than sections 539.13 and 539.14 does not apply in respect of any notice,
document or other information that under this Act or the regulations is
sent to or issued by the Minister, the Superintendent, the Commissioner
or the Bank of Canada or any prescribed notice, document or information.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 54, s. 452
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use not mandatory

**[[539.03]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-539.03 .sectionLabel}** Nothing in this
Act or the regulations requires a person to create or provide an
electronic document.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 54, s. 452
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consent and other requirements

- **[[539.04]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-539.04
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite anything in this Part, a
  requirement under this Act or the regulations to provide a notice,
  document or other information is not satisfied by providing an
  electronic document unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the addressee consents and designates an
    information system for the receipt of the electronic document;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the electronic document is, unless otherwise
    prescribed, provided to the designated information system; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the prescribed requirements are complied with.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consent and notice in electronic form

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), the requirements referred
  to in paragraph (1)(c) may provide that the consent referred to in
  paragraph (1)(a) and any notice related to that consent may be
  provided in electronic form.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations --- revocation of consent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations
  respecting the revocation of the consent referred to in paragraph
  (1)(a).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 54, s. 452
- 2018, c. 27, s. 152
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-539.04-20110601.html "Link to previous version of section 539.04"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Creation or provision of information

**[[539.05]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-539.05 .sectionLabel}** A requirement
under this Act or the regulations to create or provide a notice,
document or other information is satisfied by creating or providing an
electronic document if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the incorporating instrument or by-laws of the
  company do not provide otherwise; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the prescribed requirements are complied with.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 54, s. 452
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Creation of information in writing

**[[539.06]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-539.06 .sectionLabel}** A requirement
under this Act or the regulations to create a notice, document or other
information in writing is satisfied by creating an electronic document
if in addition to the conditions set out in section 539.05

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information in the electronic document is
  accessible so as to be usable for subsequent reference; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the prescribed requirements are complied with.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 54, s. 452
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provision of information in writing

**[[539.07]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-539.07 .sectionLabel}** A requirement
under this Act or the regulations to provide a notice, document or other
information in writing is satisfied by providing an electronic document
if in addition to the conditions set out in section 539.05

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information in the electronic document is
  accessible by the addressee and capable of being retained by them so
  as to be usable for subsequent reference; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the prescribed requirements are complied with.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 54, s. 452
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Multiple copies

**[[539.08]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-539.08 .sectionLabel}** A requirement
under this Act or the regulations to provide two or more copies of a
document at the same time to one addressee is satisfied by providing one
copy of the electronic document.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 54, s. 452
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registered mail

**[[539.09]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-539.09 .sectionLabel}** A requirement
under this Act or the regulations to provide a document by registered
mail is not satisfied by providing an electronic document except in the
prescribed circumstances.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 54, s. 452
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statutory declarations and affidavits

- **[[539.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-539.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A statutory declaration or affidavit
  required under this Act or the regulations may be created or provided
  in an electronic document if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person who makes the statutory declaration or
    affidavit signs it with their secure electronic signature;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the authorized person before whom the statutory
    declaration or affidavit is made signs it with their secure
    electronic signature; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the requirements of sections 539.03 to 539.09 are
    complied with.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, [[electronic
  document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} and [[secure electronic
  signature]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} have the same meaning as in subsection
  31(1) of the [Personal Information Protection and Electronic Documents
  Act](/eng/acts/P-8.6).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}References to "electronic document"

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of complying with paragraph (1)(c),
  references to "electronic document" in sections 539.03 to 539.09 are
  to be read as references to "electronic document within the meaning of
  subsection 31(1) of the [Personal Information Protection and
  Electronic Documents Act](/eng/acts/P-8.6)".

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 54, s. 452
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Signatures

**[[539.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-539.11 .sectionLabel}** A requirement
under this Act or the regulations for a signature or for a document to
be executed, except in respect of a statutory declaration or affidavit,
is satisfied in respect of an electronic document if the prescribed
requirements are complied with and the signature results from the
application by the person of a technology or process that permits the
following to be proved:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the signature resulting from the use by the person of
  the technology or process is unique to the person;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the technology or process is used by the person to
  incorporate their signature into, attach it to or associate it with
  the electronic document; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the technology or process can be used to identify the
  person using the technology or process.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 54, s. 452
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations --- provision and receipt of
documents

**[[539.12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-539.12 .sectionLabel}** The Governor in
Council may make regulations respecting the time and place at which and
the circumstances under which an electronic document is considered to be
provided or received.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 54, s. 452
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content and form of notices and documents

**[[539.13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-539.13 .sectionLabel}** The Minister,
Superintendent, Commissioner or Bank of Canada may establish the
requirements for the content and fix the form, including electronic and
other forms, of notices and documents sent to or issued by each of them
under this Act or the regulations, including

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the notices and documents that may be sent in
  electronic or other form;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the persons or classes of persons who may send
  notices and documents;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} their signature in electronic or other form or their
  execution, adoption or authorization in a manner that is to have the
  same effect for the purposes of this Act as their signature;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the time and place at which and the circumstances
  under which electronic documents are considered to be sent or
  received; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} any matter necessary for the purposes of the
  application of this section.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 54, s. 452
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption

**[[539.14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-539.14 .sectionLabel}** In the prescribed
circumstances, the Minister, the Superintendent, the Commissioner or the
Bank of Canada may, on any conditions that they consider appropriate,
exempt from the application of any provision of this Act requiring a
notice or document to be sent to them any notice or document, or class
of notice or document, containing information similar to that contained
in a notice or document required to be made public under any other Act
of Parliament or any Act of the legislature of a province.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 54, s. 452
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Coming into force

- **[[Footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^\*^](#T-19.8_en_1){#T-19.8_en_1-ID0EBCA}[[563]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-563
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), this Act
  or any provision thereof shall come into force on a day or days to be
  fixed by order of the Governor in Council.

  - ::: Footnote
    [[Return to footnote
    ]{.wb-invisible}^\*^](#T-19.8_en_1-ID0EBCA){#T-19.8_en_1}\[Note:
    Act, except subsections 250(1) and (2), in force June 1, 1992, *see*
    SI/92-89.\]
    :::

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsections 250(1) and (2) shall come into force on
  the day that is six months after the coming into force of subsections
  243(1) and (2).

## RELATED PROVISIONS {#related-provisions .scheduleTitleText}

- --- 2015, c. 36, s. 239

  - ###### Retroactivity --- section 504 of [Trust and Loan Companies Act](/eng/acts/T-19.8) {#retroactivity-section-504-of-trust-and-loan-companies-act .MarginalNote}

    [239]{.lawlabel} Section 504 of the [Trust and Loan Companies
    Act](/eng/acts/T-19.8) applies to information referred to in that
    section that has been used or in relation to which oral testimony
    has been given or a document has been produced, before the day on
    which this Division comes into force, in any civil proceedings in
    respect of which a final decision has not been made before that day.

- --- 2015, c. 36, s. 246

  - ###### Regulations apply --- section 504 of [Trust and Loan Companies Act](/eng/acts/T-19.8) {#regulations-apply-section-504-of-trust-and-loan-companies-act .MarginalNote}

    [246]{.lawlabel} The regulations made under paragraph 531(1)(a) of
    the [Trust and Loan Companies Act](/eng/acts/T-19.8) that prescribe
    supervisory information for the purposes of section 503.1 of that
    Act apply for the purposes of section 504 of that Act until
    regulations made under that paragraph for the purposes of that
    section 504 are in force.
:::
::::

:::: section
::: ScheduleNIF
## AMENDMENTS NOT IN FORCE {#amendments-not-in-force .scheduleTitleText}

- --- 2018, c. 12, s. 310(1), as amended by 2024, c. 15, s. 168(1)

  - - [310]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Paragraphs 410(1)(c) and (c.1)
      of the [Trust and Loan Companies Act](/eng/acts/T-19.8) are
      replaced by the following:

      :::: section
      ::: AmendedText
      - [(b.1)]{.lawlabel} subject to sections 416 and 417 and the
        regulations, engage in any activity that relates to the
        provision of financial services by the company or any of its
        affiliates;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} subject to the regulations, engage in any of
        the following activities:

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} collecting, manipulating and transmitting
          information, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} designing, developing, manufacturing,
          selling and otherwise dealing with information technology, if
          those activities relate to

          - [(A)]{.lawlabel} an activity referred to in this subsection
            that is engaged in by the company or any of its affiliates,
            or

          - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the provision of financial services by any
            other entity,
      :::
      ::::

- --- 2018, c. 12, ss. 310(3) to (5), as amended by 2024, c. 15, s.
  168(2)

  - - [310]{.lawlabel} [(3)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph 410(3)(a) of the Act
      is replaced by the following:

      :::: section
      ::: AmendedText
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting what a company may or may not do
        with respect to the carrying on of the activities referred to in
        paragraphs (1)(b.1), (c) and (d.1);
      :::
      ::::

    - [(4)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph 410(3)(b) of the English version of the
      Act is replaced by the following:

      :::: section
      ::: AmendedText
      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} imposing terms and conditions in respect of the
        provision of the services referred to in paragraphs (1)(a) and
        409(2)(c) and the carrying on of the activities referred to in
        paragraphs (1)(b.1), (c) and (d.1); and
      :::
      ::::

    - [(5)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph 410(3)(c) of the Act is replaced by the
      following:

      :::: section
      ::: AmendedText
      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting the circumstances in which a company
        may engage in the activities referred to in paragraphs (1)(b.1)
        and (c), including the circumstances in which it may collect,
        manipulate and transmit information under subparagraph
        (1)(c)(i).
      :::
      ::::

- --- 2018, c. 12, s. 311

  - [311]{.lawlabel} Section 411 of the Act is replaced by the
    following:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - ###### Networking {#networking .MarginalNote}

      - [411]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 416 and the
        regulations, a company may

        - [(a)]{.lawlabel} act as agent for any person in respect of

          - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the carrying on of any activity referred to
            in subsection 410(1) that is engaged in by a financial
            institution, by a [[permitted entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}
            as defined in subsection 449(1), if that definition were
            read without reference to the requirements of subsections
            453(4) to (6), or by a prescribed entity, and

          - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the provision of any service that relates
            to financial services and that is provided by an institution
            or entity referred to in subparagraph (i);

        - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} enter into an arrangement with any person
          in respect of the carrying on of an activity referred to in
          subparagraph (a)(i) or the provision of a service referred to
          in subparagraph (a)(ii); and

        - [(b)]{.lawlabel} refer any person to another person.

      - ###### Regulations {#regulations .MarginalNote}

        [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations

        - [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting the circumstances in which a
          company may act as an agent, enter into an arrangement or
          refer a person under subsection (1); and

        - [(b)]{.lawlabel} imposing terms and conditions in respect of
          the carrying on of activities under that subsection.

    - ###### Regulations {#regulations-1 .MarginalNote}

      [411.1]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, for the purposes
      of section 409 and subsection 411(1), make regulations respecting
      what a company is prohibited from doing when acting as an agent or
      when making referrals.
    :::
    ::::

- --- 2018, c. 12, s. 312, as amended by 2024, c. 15, s. 169(F)

  - - [312]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Section 453 of the Act is
      amended by adding the following after subsection (2):

      :::: section
      ::: AmendedText
      - ###### Permitted investments {#permitted-investments .MarginalNote}

        [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (3) to (6), Part XI
        and the regulations made under paragraphs (2.2)(b) and (c), a
        company may acquire control of, or acquire or increase a
        substantial investment in, an entity, other than an entity
        referred to in any of paragraphs (1)(a) to (j), if a majority,
        as defined in the regulations, of the entity's business consists
        of financial service activities or any other activity that a
        company is permitted to engage in under paragraphs 409(2)(b) to
        (d).

      - ###### Regulations {#regulations-2 .MarginalNote}

        [(2.2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations

        - [(a)]{.lawlabel} defining, for the purposes of subsection
          (2.1), the word "majority";

        - [(b)]{.lawlabel} imposing terms and conditions in respect of
          the acquisition of control of, or acquisition or increase of a
          substantial investment in, an entity that a company may
          acquire control of, or acquire or increase a substantial
          investment in, under subsection (2.1); and

        - [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting the circumstances in which a
          company may acquire control of, or acquire or increase a
          substantial investment in, an entity under subsection (2.1).
      :::
      ::::

    - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subparagraph 453(3)(f)(ii) of the Act is replaced
      by the following:

      :::: section
      ::: AmendedText
      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an entity that is not
        controlled by the company, the company itself would be permitted
        to acquire a substantial investment in the other entity under
        subsection (1), (2) or (2.1) or 451(2), paragraph 451(3)(b)
        or (c) or subsection 451(4); or
      :::
      ::::

    - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 453(5) of the Act is amended by adding
      "or" at the end of paragraph (c) and by repealing paragraphs
      (5)(d) and (d.1).

    - [(4)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph 453(7)(a) of the Act is replaced by the
      following:

      :::: section
      ::: AmendedText
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the company is acquiring control of an entity,
        other than a specialized financing entity, under subsection (2)
        or (2.1) and the only reason for which the company would, but
        for this subsection, require approval for the acquisition is
        that the entity carries on activities referred to in paragraph
        (2)(b);
      :::
      ::::

- --- 2018, c. 12, s. 313, as amended by 2024, c. 15, s. 170(F)

  - [313]{.lawlabel} The Act is amended by adding the following after
    section 453:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - ###### Regulations {#regulations-3 .MarginalNote}

      [453.1]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting the circumstances in which a company
        may acquire control of, or acquire or increase a substantial
        investment in, an entity that engages in activities that a
        company is permitted to engage in under paragraph 410(1)(b.1) or
        (c), including the circumstances in which a company is
        prohibited from doing so; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} imposing terms and conditions in respect of the
        acquisition of control of, or acquisition or increase of a
        substantial investment in, an entity referred to in paragraph
        (a).
    :::
    ::::

- --- 2018, c. 12, s. 314

  - [314]{.lawlabel} Paragraph 483(1)(c) of the Act is replaced by the
    following:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} consists of a written contract with the related
      party for the purpose of having either one of them act as an agent
      or make referrals;
    :::
    ::::

- --- 2018, c. 12, s. 315

  - [315]{.lawlabel} Subsection 529.2(1) of the Act is replaced by the
    following:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - ###### Application for certain approvals {#application-for-certain-approvals .MarginalNote}

      - [529.2]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} An application for the prior
        written approval of the Minister in respect of paragraph
        453(5)(b.1) or (c) must be filed with the Superintendent and
        contain the information, material and evidence that the
        Superintendent may require.
    :::
    ::::

- --- 2024, c. 15, s. 186

  - - [186]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 139(1) of the French
      version of the [Trust and Loan Companies Act](/eng/acts/T-19.8) is
      replaced by the following:

      :::: section
      ::: {.AmendedText lang="fr"}
      - ###### Lieu des assemblées {#lieu-des-assemblées .MarginalNote}

        - [139]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Les assemblées des
          actionnaires se tiennent au Canada, au lieu que prévoient les
          règlements administratifs ou, à défaut, que choisissent les
          administrateurs.
      :::
      ::::

    - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 139(2) of the Act is replaced by the
      following:

      :::: section
      ::: AmendedText
      - ###### Participation by electronic means {#participation-by-electronic-means .MarginalNote}

        [(2)]{.lawlabel} Unless the by-laws provide otherwise and in
        accordance with any regulations, any person who is entitled to
        attend a meeting of shareholders may participate in the meeting
        by means of a telephonic, electronic or other communication
        facility that permits all participants to communicate adequately
        with each other during the meeting if the company makes one
        available. A person who is participating in a meeting by one of
        those means is deemed for the purposes of this Act to be present
        at the meeting.

      - ###### Meeting held by electronic means {#meeting-held-by-electronic-means .MarginalNote}

        [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} If the directors or shareholders of a company
        call a meeting of shareholders under this Act, those directors
        or shareholders may determine that the meeting shall be held, in
        accordance with any regulations, entirely by means of a
        telephonic, electronic or other communication facility that
        permits all participants to communicate adequately with each
        other during the meeting, if the by-laws so provide.
      :::
      ::::

- --- 2024, c. 15, s. 187

  - [187]{.lawlabel} Subsection 154(4) of the Act is replaced by the
    following:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - ###### Voting while participating electronically {#voting-while-participating-electronically .MarginalNote}

      [(4)]{.lawlabel} Unless the by-laws provide otherwise and in
      accordance with any regulations, any person who is participating
      in a meeting of shareholders under subsection 139(2) or (2.1) and
      entitled to vote at that meeting may vote by means of the
      telephonic, electronic or other communication facility that the
      company has made available for that purpose.
    :::
    ::::

- --- 2024, c. 17, s. 397

  - [397]{.lawlabel} The [Trust and Loan Companies
    Act](/eng/acts/T-19.8) is amended by adding the following after
    section 162:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    ##### [Disclosure Relating to Diversity]{.HTitleText3} {#disclosure-relating-to-diversity .SchedHeadL3}

    - ###### Diversity {#diversity .MarginalNote}

      - [162.1]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} The directors of a company
        of a prescribed class must make available, in accordance with
        regulations made under subsection (2), prescribed information
        respecting diversity among directors and members of senior
        management, as defined by regulation, at the same time that a
        notice of annual meeting is sent under subsection 141(1) to
        shareholders entitled to receive that notice and to the
        Superintendent.

      - ###### Regulations {#regulations-4 .MarginalNote}

        [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), the
        Governor in Council may make regulations respecting the
        disclosure of information related to diversity among the
        directors and members of senior management of a company of a
        prescribed class.
    :::
    ::::
:::
